Furthermore, we propose Flatness Preference Optimization (FlatPO) to flatten these key sharpness dimensions, leading various PEFTs toward better generalization. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our findings and the proposed method. Code is available at https://github.com/Can-Lin/FlatPO.

</details>


### [35] [PathRelax: Parallel-Path Relaxed Speculative Jacobi Decoding for Accelerating Auto-Regressive Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10492)
*Haodong Lei,Hongsong Wang,Bingxuan Dai,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自回归文本到图像模型中因长序列导致的推理效率问题，提出了PathSpec框架，包含并行路径推测Jacobi解码(PathExplore)和跨路径松弛验证(PathRelax)，通过多序列草稿树结构和跨序列语义相似性利用，实现了最高4.18倍加速，且不牺牲图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归文本到图像模型的加速方法依赖链式草稿token序列，导致草稿token搜索效率低、接受长度有限。需要一种新的多序列并行推测解码框架来扩展搜索空间并提高接受率。

Method: 提出PathSpec框架：(1) PathExplore——并行路径推测Jacobi解码，构建多序列草稿树结构，扩展token搜索空间以提升加速比；(2) PathRelax——跨路径松弛验证，利用不同序列间的语义相似性进一步提高token接受率。两者可与其他松弛技术无缝集成。

Result: 在Parti-Prompts、MSCOCO2017和T2ICompBench三个数据集上分别实现4.14倍、3.95倍和4.18倍的加速比。PathExplore在不使用任何松弛采样的情况下，加速比已超越GSD和LANTERN等现有松弛采样方法。PathRelax的松弛机制可与其他松弛技术无缝集成以进一步加速。

Conclusion: PathSpec通过并行多序列草稿树和跨路径松弛验证，显著提升了自回归文本到图像模型的推理效率，为实时文本到图像生成提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The growing need for high-resolution image generation in autoregressive text-to-image models has resulted in extended token sequences, significantly increasing computational costs and inference times. However, existing state-of-the-art methods for accelerating autoregressive text-to-image models rely on chain-structured draft token sequences, leading to inefficient draft token search and limited acceptance lengths. To address this, we propose parallel-path cross-relaxed speculative Jacobi decoding (\textbf{PathSpec}), a novel framework that enhances efficiency through a multi-sequence draft tree structure. Our parallel-path speculative Jacobi decoding (\textbf{PathExplore}) expands the token search space, achieving a higher speedup ratio without sacrificing image quality. Additionally, we introduce cross-path relaxed verification (\textbf{PathRelax}) that exploits semantic similarities across sequences to further boost token acceptance rates. Evaluated on the Parti-Prompts, MSCOCO2017, and T2ICompBench datasets, our method achieves a speedup ratio of 4.14 $\times$, 3.95$\times$, and 4.18$\times$, respectively. Remarkably, PathExplore, without any relaxed sampling, outperforms relaxed sampling methods in the speedup ratio, such as GSD and LANTERN. Moreover, PathRelax's relaxation mechanism can be seamlessly integrated with other relaxation techniques, enabling further acceleration and providing an efficient solution for real-time text-to-image generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Haodong-Lei-Ray/PathSpec.

</details>


### [36] [LAFP: Preserving Latent Action Structure in Latent Policy Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10517)
*Jiexi Lyu,Xizhou Bu,Qingqiu Huang,Chufeng Tang,Xiaoshuai Hao,Hongbo Wang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAFP 利用流匹配（flow matching）进行潜在策略学习，并通过推理时插值机制解决随机性导致的潜在动作与物理动作之间的错位问题，在下游模仿学习任务中比先前方法成功率提升10-15%，推理开销增加不到1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大规模无标注视频学习潜在动作的方法，通常采用行为克隆训练动作解码器，这会将多模态动作分布塌缩为单模态，破坏预训练的潜在动作结构。流匹配虽然可替代行为克隆，但直接应用会因策略的随机性导致潜在动作与物理动作之间产生错位（misalignment），限制了其效果。

Method: 提出 Latent Action Flow Policy (LAFP)，核心包含两部分：(1) 利用流匹配（flow matching）替代行为克隆进行潜在策略学习，以保留动作分布的多模态特性；(2) 引入推理时插值机制（inference-time interpolation mechanism），在动作解码器训练阶段缓解因流匹配策略随机性引起的潜在动作与物理动作之间的错位问题。

Result: 实验表明，LAFP 在下游模仿学习任务中一致优于先前方法，成功率提升达 10-15%，且推理阶段额外开销不到 1 倍。

Conclusion: LAFP 有效结合了流匹配在保持多模态分布上的优势与推理时插值带来的对齐改进，为解决大规模潜在策略学习中动作分布塌缩与错位问题提供了一种实用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Learning high-quality latent actions from large-scale unlabeled videos, coupled with limited real-world interaction data for training an action decoder, has emerged as a promising paradigm for scalable latent policy learning. However, existing approaches typically rely on behavior cloning, which tends to collapse inherently multimodal action distributions into unimodal ones, thereby degrading the pretrained latent action structure. While flow matching provides a potential alternative, directly applying it leads to a misalignment between latent actions and physical actions during action decoder training, due to the stochastic nature of the learned policy. To address these, we propose Latent Action Flow Policy (LAFP), which leverages flow matching for latent policy learning and introduces an inference-time interpolation mechanism to mitigate stochasticity-induced misalignment. Experimental results demonstrate that LAFP consistently outperforms prior methods on downstream imitation learning tasks, achieving up to 10-15% improvement in success rate while incurring less than 1x additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [37] [GUI-AC: Enhancing Continual Learning in GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10522)
*Can Lin,Tao Feng,Hangjie Yuan,Dan Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Zhonghong Ou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI代理面临界面环境非稳态带来的持续分布偏移问题，强化微调存在grounding不稳定。本文提出GUI-AC方法，通过Adaptive Advantage降噪和Dynamic Clipping放松裁剪边界，增强GUI代理的持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI数据具有非稳态特性，新界面实例的持续出现导致分布偏移，阻碍代理的持续学习。现有强化微调(RFT)存在grounding能力不稳定：奖励不连续、高方差振荡，以及因rollout分布不均衡引入的噪声优势估计导致策略过自信，固定裁剪边界抑制了适应新分布所需的探索能力。

Method: 提出GUI-AC方法，引入grounding certainty支持两个核心机制：(1) Adaptive Advantage：对噪声优势估计进行降权，防止策略过自信；(2) Dynamic Clipping：放松裁剪边界以扩展探索范围。

Result: 大量实验表明，两个机制联合提升了性能，使GUI-AC方法超越了现有的最先进基线。

Conclusion: GUI-AC通过Adaptive Advantage和Dynamic Clipping有效解决了RFT在GUI场景中的不稳定性问题，显著增强了GUI代理的持续学习能力，性能达到SOTA水平。

Abstract: Graphical User Interfaces (GUIs) serve as the dominant medium for human-computer interaction, yet building GUI agents that generalize across the vast diversity of real-world interface environments, with the same flexibility and robustness that humans naturally exhibit, remains unsolved. Notably, GUI data are inherently non-stationary: the continual emergence of previously unseen interface instances (e.g., novel domains and resolutions) induces persistent distribution shifts, significantly impeding the continual learning of existing GUI agents. Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) has attracted considerable attention as a promising approach. Nevertheless, RFT exhibits pronounced instability in its grounding capability, manifested as sharp reward discontinuities and high-variance oscillations. The imbalanced distribution of rollout outcomes introduces substantial noise into advantage estimation, leading to policy overconfidence. The fixed clipping bound suppresses the increase in policy probabilities needed to adapt to new distributions, leading to a collapse in exploration capacity. To address these challenges, we propose GUI-AC, a method that enhances the continual learning capability of GUI agents. GUI-AC introduces grounding certainty to support two core mechanisms: (i) Adaptive Advantage, which down-weights noisy advantage estimates to prevent policy overconfidence; and (ii) Dynamic Clipping, which relaxes the clipping bound to encourage exploration range. Extensive experiments show that these mechanisms jointly improve performance, enabling our method to surpass state-of-the-art baselines. Code is available anonymously at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GUI-AC.

</details>


### [38] [Audio-Visual Exchange-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Audio-Visual Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10533)
*Zihan Meng,Dexiang Hong,Weidong Chen,Ziyu Zhou,Bo Hu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVEX-Prune，一种基于强化学习的音视频动态令牌剪枝方法。其核心创新是音视频令牌交换策略——通过将低置信度保留令牌与同模态或跨模态的高置信度候选令牌进行交换，并衡量交换后字幕生成质量的差异，从而更精准地选取真正有价值的令牌。该方法在 VILA 1.5-8B 和 VideoLLaMA 2 上以 40% 保留率实现了与全令牌相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频字幕生成中，多模态 LLM 需要处理大量音视频令牌，导致预填充自注意力计算呈二次方增长。现有令牌剪枝方法（基于注意力、显著性或交叉熵损失）依赖硬阈值选择，难以精准保留真正有价值的令牌，尤其是靠近决策边界的高混淆令牌。

Method: 提出 AVEX-Prune：基于强化学习的动态令牌剪枝方法，核心为音视频令牌交换策略。该策略将低置信度保留令牌替换为同模态或跨模态的高置信度候选令牌，通过衡量令牌交换前后字幕生成质量的差异来判断令牌价值，从而突破硬阈值选择的局限。

Result: 在 VILA 1.5-8B 上，保持率 40% 时达到 54.5（全令牌 54.6）；在 VideoLLaMA 2 上，保持率 40% 时达到 57.0（全令牌 56.8），均实现了与全令牌相当的性能。

Conclusion: AVEX-Prune 通过 RL 驱动的令牌交换策略，有效解决了硬阈值剪枝在决策边界附近难以区分有价值令牌的问题，在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持了字幕生成质量。

Abstract: Audio-visual captioning generates natural language descriptions from video and audio content. Multimodal LLMs have advanced this task, but both modalities contribute many tokens to the LLM input, where prefill self-attention scales quadratically. Existing token-pruning methods usually retain tokens by attention, saliency, or cross-entropy loss, yet the hard threshold selection makes it difficult to retain tokens that are truly valuable, especially for high-confusing tokens near the decision boundary. To this end, we propose a AVEX-Prune, an RL-based audio-visual dynamic token pruning method in this work. In our AVEX-Prune, an audio-visual token exchange strategy is proposed to select truly valuable tokens by replacing low-confidence retained tokens with high-confidence candidate tokens from the same or the other modality, and measuring the differences in caption generation from token swaps. AVEX-Prune preserves full-token quality at a 40% retention ratio on both VILA 1.5-8B (54.5 vs. 54.6) and VideoLLaMA 2 (57.0 vs. 56.8).

</details>


### [39] [GRAR: Glass-induced Reflection Artifact Removal in LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10541)
*Wanpeng Shao,Zeyi Guo,Bo Zhang,Yifei Xue,Tie Ji,Yizhen Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段框架，结合多模态视觉基础模型与物理驱动的几何描述符（RE-LGGS），用于稳健地去除TLS点云中的玻璃反射伪影，在多个公开数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中TLS点云常受玻璃反射伪影影响，现有方法依赖理想反射对称假设，在玻璃估计不准确和几何表征不足时性能受限。

Method: 第一阶段：利用多模态视觉基础模型生成初始玻璃掩膜，通过几何线索细化获得高精度玻璃区域，并完成因透明表面无回波导致的缺失区域补全；第二阶段：提出物理驱动的描述符RE-LGGS，基于实际激光反射几何，结合PCA局部形状表征，联合编码多尺度几何结构与方向一致性。

Result: 在多个公开TLS数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架在反射伪影去除任务上一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架通过精准玻璃估计与物理驱动的几何描述符，显著提升了对不完美观测的鲁棒性，有效解决了TLS点云中玻璃反射伪影的去除问题。

Abstract: Terrestrial Laser Scanning (TLS) point clouds captured in urban environments frequently suffer from glass-induced reflection artifacts, severely degrading downstream applications. Existing reflection artifact removal methods generally rely on ideal reflection symmetry assumptions, yet their performance is limited by inaccurate glass estimation and insufficient geometric representations. To address these issues, we propose a novel unified framework aimed at robust reflection artifact removal: In the first stage, we leverage a multi-modal vision foundation model to produce initial glass masks, which are then refined using geometric cues to achieve high-precision glass regions, followed by glass completion to recover missing regions caused by no-return measurements on transparent surfaces; In the second stage, we propose a physics-driven descriptor, termed Reflection-aware Local-Global Geometric Similarity (RE-LGGS), which is grounded in actual laser reflection geometry and jointly encodes multi-scale geometric structures and orientation consistency using PCA-based local shape representations, thereby significantly improving robustness against imperfect observations. Extensive experiments on multiple public TLS datasets demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in reflection artifacts removal.

</details>


### [40] [PrismAvatar: Pseudo-Multiview Reconstruction and Subpixel Prism Rendering for Real-Time Stereoscopic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10550)
*Chufeng Fang,Dongdong Teng,Lilin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PrismAvatar是一个实时裸眼立体通信系统，通过单目视频重建可控高斯头部化身，并将其优化适配于亚像素编码的柱状透镜显示器，实现无需特殊眼镜的3D视频通话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时立体视频通信系统要么依赖专用多相机采集设备，要么将远程用户简化为单一平面视图，无法在消费级设备上实现真正的沉浸式裸眼立体通信。

Method: 提出PrismAvatar系统：（1）从单目人像视频重建可控3D高斯头部化身；（2）利用自然头部转动作为伪多视图（PMV）监督，对头发、耳朵、下颌轮廓等单目训练中弱观测区域进行约束；（3）将可靠侧脸帧按偏航角分箱、对齐到虚拟相机，并在严格的头-发域内进行监督；（4）引入轮廓感知损失和分阶段正则化抑制鬼影、alpha泄漏和深度不稳定；（5）运行时渲染32个虚拟视图，通过校准的亚像素路由掩码编码为4K柱状透镜光栅图像。

Result: 实时追踪原型达到10.65 FPS，经特定主体蒸馏的驱动版本将同一显示管线提升至38.49 FPS。

Conclusion: PrismAvatar首次实现了从单目视频到裸眼立体显示的端到端实时通信管线，通过伪多视图监督和显示感知优化，在消费级硬件上提供了可用的沉浸式远程沟通体验。

Abstract: Real-time stereoscopic video communication has long been a goal of immersive telepresence, yet practical systems still require specialized capture rigs or reduce remote users to a single portrait view. We present PrismAvatar, a Gaussian head-avatar system that connects monocular avatar capture with subpixel-encoded glasses-free lenticular display for real-time autostereoscopic communication. From a monocular portrait video, PrismAvatar reconstructs a controllable head avatar and optimizes it for the lateral viewing zones induced by the display. The method uses natural head turns as pseudo-multiview (PMV) supervision to constrain regions that are otherwise weakly observed in monocular training, including hair, ears, jaw contours, and neck boundaries. Reliable side frames are yaw-binned, aligned to virtual cameras, and supervised within a strict head-and-hair domain; contour-aware losses and staged regularization further suppress ghosting, alpha leakage, and depth instability while preserving lateral detail. At runtime, PrismAvatar renders 32 virtual views and encodes them into a 4K lenticular raster with calibrated subpixel-routing masks. The live-tracker prototype sustains 10.65 FPS, and a subject-specific distilled driver raises the same display pipeline to 38.49 FPS.

</details>


### [41] [Improving Adversarial Transferability on Vision-Language Pre-training Models via Surrogate-Specific Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10571)
*Lijia Yu,Jiuxin Cao,Yuchen Qiang,Changhao Chen,Yifei Huang,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeBias-Attack 通过梯度校正消除代理模型特异性偏差，从而提升 VLP 模型对抗样本的跨模型迁移性。它维护主分支和参考分支两条扰动路径，利用弱语义图像估计偏差并从主梯度中剔除，再结合上下文感知文本替换实现更强的黑盒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迁移的 VLP 黑盒攻击中，对抗优化过度依赖代理模型的响应而非输入语义，导致更新方向仅在代理模型上有效、迁移到未知目标模型时性能骤降。作者将这一现象定义为"代理模型特异性偏差"（surrogate-specific bias），并以此为动机提出通过校正该偏差来提升迁移性的方法。

Method: DeBias-Attack 维护两条扰动分支：（1）主分支在原始图像上优化扰动，获取用于破坏图像-文本对齐的对抗梯度；（2）参考分支在弱语义图像（数据集均值图像叠加每轮重采样的小高斯噪声）上优化扰动，因其缺乏清晰视觉内容，参考梯度主要反映代理模型响应而非图像语义，从而估计代理特异性偏差。随后，将主梯度在参考梯度方向上的投影分量剔除，用校正后的梯度更新对抗图像，最后结合上下文感知的文本替换完成攻击。

Result: 实验表明，DeBias-Attack 在多种 VLP 模型、下游任务以及开源和闭源多模态大语言模型上均展现出强大的跨模型攻击性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DeBias-Attack 是首个通过梯度校正来纠正代理特异性偏差的基于迁移的 VLP 攻击方法，有效提升了对抗样本的跨模型迁移性，为 VLP 模型鲁棒性研究提供了新的视角和更强的攻击基线。

Abstract: Adversarial examples reveal vulnerabilities in Vision-Language Pre-training (VLP) models and provide insights for improving robustness. A key property is cross-model transferability, which enables transfer-based black-box attacks. However, existing attacks often rely heavily on the surrogate model, causing cross-model performance drops. One reason is that adversarial optimization may follow surrogate model responses more than input semantics, making the update direction effective on the surrogate but less transferable to unseen targets. We refer to this dependency as surrogate-specific bias. Motivated by this observation, DeBias-Attack improves transferability by correcting surrogate-specific bias in adversarial optimization directions. It maintains two perturbation branches. The main branch optimizes a perturbation on the original image and obtains the adversarial gradient used to disrupt image-text alignment. The reference branch optimizes a perturbation on a weak-semantic image constructed from the dataset mean image with small Gaussian noise resampled at each iteration. Since this weak-semantic image contains little clear visual content, its optimization reflects surrogate responses more than image semantics, and its reference gradient estimates surrogate-specific bias. DeBias-Attack removes the aligned projection of the main gradient on the reference gradient before updating the adversarial image, then performs context-aware text substitution using the updated adversarial image. DeBias-Attack is the first transfer-based VLP attack that corrects surrogate-specific bias through gradient correction. Experiments show strong performance across VLP models, downstream tasks, and open-source and closed-source multimodal large language models.

</details>


### [42] [Segment and Select: Vision-Language Segmentation in 3D Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10594)
*Yulin Chen,Zhihang Zhong,Yuenan Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEGA3D提出了一种直接基于细粒度视觉信息的三维视觉-语言分割范式，摒弃了传统粗粒度超点（superpoint）表示，通过掩码候选生成、LLM语义空间选择以及回环验证模块，在多个基准上显著提升了分割精度和边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉-语言分割方法依赖粗粒度的超点（superpoint）表示来降低计算复杂度，但这导致分割质量差、物体边界模糊。本文旨在摆脱超点依赖，直接利用细粒度视觉信息来实现更精确的分割。

Method: 提出SEGA3D范式，包含三大模块：(1) 掩码候选生成器，产生细粒度的类别掩码候选；(2) 利用大语言模型（LLM）基于语言描述和视觉特征生成语义与空间信息，再通过语义-空间选择器（SSS）筛选出排名靠前的掩码候选；(3) 回环验证模块（LVM），从候选掩码中最终生成精确分割掩码。

Result: 在ScanRefer、ScanNet和Matterport3D基准上取得有竞争力的性能：在ScanNet和Matterport3D上分别超越最佳方法8.3 mIoU和5.3 mIoU。

Conclusion: SEGA3D通过摒弃超点依赖、直接在细粒度视觉信息上操作，显著提升了3D视觉-语言分割的精度和边界完整性，展现了该范式的优越性。

Abstract: 3D vision-language segmentation aims to segment target objects in 3D scenarios according to the linguistic instructions and visual observations. Prior art heavily relies on the coarse superpoint representation to reduce the computation complexity, which suffers from poor segmentation quality and messy object boundaries. In this paper, we propose the SEGment-And-select (SEGA3D) paradigm for 3D visionlanguage segmentation that directly operates on the fine-grained visual information and is free from the superpoint dependency. Specifically, we first leverage a mask candidate generator to provide fine-grained categorical mask candidates, substantially improving the quality of candidate masks over the superpoint counterparts. Then, a Large Language Model (LLM) is utilized to generate the semantic and spatial information based on the linguistic description and visual features. The LLM output and visual features are fed to the Semantic-Spatial Selector (SSS) to produce the top-ranking mask candidates. Eventually, the Loopback Verification Module (LVM) is designed to yield the segmentation mask from the selected candidate masks. Our SEGA3D attains competitive performance on ScanRefer, ScanNet and Matterport3D benchmarks. Notably, our SEGA3D surpasses the top-performing counterpart by 8.3 mIoU and 5.3 mIoU on ScanNet and Matterport3D, respectively. Codes will be available upon publication.

</details>


### [43] [Globally Localizing Lunar Rover in Pixels via Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10602)
*Mao Chen,Xu Yang,Chuankai Liu,Xiangkai Zhang,Xiaoxue Wang,Zheng Bo,Zuoyu Zhang,Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WARG是一种面向月球车跨视角定位的图匹配框架，通过统一图学习和重投影图匹配解决月球环境中的跨视角对齐难题，在玉兔二号真实数据上实现1.68米定位误差，模型仅1.56M参数，运行频率5.49Hz，接近GNSS级更新率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球车在无GNSS的月面环境中进行长距离自主探索时，局部定位方法存在累积漂移问题。跨视角定位（匹配 rover-view 和 satellite-view 图像）提供了一种无漂移的全局定位方案，但月球环境面临三大独特挑战：实体间纠缠（inter-entity entanglement）、视角差异（inter-viewpoint divergence）以及仿真到真实的域偏移（sim-to-real domain shift），现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出WARG（Warped Alignment of Reprojected Graphs）框架，核心包括：1）统一图学习（unified graph learning），将 rover 视图和卫星视图的图像特征编码为图结构表征；2）重投影图匹配（reprojected graph matching），通过几何重投影建立跨视角图节点间的对应关系，实现鲁棒的跨视角对齐。模型在合成数据集LuSNAR上预训练，仅含1.56M参数。

Result: 1）在LuSNAR合成数据集上平均测试误差0.32米；2）零样本泛化至月球南极合成区域，误差3.63米；3）在玉兔二号真实数据上（100m×100m搜索范围），定位误差仅1.68米，对应低分辨率卫星影像（1.40m/pixel）近单像素精度；4）推理速度5.49Hz（NVIDIA RTX A6000），模型参数量仅为先前轻量模型的16.12%。

Conclusion: WARG在月球跨视角定位任务中实现了高精度、高效率与强泛化能力，在真实月面数据上验证了近单像素级定位精度。更重要的是，作者发现WARG在跨视角定位学习过程中自然涌现了语义分割和结构推理等低级空间感知能力，揭示了其作为一种以极低标注成本获得空间智能的潜力范式。

Abstract: Precise rover localization is a prerequisite for autonomous lunar exploration, yet the absence of Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and the cumulative drift of local localization methods severely constrain long-range missions. Cross-view localization provides a promising drift-free global solution by matching rover-view and satellite-view imagery. However, the lunar environment poses unique challenges for correspondence alignment, including inter-entity entanglement, inter-viewpoint divergence, and simulation-to-real domain shift. To address these challenges, we propose Warped Alignment of Reprojected Graphs (WARG), a framework that leverages unified graph learning and reprojected graph matching for robust cross-view alignment. Pretrained on the synthetic LuSNAR dataset, WARG achieves an average test error of 0.32 m and demonstrates robust zero-shot generalization to the synthetic lunar south pole region with an error of 3.63 m. More importantly, when validated on real-world data from the YuTu-2 rover, WARG achieves a localization error of 1.68 m within a 100 m x 100 m search area, corresponding to nearly one-pixel precision in low-resolution satellite imagery with a spatial resolution of 1.40 m/pixel. Beyond accuracy, WARG is computationally efficient, containing only 1.56M parameters, corresponding to 16.12% of previous lightweight models, and operating at 5.49 Hz on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU, approaching GNSS-level update frequency. Finally, we observe that WARG naturally develops low-level spatial awareness, including semantic segmentation and structural reasoning, through cross-view localization learning, highlighting its potential as a promising paradigm for spatial intelligence with minimal annotation cost. The source code is available at https://github.com/maochen-casia/warg.

</details>


### [44] [GaussTrace: Provenance Analysis of 3D Gaussian Splatting Models with Evidence-based LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10612)
*Haoliang Han,Ziyuan Luo,Renjie Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussTrace 提出了一种为3DGS模型构建有向溯源图的方法，通过属性统计分析和编辑模拟，结合LLM的链式推理，在无需训练或编辑历史的情况下推断模型间的演化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS模型在数字平台上的广泛共享与迭代修改，使得知识产权保护和溯源追踪面临严峻挑战，现有方法缺乏对3DGS模型间演化关系的有效推断能力。

Method: GaussTrace将溯源分析形式化为基于证据的推理问题：(1) 对3DGS参数进行逐属性统计画像，捕获其内在特性；(2) 引入假设驱动的常见编辑操作模拟，为可能的变换路径提供辅助证据；(3) 统计线索与模拟线索联合输入大语言模型，通过结构化链式思维(CoT)推理，输出有向溯源推断及可解释的边理由。

Result: 实验表明，GaussTrace能有效构建不同3DGS模型间的演化关系图，提供准确、可解释且鲁棒的溯源图，且无需模型训练或访问编辑历史。

Conclusion: GaussTrace为3DGS模型的知识产权保护与溯源分析提供了一种无需训练、不依赖编辑历史的新范式，其基于证据推理与LLM结合的思路具有良好的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a powerful technique for creating high-fidelity 3D assets. However, the widespread sharing and iterative modification of 3DGS models across digital platforms create pressing challenges for intellectual property protection and forensic traceability. To address this, we propose GaussTrace, a novel framework for constructing directed provenance graphs for 3DGS models. GaussTrace formulates provenance analysis as an evidence-based reasoning problem. It builds upon attribute-wise statistical profiling of 3DGS parameters to capture intrinsic properties. Moreover, we introduce hypothesis-driven editing simulations of common operations to provide auxiliary evidence for plausible transformation pathways. These statistical and simulated cues jointly enable a Large Language Model (LLM) to perform structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, yielding directional provenance inferences and explainable edge reasons. Experimental results demonstrate that GaussTrace effectively constructs evolutionary relationships among diverse 3DGS models, delivering accurate, interpretable, and robust provenance graphs without requiring model training or access to editing histories. Project page: https://haolianghan.github.io/GaussTrace.

</details>


### [45] [SSR-Merge: Subspace Signal Routing for Training-Free LoRA Merging in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10617)
*Zhengxuan Wei,Yi Dong,Zonghui Li,Xianhui Lin,Xing Liu,Hong Gu,Shaofeng Zhang,Wenbin Li,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出子空间信号路由(SSR)方法，通过在信号层面进行路由而非参数空间合并来解决LoRA合并中的参数干扰问题，并从理论上证明其等价于最小二乘解的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA合并方法在共享参数空间中存在严重的参数干扰，导致不同生成能力之间发生破坏性冲突，需要一个更有效的合并方案。

Method: SSR通过三步解决干扰：(1)沿秩维度拼接候选LoRA构建统一子空间；(2)利用逆相关矩阵对混合信号进行去相关；(3)使用方向引导矩阵将纯化后的信号路由至各自任务特定子空间。理论上证明了SSR与OLS解一致，并利用充分统计量的可加性设计了流式算法以实现在线更新、降低内存和计算开销。

Result: 大量实验验证SSR在保持可比效率的同时，显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SSR通过信号层面的路由机制有效解决了LoRA合并中的参数干扰问题，兼具数学最优性和实际部署效率。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) merging can efficiently combine diverse generative capabilities from multiple trained LoRAs for a diffusion model. However, existing LoRA merging techniques often suffer from severe parameter interference, causing destructive collisions in the shared parameter space. To address this, we propose Subspace Signal Routing (SSR), which resolves interference by routing internal signals instead of performing parameter-space merge. Specifically, SSR first constructs a unified subspace by concatenating candidate LoRAs along the rank dimension. Next, SSR employs an inverse correlation matrix to decorrelate mixed signals within this space. Finally, a directional guide matrix steers these purified signals into their respective task-specific subspaces. We provide a rigorous theoretical analysis proving that SSR aligns with the Ordinary Least Squares (OLS) solution, thereby ensuring mathematical optimality. We utilize the additivity of sufficient statistics to design a streaming algorithm. This enables on-the-fly updates that significantly reduce memory overhead and computation time. Extensive experiments validate that SSR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/nagara214/SSR-Merge.

</details>


### [46] [Can Image Models Imagine Time? ImageTime: A Novel Benchmark for Probing Visual World Modeling Through Spatiotemporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10620)
*Xinrui Wu,Lichen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ImageTime 是一个诊断基准，通过要求模型在单张图像中生成包含初始状态、动作开始、过渡状态和最终状态四个有序关键帧，来探测图像生成模型的时空一致性能力，揭示当前模型在维持时序视觉世界状态方面的成败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图像生成模型虽能产出高质量静态图像，但它们在表达视觉世界随时间变化方面的能力尚不清晰。现有评测多聚焦于单图像正确性、组合对齐或视频质量，无法回答模型是否能连贯地想象一个时序有序的过程。而实际工作流（如故事板、分步插图、参考引导编辑、视频预可视化）恰需要跨多个视觉状态保持身份、物体、空间关系和因果顺序。

Method: 提出 ImageTime 诊断基准：(1) 给定动作指令和可选参考图像（指定初始状态），模型需在一张图像中生成四个有序关键帧（初始状态、动作开始、过渡状态、最终状态）；(2) 任务按渐进能力层级组织，每个场景分解为阶段性状态谓词、跨帧时序约束和禁止性因果违规；(3) 采用 GPT-5.5 作为结构化 VLM-as-judge 进行评分，产出可解释的能力分数、诊断子分数和失败标签。

Result: 通过多模型家族的基准测试，ImageTime 系统性地揭示了当前图像生成系统在要求维持连贯视觉世界状态随时间变化时的成功之处、失败模式及漂移现象。

Conclusion: ImageTime 为评估图像生成模型的时序世界建模能力提供了一种有效的诊断工具，四关键帧协议在时间要求上比单图像生成更高，同时避免了密集视频动态带来的混淆因素，有助于推动模型在时序一致性方面的发展。

Abstract: Image generation models now produce high-quality static images, yet their ability to represent how a visual world changes over time remains poorly understood. Practical workflows such as storyboarding, step-by-step illustration, reference-guided editing, and video previsualization require models to preserve identities, objects, spatial relations, and causal order across multiple visual states. Existing evaluations largely measure single-image correctness, compositional alignment, or video quality, leaving open whether an image model can coherently imagine a temporally ordered process. We introduce ImageTime, a diagnostic benchmark that uses spatiotemporal consistency as a behavioral probe of visual world modeling in image generation. Given an action instruction, and optionally a reference image specifying the initial state, a model must generate one image containing four ordered key states: initial state, action onset, transition state, and final state. This four-keyframe protocol is more temporally demanding than single-image generation while avoiding the confounds of dense video dynamics. ImageTime organizes tasks with a progressive capability hierarchy and decomposes each scenario into stage-wise state predicates, cross-frame temporal constraints, and forbidden causal violations. GPT-5.5 scores all generated images under a structured VLM-as-judge protocol, producing interpretable capability scores, diagnostic subscores, and failure labels. Through multi-family benchmarking, ImageTime reveals where current image generation systems succeed, fail, and drift when asked to maintain coherent visual world states over time.

</details>


### [47] [Leveraging Metric Depth for Relative Depth Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10628)
*Xiaoyang Bi,Shuaikun Liu,Zhaohong Liu,Yuxin Yang,Zhe Zhao,Mengshi Qi,Liang Liu,Huadong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向2025年SoccerNet单目深度估计竞赛的解决方案，利用大规模预训练模型的零样本能力进行度量深度学习，在仅有数千训练样本的足球场景中实现有效的相对深度预测，最终在挑战集上获得2.68×10⁻³的得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球场景下的相对深度预测极具挑战性，主要困难在于可用训练样本仅有数千张，数据规模严重受限。因此需要探索如何利用大规模预训练模型的零样本能力来克服数据稀缺问题。

Method: 该方法的核心思路是：借助在大规模数据集上预训练的深度估计模型所具备的强大零样本泛化能力，将其迁移至足球场景，用于学习度量深度（metric depth），进而实现有效的相对深度预测。论文未披露具体的模型架构、微调策略或损失函数等技术细节。

Result: 该方法在2025年SoccerNet单目深度估计竞赛的挑战集上取得了2.68×10⁻³的得分。

Conclusion: 即使在训练数据极为有限的情况下，充分利用大规模预训练模型的零样本能力是解决特定领域单目深度估计问题的有效途径，该方法在足球场景相对深度预测任务上取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: We present our solution to the 2025 SoccerNet Monocular Depth Estimation Competition Challenge. Predicting the relative depth in football scenarios is challenging, especially with only thousands of training samples available. To address this issue, our method leverages the powerful zero-shot capabilities of models pretrained on large-scale datasets to learn metric depth for effective relative depth prediction, achieving a score of $2.68 \times 10^{-3}$ on the challenge set.

</details>


### [48] [ChartLens: A Dual-Branch Framework for Chart Data Correction and Factual Summary Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10640)
*Hao Liu,Ruping Cao,Kun Wang,Zhiran Li,Fan Liu,Yupeng Hu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ChartLens双分支框架，用于图表数据纠正与摘要精炼，在DataMFM Challenge Track 2中获得冠军（总分69.10）。该框架包含SAVC（结构感知CSV验证与纠正）和TRSR（文本保留引导的摘要精炼）两个模块，分别提升结构化数据提取可靠性和摘要生成的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表理解任务需要同时满足两个互补要求：准确的结构化数据提取和事实忠实的自然语言摘要生成。现有方法在这两方面难以兼顾，需要一种能协同提升数据恢复准确性与摘要事实性的框架。

Method: 提出ChartLens双分支框架：(1) SAVC模块——结构感知的CSV验证与纠正，通过验证和修正提升结构化数据提取的可靠性；(2) TRSR模块——文本保留引导的摘要精炼，通过保留图表中的关键文本和数值证据来增强摘要生成质量。整体结合了模型自适应、基于纠正的生成以及OCR辅助证据锚定三种策略。

Result: 在DataMFM Challenge Track 2测试集上，最终系统取得69.10的总分，排名第一，验证了该方法在准确图表理解方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ChartLens通过双分支设计有效解决了图表理解中数据提取与摘要生成双重挑战，其冠军成绩证明了结构感知验证与文本保留精炼策略的协同优势。代码将开源。

Abstract: In this report, we present our champion solution for the DataMFM Challenge Track 2: Chart Understanding. This track requires models to recover structured chart data and generate faithful natural-language summaries from chart images. To address the complementary requirements of accurate data extraction and factual narration, we propose ChartLens, a dual-branch framework for chart data correction and summary refinement. ChartLens consists of two key modules: Structure-Aware CSV Verification and Correction (SAVC) and Text-Retention-Guided Summary Refinement (TRSR). SAVC improves the reliability of structured data extraction through verification and correction, while TRSR enhances summary generation by preserving critical textual and numerical evidence from charts. By combining model adaptation, correction-based generation, and OCR-assisted evidence grounding, ChartLens improves both structured data recovery and summary factuality. On the test set, our final system achieves an overall score of 69.10 and ranks first in Track 2, demonstrating its effectiveness for accurate chart understanding. Our code will be released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/CVPRW26-ChartLens.

</details>


### [49] [ManiSplat: Manipulation Trajectory Synthesis from Monocular Video via Decoupled 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10645)
*Wenhao Hu,Haonan Zhou,Liu Liu,Yun Du,Xinjie Wang,Ziang Li,Zhizhong Su,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManiSplat 提出一种从单目机器人视频中重建可控、解耦的3D高斯数字孪生框架，通过图结构解耦表示和任务导向时空对齐，实现交互式动态场景的高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法在静态重建上表现优异，但难以扩展到具有铰接机器人和可操作物体的交互式环境中，主要挑战在于复杂的接触交互和突发的姿态变化。因此需要一个能直接从单目机器人视频中重建可控、解耦的数字孪生的统一框架。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) 图结构解耦表示（Graph-Structured Disentangled Representation），将机器人、物体和背景分离为场景图中独立可优化的高斯子场；(2) 任务导向时空对齐模块（Task-Oriented Spatio-Temporal Alignment），利用操作任务在"运动阶段"和"技能阶段"之间交替的内在逻辑，构建准确的伪真值轨迹；(3) 联合光度-几何优化，确保重建场景的时间一致性、物理一致性和仿真就绪性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能高保真、高可控性地重建交互驱动的动态场景，并有效支持下游机器人任务和策略学习。

Conclusion: ManiSplat 通过解耦表示和任务感知的时空对齐，成功将3D高斯泼溅扩展到机器人交互场景，为机器人操作任务的可控仿真重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic and interactive 3D scenes from real-world observations remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. While recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting have enabled high-fidelity static reconstruction, extending it to interactive environments with articulated robots and manipulable objects remains difficult due to complex contact interactions and abrupt pose changes. To address these challenges, we introduce ManiSplat, a unified framework that reconstructs controllable and decoupled Gaussian digital twins directly from monocular ego-view robotic videos. Our method introduces a Graph-Structured Disentangled Representation that separates the robot, objects, and background into independently optimizable Gaussian subfields organized within a scene graph. To ensure stability, we propose a Task-Oriented Spatio-Temporal Alignment module that leverages the inherent logic of manipulation tasks-alternating between Motion and Skill phases-to construct accurate pseudo-ground-truth trajectories. Finally, a joint photometric-geometric optimization ensures the reconstructed scenes are temporally coherent, physically consistent, and simulation-ready. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach reconstructs interaction-driven dynamic scenes with high fidelity and controllability, effectively supporting downstream robotic tasks and policy learning.

</details>


### [50] [Kwai Keye-VL-2.0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10651)
*Kwai Keye Team,Bin Wen,Changyi Liu,Chengru Song,Chongling Rao,Guowang Zhang,Han Li,Haonan Fan,Hengrui Ju,Jiankang Chen,Jiapeng Chen,Jiawei Yuan,Kaixuan Yang,Kaiyu Jiang,Kun Gai,Lingzhi Zhou,Na Nie,Sen Na,Tianke Zhang,Tingting Gao,Xuanyu Zheng,Yulong Chen,Fan Yang,Haixuan Gao,Lele Yang,Mingqiao Liu,Muxi Diao,Qi Zhang,Qile Su,Wei Chen,Wentao Hong,Xingyu Lu,Yancheng Long,Yankai Yang,Yingxin Li,Yiyang Fan,Yu Xia,Yuzhe Chen,Ziliang Lai,Chuan Yi,Haonan Jia,Tianming Liang,Weixin Xu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yang Tian,Yufei Han,Feng Han,Hang Li,Jing Wang,Jinghui Jia,Junmin Chen,Junyu Shi,Ruilin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 快手开源了Kwai Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B，一个基于MoE架构的多模态基础模型，首次将DeepSeek稀疏注意力适配到GQA多模态架构中，实现256K无损上下文处理，并通过跨模态多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）结合强化学习，在长视频理解和智能体任务上达到同等规模模型的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小时级长视频理解面临三大核心挑战：(1)超长上下文处理困难；(2)视频信息高度冗余；(3)计算成本过高。此外，多任务对齐训练中存在灾难性遗忘问题。现有方案难以在保持高效推理的同时实现精细的时序定位和长程依赖建模。

Method: (1)首次将DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA)适配到基于GQA的多模态架构，实现无损256K上下文处理，兼顾关键帧捕获与长程时序依赖；(2)构建高度优化的训练推理基础设施，包括可扩展视频I/O、异构ViT-LM并行及定制化DSA内核；(3)提出跨模态多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD），结合Context-RL和Video-RL，将密集token级教师反馈蒸馏至仅激活3B参数的MoE骨干网络，实现多模态自我修正的智能体协作能力。

Result: 在视频理解、时序定位、推理、STEM和智能体等多个基准上的评估表明，Keye-VL-2.0在同等规模模型中达到SOTA性能，尤其在TimeLens的细粒度时序定位，以及Video-MME-v2和LongVideoBench的长视频理解上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏注意力架构创新与多教师蒸馏训练策略，Keye-VL-2.0成功在仅激活3B参数的高效推理下实现了超长视频的精细理解与智能体任务能力，模型已开源以推动多模态智能体应用的社区发展。

Abstract: We introduce Kwai Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B, an open-source Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) multimodal foundation model designed to advance long-video understanding and agentic intelligence. To address the challenges of ultra-long contexts, information redundancy, and prohibitive computational costs inherent in hour-level videos, Keye-VL-2.0 is the first to adapt DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) to GQA-based multimodal architectures, enabling lossless 256K context processing while capturing critical frames and long-range temporal dependencies. This architecture is underpinned by a highly optimized training and inference infrastructure, including scalable video I/O, heterogeneous ViT-LM parallelism, and custom DSA kernels that significantly maximize throughput and minimize computational overhead. Furthermore, to overcome the algorithmic dilemma of catastrophic forgetting during multi-task alignment, we introduce Cross-Modal Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) paired with Context-RL and Video-RL. By distilling dense token-level teacher feedback from on-policy rollouts back into the MoE backbone, which activates only 3B parameters, Keye-VL-2.0 natively empowers advanced agent collaboration across Code, Tool, and Search scenarios with multimodal self-correction. Extensive evaluations across video understanding, temporal grounding, reasoning, STEM, and agent benchmarks demonstrate that Keye-VL-2.0-30B-A3B achieves state-of-the-art performance among models of similar scale, particularly excelling in fine-grained temporal localization on TimeLens and long-video comprehension on Video-MME-v2 and LongVideoBench. We release our model checkpoints to accelerate community progress toward scalable and robust multimodal agentic applications.

</details>


### [51] [STEDiff: Strengthening Text Embedding for Text-to-Image Alignment in Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10653)
*Hailan Zhang,Haipeng Liu,Bo Fu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STEDiff 是一种无需训练的方法，通过直接在文本嵌入空间中操作 [EOT] token 来增强子句语义，并引入语义增强损失施加空间约束，从而显著改善文本到图像生成中的语义对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练 T2I 模型在复杂提示下常因随机噪声和模型固有局限，导致语义对齐失败——如遗漏对象、属性与实体绑定错误。而当前解决方案要么依赖昂贵的微调，要么需要繁重的布局先验标注，缺乏一种轻量高效的改进手段。

Method: 提出 STEDiff（训练自由）：(1) 利用 [EOT] token 聚合子句的语义信息，强化后将对应 token 替换回原始提示的文本嵌入中；(2) 引入新颖的语义增强损失函数，施加空间约束，确保每个实体的语义精确映射到图像对应区域。

Result: 在 T2I-CompBench 上进行了广泛的定量与定性评估，结果表明 STEDiff 在复杂场景下显著提升了语义一致性和生成完整性。

Conclusion: STEDiff 通过在嵌入空间进行语义增强，无需训练即可有效解决复杂提示下的语义对齐难题，为 T2I 生成提供了一种高效、实用的新方案。

Abstract: Although pretrained text-to-image (T2I) generation models can produce high-quality images, they often fail to faithfully reflect the semantic intent of complex prompts due to stochastic noise and inherent model limitations. This issue frequently manifests as the model overlooking specific objects or failing to correctly bind attributes to their corresponding entities, a challenge referred to as semantic alignment. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive fine-tuning or labor-intensive layout priors, we propose STEDiff, a training-free method designed to enhance semantic representations directly within the text-embedding space. Specifically, we introduce a method that primarily leverages the [EOT] token to strengthen the relevant semantics of sub-sentences and then replaces the corresponding tokens in the original prompt. Furthermore, a novel semantic enhancement loss is incorporated to enforce spatial constraints, ensuring that the semantics of each entity are precisely mapped to their respective image regions. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations on the T2I-CompBench demonstrate that our method notably improves semantic consistency and generation integrity in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [52] [Envision4D: Envisioning Visual Futures via Feed-forward 4D Gaussian Splatting for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10656)
*Qi Song,Yifei He,Chi Zhang,Zheng Fu,Xuhe Zhao,Mengmeng Yang,Kun Jiang,Rui Huang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Envision4D 是一个面向自动驾驶场景的自监督前馈框架，无需位姿先验即可实现动态场景的未来外推，通过未来位姿预测、层内时序注意力和渐进训练策略，解决了现有方法在大位移下的鬼影及简化运动假设问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈范式主要面向插值设计，扩展到未来外推时存在大位移下的鬼影伪影，且受限于简化的运动假设或严格的未来先验，无法有效处理非线性动态场景。

Method: 提出 Envision4D 框架：(1) Future Pose Prediction 模块，通过迭代去噪过程推断未来相机参数；(2) In-layer Temporal Attention 与 Conditioned Motion Lifting，将高不确定性外推转化为鲁棒的关系映射以捕捉非线性动态；(3) Progressive Training Strategy，稳定无监督运动学习，缓解误差累积。

Result: 大量实验表明，Envision4D 在未来视图合成任务上达到了最先进的性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Envision4D 通过无位姿自监督的前馈框架，有效克服了未来外推中的关键瓶颈，为自动驾驶中动态场景的未来演化预测提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting the future evolution of dynamic scenes is crucial in autonomous driving. However, existing feed-forward paradigms are primarily designed for interpolation. When extended to future extrapolation, they suffer from ghosting artifacts under large displacements and are constrained by simplified motion assumptions or strict future priors. To overcome these challenges, we propose Envision4D, a fully self-supervised feed-forward framework for pose-free future extrapolation. Specifically, we introduce a Future Pose Prediction module that infers future camera parameters via an iterative denoising process. Furthermore, to capture non-linear dynamics, we propose In-layer Temporal Attention and employ Conditioned Motion Lifting, which transforms the highly uncertain extrapolation process into robust relational mappings. Finally, a Progressive Training Strategy is utilized to stabilize unsupervised motion learning against error accumulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Envision4D achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in future view synthesis.

</details>


### [53] [Analyzing Training-Free Corruption Detection for Object Detection Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10666)
*Christian Sieberichs,Simon Geerkens,Thomas Waschulzik,Viswanathan Ramesh,Alexander Braun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究基于特征空间的方法在目标检测标注错误检测中的适用性，发现此类方法能可靠地暴露语义标签错误，但位置错误仍难以检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注错误在计算机视觉数据集中普遍存在，严重降低系统性能。现有特征空间方法虽快速且可解释，但其在目标检测标注（包含语义和空间信息）上的行为尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过适配一种现有特征空间方法，在多个预训练嵌入模型上评估，覆盖合成噪声类型（对称噪声、非对称噪声、位置噪声）以及VOC2012和KITTI数据集上的真实标注错误。

Result: 特征空间方法能够可靠地检测语义标签错误，但对位置（边界框）错误的检测效果不佳。

Conclusion: 特征空间方法适用于目标检测中的语义错误检测，但需要进一步研究以解决空间/位置标注错误的检测问题。代码和真实标注损坏数据已开源。

Abstract: Annotation errors are widespread in computer vision datasets and can significantly degrade the performance of systems trained on them, particularly in complex tasks such as object detection. Several approaches exist to identify annotation errors, including training-free feature-space methods which provide a fast and interpretable way to analyze annotations. However, the behavior on object detection annotations, which include semantic and spatial information, remains largely unexplored.
  In this work we analyze the applicability of feature-space-based approaches for detecting annotation errors in object detection datasets. By adapting an existing feature-space method, we show that such approaches reliably expose semantic mislabel, while positional errors remain difficult to detect. We evaluate this behavior across multiple pretrained embedding models, synthetic noise types (symmetric, asymmetric, and positional), and real-world annotation errors using VOC2012 and KITTI.
  All code and real-world corruptions are publicly available at the following repository: https://github.com/ ChristianSieberichs/BoundingBox\_corruption\_detection

</details>


### [54] [FadeMem: Distance-Aware Memory Consolidation for Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10671)
*Yu Lu,Junjie Yang,Piotr Koniusz,YuXin Song,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FadeMem 提出了一种距离感知的KV缓存合并机制，通过幂律时间分配将历史KV块组织为"近密远疏"的层级结构，在固定缓存预算下兼顾短期动态细节与长期身份/场景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成中，历史KV缓存随视频长度线性增长。现有有界缓存方法（局部窗口、sink token、压缩记忆）通常为历史不同部分分配固定角色，忽略了时间距离对信息需求的差异化：细粒度细节快速失相关，而粗粒度场景结构和身份特征在更长时域内持续有用。这种频率依赖的时间衰减特性未被充分利用。

Method: FadeMem 在固定缓存预算下，将历史KV块组织为时间层级结构。新生成的历史以细粒度条目插入；随着时间推移，相邻旧条目按幂律时间分配调度逐步合并（merge），形成"近密远疏"的记忆布局。该方法无需架构修改，直接作用于KV缓存管理。

Result: 实验表明，相比现有有界缓存策略，FadeMem 在主体一致性（subject consistency）、背景稳定性（background stability）和时间连贯性（temporal coherence）上均有提升。

Conclusion: FadeMem 通过模拟频率依赖的时间衰减，以距离感知的层级化缓存合并策略，有效平衡了短时动态保真与长程一致性，为自回归视频生成的高效缓存管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generators synthesize long videos by generating successive temporal segments, but their historical KV cache grows with video length. Existing bounded-cache methods reduce this cost with local windows, sink tokens, or compressed memory states, yet they usually assign fixed roles to different parts of the history. We propose FadeMem, a distance-aware KV memory consolidation mechanism that organizes historical KV blocks into a temporal hierarchy under a fixed cache budget. This design is motivated by frequency-dependent temporal decay: fine details decorrelate quickly, while coarse scene structure and identity remain useful over longer horizons. During generation, new history is inserted as fine-grained entries, while older adjacent entries are progressively merged under a power-law temporal allocation schedule, yielding a dense-near, sparse-far memory within one cache. Without architectural changes, FadeMem preserves recent context for short-term dynamics and compact long-range anchors for identity and scene coherence. Experiments show improved subject consistency, background stability, and temporal coherence over existing bounded-cache strategies.

</details>


### [55] [Don't waste SAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10696)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了SAM及其微调模型在三个垃圾分割数据集上的泛化能力，发现微调后的SAM-ViT-H在Zerowaste和TACO数据集上以+30 IoU显著超越SOTA，在TrashCan 1.0上仅差-1.44，证明微调SAM作为基础模型对下游垃圾分割任务至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM虽在多种任务上展现出卓越的零样本分割性能，但在特定领域（如垃圾分割）仍难以提供精确分割。鉴于垃圾分割任务面临遮挡、可变形物体、透明物体及与背景易混淆等挑战，且需要大量高技能标注，本文旨在系统评估SAM及其微调策略在垃圾分割领域的泛化能力，探索将其作为基础模型支撑分割流程的可行性。

Method: 选取三个真实场景的垃圾分割数据集（Zerowaste、TACO、TrashCan 1.0）作为评估基准；以SAM（特别是SAM-ViT-H变体）作为基础模型，对其进行下游任务微调；将微调后的模型与各数据集当前最先进方法进行IoU指标对比。

Result: 微调后的SAM-ViT-H在Zerowaste和TACO数据集上以+30 IoU的大幅优势超越现有SOTA；在TrashCan 1.0数据集上性能接近SOTA，仅差-1.44 IoU。

Conclusion: 微调SAM作为基础模型是提升下游垃圾分割任务泛化能力的关键步骤，SAM不应被忽视或浪费。

Abstract: Meta AI has recently released the Segment Anything Model (SAM), which demonstrates exceptional zero-shot image segmentation performance across various tasks with remarkable accuracy. Despite its inability to provide accurate segmentation across multiple research fields, SAM still serves as a valuable starting point for supporting the segmentation pipeline process, particularly for tasks that require extensive and senior skills annotations. This study aims to evaluate the generalization of SAM and fine-tuning SAM models using three waste segmentation datasets. Although they are captured from real scenes as SAM was pretrained on, these datasets present several challenges, including occlusions, deformable objects, transparency, and objects easily confused with backgrounds. In our findings, the fine-tuned SAM-ViT-H model outperforms the state-ofthe-art Zerowaste, and TACO datasets with a significant increase of +30 in IoU, and it closely approaches performance levels of TrashCan 1.0, with only a -1.44 difference. After evaluating these popular waste datasets, it became evident that fine-tuning SAM as a foundational model is a crucial step for providing better generalization for downstream waste segmentation tasks. Therefore, SAM should not be disregarded or wasted.

</details>


### [56] [Using the YOLOv12 Model for Verifying the Correct Color Sequence of Wires in Network Cables (Patch Cords) on the Production Line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10699)
*Amin Doroodchi,Danial Soleimany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLO第十二版本目标检测模型的智能系统，用于自动识别网络线缆连接器中线对的颜色排序，实现了约98%的精确率，能够替代人工目检，减少生产线上的人为错误并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络线缆生产中，连接器内部线对的颜色顺序正确性至关重要，任何错位或颜色排序错误都会导致产品缺陷并带来重大成本。传统基于数码显微镜的人工目检方法耗时长、繁琐且易受人为错误影响，因此亟需一种自动化、高精度的智能检测方案。

Method: 采用基于YOLO第十二版本（单阶段架构）的目标检测模型，结合注意力机制进行训练。数据集包含2500张来自网络连接器显微视图的图像，按70%训练、15%验证、15%测试的比例划分。模型通过单阶段检测架构学习线缆颜色位置特征，实现对线序的自动识别与验证。

Result: 该系统实现了约98%的线缆检测精确率；总体平均准确率约95%，分类精确率约99%，召回率约98%。结果表明该系统能够在生产线上实时可靠地验证线缆颜色排序，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于YOLOv12的智能检测系统能够可靠地、实时地完成线缆颜色排序验证，有效减少人为错误并提升制造过程效率，在工业生产线场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In the production process of network cables, ensuring the correct color sequence of wire pairs inside the standard connector plays a critical role in the final performance of the cable, as any misplacement or color-ordering error can lead to defective products and impose significant costs. Traditional inspection methods based on visual examination through digital microscopes are typically time-consuming, tedious, and prone to human error. In this study, an intelligent system based on the twelfth version of the YOLO1 object detection model was developed to identify the position and verify the correct color sequence of wires in patch cords. The dataset used consisted of 2,500 images captured from microscopic views of network connectors, which were divided into 70% for training, 15% for validation, and 15% for testing. The proposed model, leveraging a single-stage architecture and attention mechanisms during learning, achieved highly accurate wire detection with approximately 98% precision. Additionally, the overall mean accuracy, classification precision, and recall were around 95%, 99%, and 98%, respectively. The results demonstrate that this system can reliably and in real time verify the correctness of wire color sequencing on the production line without the need for human intervention, thereby reducing human error and enhancing efficiency in the manufacturing process.

</details>


### [57] [Vector Map as Language: Toward Unified Remote Sensing Vector Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10701)
*Yinglong Yan,Yunkai Yang,Haoyi Wang,Wei Fu,Linshan Wu,Honghu Pan,Shaobo Xia,Shanghang Zhang,Hao Chen,Leyuan Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VecLang提出将多类别遥感矢量制图统一为结构化文本生成任务，设计了类GeoJSON的矢量语言表达，结合渐进式视觉-语言映射框架和分层强化学习优化，实现跨类别、跨数据集的统一矢量制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感矢量制图方法通常将对象表示为多边形或图结构，但多边形难以捕捉拓扑关系，图结构又容易模糊实例边界，且这些方法各自只适用于特定类别，缺乏一个能统一处理多类别、异构地理实体的通用框架。受语言作为灵活表达媒介的启发，作者希望用文本形式统一表达几何、语义和拓扑信息。

Method: 1. 提出VecLang范式，将多类矢量制图重新表述为结构化文本生成，编码为类GeoJSON格式的矢量语言；2. 设计渐进式视觉-语言映射框架，先定位矢量化单元，再生成结构化地图元素；3. 引入分层矢量语言优化（Hierarchical Vector Language Optimization），利用强化学习提升语法有效性、内容保真度和地图可执行性；4. 构建VecMap-Bench基准数据集，包含54K图像和800K实例，覆盖标准与泛化评估设置。

Result: VecLang在单类和多类矢量制图任务上均表现优异，并在跨数据集泛化和开放词汇泛化方面展现出强大能力。

Conclusion: 将矢量制图统一为语言生成任务是可行且有效的，VecLang实现了异构地理实体的统一建模，突破了传统方法按类别分别处理的局限，具备良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Remote sensing vector mapping aims to generate structured maps of geospatial entities, such as buildings, roads, and water bodies, from remote sensing imagery. In practice, vector maps usually contain multiple category layers and heterogeneous entity structures, requiring a unified model for diverse mapping needs. However, existing methods typically represent vector objects as polygons or graphs, making them suitable only for specific categories: polygons poorly capture topological relations, while graphs often blur instance boundaries. We observe that language, as a natural medium for human communication, offers a flexible and expressive representation that can accommodate heterogeneous map elements, including geometry, semantics, and topolog. Motivated by this insight, we propose Vector Map as Language (VecLang), a unified paradigm that reformulates multiclass vector mapping as structured text generation. VecLang encodes the common elements of different geospatial entities into a GeoJSON-like vector language, enabling cross-category modeling within a shared textual format. To generate this language reliably, we design a progressive vision-language mapping framework that first localizes vectorization units and then generates structured map elements. We further introduce Hierarchical Vector Language Optimization, which uses reinforcement learning to improve syntax validity, content fidelity, and map executability. We also build VecMap-Bench with 54K images and 800K instances, supporting training and evaluation across standard and generalization settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VecLang handles both single-class and multiclass vector mapping while achieving strong cross-dataset and open-vocabulary generalization. The model and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/yyyyll0ss/VecLang.

</details>


### [58] [Patient-Level Diagnosis of Acute Myeloid Leukemia via Deep Learning Analysis of Bone Marrow Smear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10735)
*Yuqi Ma,Tianyi Wang,Weihua Meng,Hongru Chen,Fajin Tao,Qunxian Lu,Lin An,Xiaodong Mo,Gen Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从骨髓涂片图像进行AML辅助诊断的"细胞到患者"深度学习流程，通过检测和分类16类细胞（聚焦于CBLC复合类别），将单细胞预测聚合为患者级CBLC比率，在多个中心验证中取得稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨髓涂片复查对AML评估至关重要，但人工单细胞判读耗时费力，且患者级诊断需聚合大量细胞观察结果，亟需自动化辅助诊断方法。

Method: 采用YOLO分割模块检测细胞并与专家多边形标注通过轮廓IoU匹配，生成标准化单细胞裁剪图；使用EfficientNet-B0分类器，通过两阶段GT-to-YOLO和YOLO-to-YOLO训练策略，结合类别不平衡校正、中心-边界正则化和形态学辅助监督；最终将细胞级预测聚合为患者级CBLC比率以支持AML诊断。

Result: 在258例患者（主队列169例，外部验证89例）上，流程在中心4、5、6的集成加权F1分数分别达到0.9076、0.8696和0.9124，展现出稳定的内部验证性能和良好的外部泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该cell-to-patient深度学习流程能有效从骨髓涂片中提取CBLC比率，为AML辅助诊断提供可靠支持，且在多中心外部验证中保持稳健性能。

Abstract: Bone marrow smear review remains important for acute myeloid leukemia (AML) assessment, but manual single-cell interpretation is labor-intensive and patient-level diagnosis requires aggregation of many cellular observations. We present a cell-to-patient deep learning pipeline for AML-assisted diagnosis from bone marrow smear images. The study included 258 patients from six anonymized centers, including a main cohort of 169 patients from Centers 1-3 and an external validation cohort of 89 patients from Centers 4-6. A 16-category cell annotation vocabulary was used to describe the global cellular composition, including granulocytic, monocytic, erythroid, lymphoid, eosinophilic, and other cells. Rather than identifying strict AML blasts or leukemic blasts, the model targets an expert-defined composite category termed Composite Blast-like Cells (CBLC), comprising N, N1, M, M1, R, R1, J, and J1 according to the project-wide morphological standard. A fixed YOLO-based segmentation module detected cells, predicted contours were matched to expert polygon annotations by contour IoU, and standardized single-cell crops were generated. An EfficientNet-B0 classifier was trained through a two-stage GT-to-YOLO and YOLO-to-YOLO strategy with class-imbalance correction, center-border regularization, and morphology-assisted supervision. Cell-level predictions were aggregated into patient-level CBLC ratios for AML-oriented diagnostic support. The pipeline achieved stable internal validation and maintained external generalization, with ensemble weighted F1-scores of 0.9076, 0.8696, and 0.9124 on Centers 4, 5, and 6, respectively.

</details>


### [59] [DD-INR: Dynamics-Driven Implicit Neural Representation for Accelerated Whole-Brain Functional MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10756)
*Qiaoxin Li,Caini Pan,Pierre-Antoine Comby,Chaithya Giliyar,Philippe Ciuciu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DD-INR是一种针对加速fMRI重建的动力学驱动隐式神经表示框架，通过将数据分离为静态背景和动态成分并用专用INR仅建模动态变化，在高度k空间欠采样条件下实现了优于传统方法的图像质量和脑激活模式检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速fMRI采集能够增强神经血管（BOLD）活动的检测能力，但高k空间欠采样导致图像重建困难。传统解剖MRI重建方法偏向空间精度而牺牲时间保真度，无法恢复幅度较小的任务诱发BOLD信号。

Method: 提出DD-INR（Dynamics-Driven Implicit Neural Representation）框架，将fMRI数据分解为静态背景和时变动态分量，仅用专用的隐式神经表示（INR）对动态部分进行建模，结合非相干时变采样和定制化的时空先验，使模型容量集中于激活相关的变化。

Result: 在仿真和在体采集实验中，DD-INR在图像质量和激活模式检索两方面均优于传统重建方法。

Conclusion: DD-INR为加速fMRI重建提供了一个有前景的框架，有望在实际扫描时间限制内提升fMRI研究的灵敏度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accelerated acquisition of fMRI enables enhanced detection of neurovascular (BOLD) activity in the brain, but image reconstruction becomes challenging with high k-space undersampling: Task-evoked BOLD signals are small in magnitude, which traditional anatomical MRI reconstruction methods fail to recover, as they favor spatial accuracy over temporal fidelity. We present DD-INR, a Dynamics-Driven Implicit Neural Representation framework tailored for accelerated fMRI that benefits from incoherent time-varying sampling and a tailored spatiotemporal prior, outperforming traditional methods, demonstrated in simulation and in-vivo acquisition, both in terms of image quality and retrieval of activation patterns. DD-INR achieves this by splitting the fMRI data into a static background and a temporally varying dynamic component, representing only the dynamics with a dedicated INR, thereby focusing the model's capacity on activation-relevant changes while remaining compact. In general, DD-INR provides a promising framework for accelerated fMRI reconstruction, with the potential to improve the sensitivity and robustness of fMRI studies within practical scan time limits. The source code is available at https://github.com/JoosenLi/DD-INR.

</details>


### [60] [ZODS-RS -- Zero-training Oriented Detection & Segmentation for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10769)
*Zuan Gu,Tianhan Gao,Langxu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZODS-RS 是一个无需训练的闭式流程，统一了航拍图像中的水平框检测与实例分割，通过原型纯化（PP）、旋转-尺度等变匹配（R-SEM）和不确定性感知像素合并（UAM）三大模块，在FAIR1M、xView及自建UAV数据集上展现出对小目标和拥挤场景的一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感与无人机应用亟需能够跨平台、跨视角泛化的模型，但现有免训练流程难以处理有向几何、尺度/旋转变化以及拥挤场景，且很少统一检测与分割任务。

Method: ZODS-RS 基于 DINOv3 稠密特征和 SAM 式候选框，串联三个闭式模块：(1) PP——通过 Tyler 协方差进行原型纯化；(2) R-SEM——利用可分离核与全局匈牙利匹配实现旋转-尺度等变匹配；(3) UAM——结合自适应先验和可选负原型的不确定性感知像素级合并。此外，轻量级 CWLA 模块融合多层 DINOv3 特征。

Result: FAIR1M 上 mAP@0.50:0.95=13.06，AP_S=2.93（舰船/飞机类平均）；xView 上 mAP=16.69；自建 UAV 数据集上掩码 mIoU=31.10，小目标 AP 比 Grounded-SAM 提升 +30.70（单张 5090）。

Conclusion: ZODS-RS 为航拍图像提供了一种统一的免训练水平框检测与实例分割方案，其 PP/R-SEM/UAM 模块具有明确的闭式公式，与 DINOv3 紧密耦合，在跨域迁移、小目标和拥挤目标上表现出稳定增益，同时保持部署简便。

Abstract: Remote-sensing and UAV applications need models that generalize across platforms and viewpoints without task-specific training. Yet training-free pipelines often falter on oriented geometry, scale/rotation variation, and crowded ports or airfields, and rarely unify detection and segmentation. We introduce ZODS-RS, a training-free, closed-form pipeline that outputs horizontal boxes (HBB) and instance masks. Built on DINOv3 dense features and SAM-style proposals, ZODS-RS chains: PP (prototype purification via Tyler covariance), R-SEM (rotation-scale equivariant matching with separable kernels and global Hungarian assignment), and UAM (uncertainty-aware pixelwise merging with adaptive priors and optional negative prototypes). A lightweight CWLA fuses multiple DINOv3 layers. On FAIR1M (HBB) we obtain $\mathrm{mAP}_{0.50:0.95}=\mathbf{13.06}$ and $\mathrm{AP}_S=\mathbf{2.93}$ \emph{(class-averaged over ship/airplane)}; on xView (HBB) we report $\mathrm{mAP}=\mathbf{16.69}$. On our UAV dataset, ZODS-RS achieves mask $\mathrm{mIoU}=\mathbf{31.10}$ and improves small-object AP by $\mathbf{+30.70}$ over Grounded-SAM on a single 5090. This work offers a unified, \emph{no-training} solution for horizontal-box detection plus instance segmentation in aerial imagery; provides explicit closed-form formulations for PP/R-SEM/UAM tightly coupled with DINOv3; and demonstrates \emph{consistent} gains on small and crowded targets and under cross-domain shifts while keeping deployment simple.

</details>


### [61] [Spatially Selective Self-Training for Unsupervised Building Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10775)
*Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Zhi Lu,Anas M. I. Mohammed,Xiang Zhou,Ratiba A. H. Abubaker,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SST-CD 是一种空间选择性自训练框架，将无标签建筑物变化检测重新表述为噪声伪监督下的端到端检测器学习，通过局部一致性准则筛选可靠像素进行训练，在三个数据集上显著超越现有无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标签方法遵循"差异到掩码"范式，直接利用时序差异、冻结模型响应或后处理结果作为变化图，但未学习任务特定的建筑物变化检测器，且通用的时序差异与建筑物结构变化之间存在鸿沟——外观偏移、配准误差、非建筑物修改等会产生强但误导性的响应。

Method: 提出 SST-CD 框架：(1) 将时序差异作为候选伪标签，利用局部一致性准则估计像素可靠性，仅在空间可靠像素上训练检测器；(2) 引入轻量级特征适配器重新校准双时序特征；(3) 采用基于原型的解码器产生紧凑的变化与未变化表征。

Result: 在 LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD 和 DSIFN-CD 数据集上分别取得 83.08%、91.69% 和 86.60% 的 F1 分数，超越现有无监督和无标签基线方法。

Conclusion: SST-CD 通过空间选择性自训练成功将无标签建筑物变化检测转化为端到端检测器学习，有效缩小了通用时序差异与任务特定变化之间的差距，展现出优异的无监督变化检测性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised building change detection aims to learn building-change masks from unlabeled bi-temporal remote sensing images. Existing label-free methods often follow a discrepancy-to-mask paradigm, directly using temporal differences, frozen foundation-model responses, prompt-based outputs, or post-processing results as final change maps. Although these strategies provide annotation-free cues, they do not learn a task-specific building-change detector and remain vulnerable to the gap between generic temporal discrepancies and building-defined structural changes. In practice, such discrepancies are often noisy and task-irrelevant, as appearance shifts, registration errors, and non-building modifications can produce strong but misleading responses. To address this problem, we propose SST-CD, a spatially selective self-training framework that reformulates fully label-free building change detection as end-to-end detector learning under noisy pseudo supervision.
  SST-CD uses temporal discrepancies as candidate pseudo labels and trains the detector only on spatially reliable pixels, whose reliability is estimated by a local consistency criterion that filters inconsistent regions from supervision. To further stabilize noisy self-training, a lightweight feature adapter recalibrates bi-temporal features, while a prototype-based decoder produces compact change and no-change representations. Experiments on LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and DSIFN-CD show that SST-CD achieves F1 scores of 83.08\%, 91.69\%, and 86.60\%, respectively, outperforming existing unsupervised and label-free baselines. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [62] [From Patches to Patients: A study of the tile-to-slide performance transferability in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10778)
*Sofiène Boutaj,Leo Fillioux,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis,Pierre Marza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在计算病理学中，能否用高效的tile-level线性探测来代理slide-level性能，从而减少对每候选模型都跑完整WSI流水线的需求。通过在19个基础模型、42个slide-level和16个tile-level任务上基准测试，发现tile与slide性能高度相关，tile基准可有效初筛候选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前为特定临床队列选择最优基础模型需对每个模型进行多步预处理、昂贵特征提取及MIL聚合器训练，计算开销巨大。作者希望验证tile-level线性探测能否作为slide-level性能的可靠代理，以降低模型筛选成本。

Method: 在19个SOTA基础模型上，对42个slide-level任务和16个tile-level任务进行基准测试；slide-level使用ABMIL和Mean Pooling两种聚合方式；tile-level采用线性探测；通过相关性分析、敏感性分析（考察队列规模、每张slide的tile数、任务难度的影响）以及最佳模型一致性评估来验证代理可靠性。

Result: （1）tile与slide性能在不同任务难度下呈高相关性，表明编码器表示质量是WSI性能的主要决定因素；（2）迁移能力跨模型稳定，受队列规模和每slide的tile数量影响大于平均任务难度；（3）tile基准能可靠地筛选出强候选模型，与slide-level最佳模型具有较高一致性。

Conclusion: Tile-level基准测试可作为高效、实用的初筛步骤来缩小候选模型范围，但slide-level评估对临床任务的最终验证仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Foundation Models (FMs) have recently redefined the state-of-the-art in histopathology by providing robust representations for whole-slide image (WSI) analysis. However, selecting the optimal foundation model (FM) for a specific clinical cohort currently requires multiple preprocessing steps, followed by computationally expensive feature extraction and the training of a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) aggregator for every model. In this work, we investigate whether efficient tile-level linear probing can serve as a reliable proxy for slide-level performance, reducing the need to run full slide-level pipelines for every candidate encoder. We benchmark 19 state-of-the-art FMs on 42 slide-level and 16 tile-level tasks, comparing tile probing metrics against slide-level outcomes using ABMIL and Mean Pooling aggregations. We observe a high correlation between tile and slide performance across varying task difficulties, indicating that encoder representation quality is the primary determinant of WSI success. Sensitivity analyses show that transferability is stable across models and is more influenced by cohort sizes and numbers of tiles per slide than by average task difficulty. We also measure the agreement in best performing models between tile and slide-level tasks, showing tile benchmarks reliably shortlist strong candidates. Overall, our study indicates that tile-level benchmarking provides an efficient and practical first step for narrowing down candidate models, while slide-level evaluation remains essential for final validation on clinical tasks.

</details>


### [63] [A Multimodal RGB and Events Dataset for Hand Detection in First-Person View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10790)
*Bharghav Kota,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对事件相机在目标检测中训练数据不足的瓶颈，本文提出了一种合成事件数据的方法，基于RGB Egohands数据集和v2e工具箱生成了第一人称视角的合成手部事件数据集，并利用多模态（事件+RGB）设置实现了与最先进方法相当的手部检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RGB相机在移动机器人系统中受帧率限制，且在暗光条件下易产生运动模糊，导致手部检测率下降。事件相机虽具有高动态范围、高时间分辨率和低功耗优势，但其在目标检测任务中的主要瓶颈是训练数据量不足。本文旨在解决事件相机手部检测数据匮乏的问题。

Method: 1. 利用v2e工具箱将现有的RGB Egohands数据集合成为事件数据，通过改变v2e参数生成不同光照条件和尺度下的数据集版本；2. 使用微调后的YOLOv8模型对RGB图像生成真实检测标签，并将其插值到高时间分辨率的事件上；3. 利用该多模态数据集，在事件相机与RGB相机联合设置下，采用现有多模态目标检测算法进行手部检测。

Result: 构建了一个示例性的第一人称视角合成事件手部数据集，包含不同光照和尺度版本。在此多模态数据集上的手部检测实验表明，性能可与当前最先进方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 通过合成方法可以有效缓解事件相机训练数据不足的问题，所构建的事件-RGB多模态手部数据集能够支撑高精度手部检测，验证了事件相机在机器人手部检测应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Existing hand detection algorithms work on images and the detection rate is restricted by the frame rate of the camera. In hand detection applications for moving robotic systems, conventional cameras cause motion blur, especially in darker lighting conditions. We can leverage the use of event-based cameras which possess a high dynamic range, high temporal resolution, and low power consumption. Recent work has shown that using a stereo setup of an event-based and a frame-based camera improves detection accuracy and the bandwidth-latency tradeoff. The main bottleneck in using event-based cameras in object detection and recognition tasks is a relatively low amount of training data. In this work, we propose a methodology and an exemplary synthetic event-based hand dataset from an egocentric, first-person view perspective. The data is synthesized from the existing RGB Egohands dataset with the v2e toolbox. Parameters of the v2e toolbox are varied to provide versions of the dataset with different lighting conditions and scales. Ground truth detections are generated with a fine-tuned YOLOv8 model which is applied to the RGB images in the Egohands dataset and interpolated on the high-temporal resolution events. We use the multi-modal dataset to perform hand detection with existing object detection algorithms which use a multi-modal setup of event and RGB cameras and demonstrate performance comparable to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [64] [SCAIL-2: Unifying Controlled Character Animation with End-to-end In-Context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10804)
*Wenhao Yan,Fengjia Guo,Zhuoyi Yang,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAIL-2 提出端到端角色动画框架，无需骨骼或背景等中间表示，通过直接拼接驱动视频、统一子任务条件、合成 MotionPair-60K 数据集，并结合 in-context mask conditioning、mode-specific RoPE 及 Bias-Aware DPO，在多种角色动画任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控角色动画方法依赖位姿骨骼或遮罩背景等中间表示，不可避免导致信息丢失。同时缺乏端到端的训练数据也制约了直接从原始视频学习运动迁移的能力。

Method: SCAIL-2 采用端到端框架：(1) 直接将驱动视频与参考角色序列拼接，避免中间表示；(2) 将角色动画各子任务用解耦条件统一，合成 MotionPair-60K 异构运动迁移数据集；(3) 使用 in-context mask conditioning 和 mode-specific RoPE 作为软引导；(4) 提出 Bias-Aware DPO 构建偏好对以缓解合成数据在细节区域的分布偏差。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCAIL-2 在多种角色动画任务上均大幅超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 端到端范式可有效避免中间表示的信息损失，统一子任务条件与 Bias-Aware DPO 进一步弥合合成数据与真实数据间的差距。项目将开源大部分合成数据及模型权重。

Abstract: Controlled character animation requires transferring motion from a driving sequence to a reference character. Prior works heavily rely on intermediate representations, including pose skeletons to represent motion or masked background to represent environment, which inevitably leads to information loss. To address this, we present SCAIL-2, an framework that bypasses those intermediates and achieves \textbf{end-to-end} character animation. By directly concatenating driving videos to the sequence, the model can obtain all the required visual information from the input video. To address lack of end-to-end data, we unify sub-tasks of character animation with decoupled conditions and then curate a pipeline to synthesize MotionPair-60K, an end-to-end motion transfer dataset containing heterogeneous tasks of character animation. To archive the unification, we utilize in-context mask conditioning and mode-specific RoPE as soft guidance beyond textual instructions and raw visual information. To address synthetic discrepancy in detailed regions, we propose Bias-Aware DPO to construct preference items to mitigate the errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in various character animation tasks. A large subset of synthetic data as well as model weights will be released at our project page: https://teal024.github.io/SCAIL-2/.

</details>


### [65] [Deep learning for echo sounder data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10811)
*Ketil Malde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 虽然机器学习彻底改变了图像和文本数据处理，但在水下声学数据分析中应用深度学习的成果有限。本文认为，声学数据的内在特性要求开发超越简单复用图像处理模型的新型深度学习方法，而当前领域发展的主要障碍是缺乏标准数据格式、组织方式以及高质量基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年机器学习在图像和文本领域取得巨大成功，但深度学习应用于水下声学数据（如回声图）的效果远不如预期。作者旨在揭示这一差距的深层原因，并指出推动该领域突破的关键瓶颈。

Method: 本文是一篇观点性/论述性论文，通过分析声学数据的内在特性，论证为何直接复用图像处理领域的深度学习模型不足以应对水下声学任务，并识别当前领域发展的结构性障碍。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 声学数据具有不同于图像的内在特性，简单迁移图像处理模型效果有限；(2) 领域面临两大核心障碍——缺乏标准化的数据格式与组织方式，以及缺乏高质量、附带明确性能目标的公开基准数据集。

Conclusion: 要在水下声学深度学习领域取得实质性突破，必须解决数据和标准化方面的短板——建立统一的数据格式标准、完善数据组织，并构建高质量、公开可用的基准数据集。同时需要针对声学数据特性研发专用深度学习方法，而非继续依赖图像处理模型的简单复用。

Abstract: There is no doubt that over the last decade, techniques from the field of machine learning have revolutionized how we process and interpret data, especially images and text. For underwater observations acoustics is a primary source of information, and naturally, deep learning methods have been applied to echograms and other acoustics data, but so far with rather modest results. Here, we argue that due to intrinsic properties of acoustic data, substantial advances will likely require research into deep learning methods beyond mere recycling of models and techniques from image processing. Currently, the potential for breakthroughs in method development is hindered by the lack of standard data formats and organization, and even more by the lack of readily available, high quality data sets with established performance goals. To advance the field, these shortcomings should be remedied

</details>


### [66] [Earth-OneVision: Extending Remote Sensing Multimodal Large Language Models to More Sensor Modalities and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10819)
*Miaoxin Cai,Guanqun Wang,Wei Zhang,Guangyao Zhou,Yin Zhuang,Tong Zhang,Hao Wang,He Chen,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Earth-OneVision 是一个统一的 2B 参数遥感多模态大语言模型，首次将六种传感器模态（光学、SAR、红外、多光谱、时序、视频）和跨传感器融合整合到单一自回归框架中，在多个基准上达到或超越 4B-72B 模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RS-MLLM 仅支持有限的传感器类型和任务，形成碎片化的地球观测视角，跨模态地学知识未被充分利用。因此需要构建一个统一多传感器模态、多任务类别的遥感多模态大模型，以消除模态壁垒并实现跨模态推理。

Method: 提出三大机制：(1) Full-Granularity Vision-Language Alignment (FGVLA)：将多层次视觉特征与多维语言空间对齐；(2) Spatial-Linguistic Isomorphic Serialization (SLIS)：将异构空间输出统一为自回归token；(3) Progressive Cross-Modality Adaptation (PCMA)：将复合领域差距分解为视点差距和成像物理差距两个阶段逐步适应。同时构建 MMRS-OneVision 数据集，包含约3400万 QA 对，覆盖六种传感器模态和九类任务。

Result: 仅 2B 参数即取得竞争性或最先进结果：OPT-RSVG 光学视觉定位 P@0.5 达 87.52%；SARLANG-Bench SAR VQA 达 80.68%，超越 7B 模型 7% 以上；BigEarthNet-MS 多光谱分类召回率 75.74%；EarthMind-Bench 跨模态推理 MCQ 精度 81.94%。

Conclusion: Earth-OneVision 成功将六种遥感传感器模态统一于单一自回归框架之下，通过三阶段对齐、空间序列化和渐进式跨模态适应机制，以仅 2B 参数规模实现了跨多种基准的领先性能，证明了小模型在统一多模态遥感理解中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: RS-MLLMs enable natural-language understanding and spatial reasoning over earth observation imagery. However, existing models support only a narrow range of sensor types and tasks, yielding a fragmented view of the earth and leaving cross-modal geoscientific knowledge largely unexploited. This work presents Earth-OneVision, a 2B RS-MLLM that unifies six sensor modalities (i.e., optical, SAR, infrared, multispectral, temporal, and video) and cross-sensor fusion across 9 task categories within a single autoregressive framework. Three dedicated mechanisms address three bottlenecks. Full-Granularity Vision-Language Alignment (FGVLA) aligns multi-level visual features with the multi-dimensional language space. Spatial-Linguistic Isomorphic Serialization (SLIS) unifies heterogeneous spatial outputs as autoregressive tokens. Progressive Cross-Modality Adaptation (PCMA) decomposes the compound domain gap into sequential stages, tackling the viewpoint and imaging physics gaps in turn. To support joint training, MMRS-OneVision is constructed with ~34M QA pairs spanning all six sensor modalities and cross-sensor fusion across 9 task categories, substantially exceeding existing RS multimodal instruction datasets. With only 2B parameters, Earth-OneVision achieves competitive or state-of-the-art results across extensive benchmarks, consistently matching or outperforming 4B-72B RS-MLLMs. It achieves 87.52% P@0.5 on the OPT-RSVG testset for optical visual grounding and 80.68% on the SAR VQA benchmark SARLANG-Bench, exceeding 7B models by over 7%. It further achieves 75.74% recall on the BigEarthNet-MS testset for multispectral classification, and 81.94% MCQ accuracy on EarthMind-Bench for cross-modality reasoning.

</details>


### [67] [HarmoView: Harmonizing Multi-View Constraints for Identity-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10839)
*Cong Wang,Zhentao Yu,Hongmei Wang,Weicong Liang,Zixiang Zhou,Zilin Yang,Jiarong Ou,Rui Chen,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HarmoView 是一个面向身份一致性视频生成的框架，通过多层次特征注入、可学习代理 token、Jump-RoPE 身份特征隔离以及渐进式视角课程训练策略，有效整合多视角参考输入，在保持外观保真度的同时应对大视角变化，达到了该领域的 SOTA 水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前身份一致性视频生成方法在大视角变化下难以保持外观保真度。多视角参考输入虽能提供自然解决方案，但因缺乏有效的多视角输入框架和多视角数据稀缺，进展受限。

Method: （1）Multi-level Feature Injection (MFI)：将前向参考的原始 ViT 特征与文本 token 通过交叉注意力注入，提供底层外观锚点，与 DiT 块中的高层身份特征互补；（2）Learnable Proxy Tokens：统一单视角/多视角设置下的异构参考布局，解决参考-视角不匹配问题；（3）Jump-RoPE：实现按身份的特征隔离，减少身份串扰；（4）Progressive View Curriculum：四阶段训练策略，利用视角 dropout 从普通 T2V 生成平滑过渡到高保真身份持续的空间推理；（5）构建大规模多视角数据集以解决数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在包含 100 个手工策划案例、覆盖 52 个独特身份的多视角基准评测中，HarmoView 显著优于开源基线方法，并可与领先的闭源引擎匹敌，在身份一致性视频生成任务中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: HarmoView 通过三项架构改进与渐进式训练课程，成功将多视角线索有效融入身份一致性视频生成，解决了大视角变化下的外观保真度难题，性能达到 SOTA。

Abstract: Current identity-consistent video generation methods struggle to preserve appearance fidelity under large viewpoint changes. While introducing multi-view reference input offers a natural solution, progress remains constrained by the lack of effective frameworks for multi-view inputs and the scarcity of multi-view data. We address these challenges by proposing HarmoView, a robust framework for identity-consistent video generation that effectively integrates multi-view cues through three architectural refinements complemented by a staged training curriculum. Specifically, we first introduce Multi-level Feature Injection to anchor identity fidelity; by injecting raw ViT features from frontal references alongside text tokens via cross-attention, MFI provides persistent low-level appearance anchors that complement the high-level identity features within DiT blocks, leading to enhanced identity preservation. Then, we employ learnable proxy tokens to unify heterogeneous reference layouts across single-/multi-view settings while simultaneously resolving the reference-view mismatch problem. Jump-RoPE is further developed for identity-wise feature isolation to reduce identity crosstalk. To activate these structural capabilities while preserving the original generative priors, we propose the Progressive View Curriculum. This four-stage training strategy employs view dropout to facilitate a stable transition from vanilla T2V generation to high-fidelity, identity-persistent spatial reasoning. Furthermore, we construct a large-scale multi-view dataset to address the issue of data scarcity. Extensive evaluation on our multi-view benchmark, comprising 100 manually-curated cases spanning 52 unique identities, demonstrates that HarmoView significantly outperforms open-source baselines and matches leading closed-source engines, achieving state-of-the-art performance in identity-consistent video generation.

</details>


### [68] [LIBERO-Occ: Evaluating and Improving Vision-Language-Action Models under Scene-Induced Occlusion via Viewpoint Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10862)
*Taishan Li,Jiwen Zhang,Siyuan Wang,Xuanjing Huang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究遮挡对VLA模型的影响，提出遮挡基准LIBERO-Occ和视角想象方法VIM，通过生成互补视角提升部分可观测场景下的操作鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估假设任务相关物体完全可见，但真实场景中遮挡导致部分可观测，模型性能大幅下降，缺乏专门针对遮挡的基准和方法。

Method: 1) 构建LIBERO-Occ基准，作为LIBERO的遮挡扩展；2) 提出Viewpoint Imagination (VIM)，从被遮挡的主视角生成互补视角，并结合观测与想象的双重视觉证据进行动作预测，无需额外部署摄像头。

Result: SOTA VLA模型在遮挡下性能显著退化；VIM在不同任务套件、遮挡类型和严重程度下均提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 视角想象是部分可观测操作中感知补全的有效机制，为VLA模型应对现实遮挡挑战提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong performance on standard manipulation benchmarks, but most evaluations assume that task-relevant objects are fully visible. This assumption often fails in realistic settings, where occlusion makes manipulation partially observable. In this paper, we study \textit{scene-induced occlusion} as a fundamental challenge for VLA models and introduce \textbf{LIBERO-Occ}, an occlusion-oriented extension of LIBERO. Experiments show that state-of-the-art VLAs suffer substantial performance degradation under occlusion. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{Viewpoint Imagination (VIM)}, which generates a complementary view from an occluded primary observation and conditions action prediction on both observed and imagined evidence. VIM improves robustness across task suites, occlusion types, and severity levels without requiring additional cameras at deployment time, suggesting that viewpoint imagination is an promising mechanism for perception completion in partially observable manipulation. Our benchmark and corresponding code are available at: \href{https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ}{https://github.com/litsh/Libero-Occ}.

</details>


### [69] [Schmidt Decomposition-Based Methods for Efficient Quantum Image Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10874)
*Ana-Maria Pangeva,Yassine Ferhi,Alexander Geng,Andreas Weinmann,Desislava Ivanova,Ali Moghiseh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用施密特分解进行低秩状态近似，以降低量子图像编码（FRQI、QPIE、NEQR）在真实量子硬件上的电路复杂度。结果表明，低秩近似可在保持图像质量的同时大幅减少资源消耗，其中FRQI模型电路深度减少97%，MSE仅约0.27。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子图像编码方法（FRQI、QPIE、NEQR）在NISQ设备上执行时，往往产生大量量子门、极深的电路和高昂的量子比特开销，严重制约了实际部署。因此，迫切需要一种能在保持图像信息的前提下，降低量子电路复杂度的技术。

Method: 作者采用施密特分解（Schmidt decomposition）对三种量子图像编码（FRQI、QPIE、NEQR）生成的量子态进行低秩近似，仅保留量子态纠缠结构中最重要的部分，从而简化态制备过程。随后对比原始编码与低秩近似编码在电路深度、CNOT门数量、均方误差（MSE）及重建图像视觉质量上的表现。

Result: 低秩近似在精度与资源效率之间实现了有意义的权衡。其中，FRQI模型表现最优：电路深度减少97%，同时保持近乎完美的图像重建质量（MSE约为0.27）。

Conclusion: 低秩状态近似技术（基于施密特分解）能够显著降低量子图像编码的电路复杂度，有望推动在近期NISQ硬件上的实用化量子图像处理。

Abstract: In quantum image processing, a fundamental step is encoding classical image data into quantum states. This can be achieved using methods such as Flexible Representation of Quantum Images (FRQI), Quantum Probability Image Encoding (QPIE), and Novel Enhanced Quantum Representation (NEQR). However, on real quantum hardware, these encodings can quickly lead to circuits with many gates, large circuit depth, and high qubit usage, which is a problem for Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. In this work, we investigate whether low-rank state approximation, formulated via Schmidt decomposition, can help reduce this complexity. The method keeps only the most significant parts of a quantum state's entanglement structure, making state preparation more efficient while preserving most of the image information. We compare the three encoding techniques in their original form and with low-rank approximation, evaluating metrics such as circuit depth, CNOT count, MSE, and visual quality of reconstructed images.
