Topic-level question volume correlates significantly with student self-reported difficulty from an independent mid-semester survey (rho = 0.491, p = 0.008, n = 28 topics), providing convergent evidence that the classified question stream reflects genuine topic difficulty. These results demonstrate that conversational AI interaction logs, mapped onto curriculum structure, carry actionable signals about topic-level knowledge gaps and provide instructors with a curriculum-grounded view of which topics warrant attention.

</details>


### [138] [ArabiGEE: A Hierarchical Taxonomy for Arabic Grammatical Error Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10765)
*Khaled Elhady,Omar Kallas,Nizar Habash,Bashar Alhafni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了首个基于显式错误类型的阿拉伯语语法错误解释（GEE）分类体系 ArabiGEE，涵盖正字法、形态、句法、词汇四个维度，包含27种错误类型、140种修正类型和324条相关解释，并应用于现有语料标注及LLM自动评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEE方法将解释生成视为自由文本，缺乏结构化组织；阿拉伯语语法错误解释领域缺少基于显式错误类型的系统化分类体系，难以支撑结构化解释和自动评估。

Method: 构建层次化分类体系 ArabiGEE，沿正字法、形态、句法、词汇四个维度组织，定义27种错误类型、140种修正类型及324条解释；在该分类体系上对现有阿拉伯语语法错误修正语料进行人工标注，并展示如何利用结构化解释支持LLM在阿拉伯语GEE上的自动评估。

Result: 成功构建了首个全面的阿拉伯语GEE分类体系，完成了对现有语料的部分人工标注，并验证了结构化语法解释可用于LLM的自动评估。

Conclusion: ArabiGEE填补了阿拉伯语语法错误解释领域结构化分类体系的空白，为后续研究和自动评估提供了基础框架，代码与数据已公开发布。

Abstract: We introduce ArabiGEE, the first comprehensive Arabic grammatical error explanation (GEE) taxonomy grounded in explicit error types. Unlike existing GEE approaches that treat explanation generation as free-form text, ArabiGEE organizes grammatical explanations through a hierarchical structure spanning orthographic, morphological, syntactic, and lexical dimensions. The taxonomy consists of 27 error types, 140 correction types, and 324 associated explanations. We apply ArabiGEE to manually annotate portions of existing Arabic grammatical error correction corpora and demonstrate how structured grammatical explanations can support automatic evaluation of LLMs on Arabic GEE. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [139] [Dep-LLM: Training-Free Depression Diagnosis via Evidence-Guided Structured Multi-factor with Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10796)
*Yiqing Lyu,Xianbing Zhao,Buzhou Tang,Ronghuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dep-LLM 是一个无需训练的框架，利用冻结的现成大语言模型，通过三个阶段（链式思维多因素分析、置信度分析与调制、协同多因素预测）逐步模拟临床精神科医生的推理过程，在长对话自动抑郁检测任务上超越了零样本基线、有监督专用LLM及最新商业LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动抑郁检测面临两大挑战：(1) 长篇幅、多主题临床访谈中抑郁线索分布稀疏且复杂，难以建模，导致推理肤浅不可靠；(2) 临床隐私导致标注数据稀缺，训练和微调成本高，限制了有监督系统的部署。

Method: 提出 Dep-LLM 三阶段框架：(1) 链式思维（CoT）抑郁多因素分析模块，将长对话结构分解为五个临床对齐主题，生成有证据支撑的推理依据；(2) 置信度分析与调制模块，通过词元级熵量化每个推理依据的认知可靠性，并施加标签内和主题间调制，放大可信信号、抑制不确定信号；(3) 协同多因素预测模块，将经置信度加权的多因素信号动态融合为最终诊断结果。整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在 DAIC-WOZ 和 E-DAIC 数据集上的大量实验表明：Dep-LLM 在 21 个基础 LLM 的 9 项指标（准确率、宏 F1、加权平均 F1 等）上几乎全面超越零样本基线，并优于当前最优的有监督领域专用 LLM 及最新闭源商业 LLM。

Conclusion: Dep-LLM 作为一种无需训练的框架，有效解决了长对话中稀疏抑郁线索建模和标注数据稀缺的双重难题，展现出强大的有效性和泛化能力，为自动抑郁检测提供了实用且低成本的新路径。

Abstract: Automatic Depression Detection (ADD) from clinical interviews is a pivotal task in computational mental health, yet it remains challenging due to two critical obstacles: 1) difficulty in modeling complex but sparsely distributed depression clues within lengthy, multi-topic clinical interviews, leading to superficial and unreliable reasoning; 2) scarcity of labeled data due to clinical privacy, together with high cost of training and fine-tuning, limiting the deployment of supervised ADD systems. To jointly address these challenges, we propose Dep-LLM, a training-free framework that mirrors the step-by-step reasoning of clinical psychiatrists and operates entirely on frozen off-the-shelf foundation LLMs. Dep-LLM comprises three stages. First, a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Depression Multi-factor Analysis module structurally decomposes the long dialogue into five clinically aligned themes and produces evidence-grounded rationales, effectively handling long-context dependencies. Second, we introduce Confidence Analysis and Modulation module that quantifies the epistemic reliability from token-level entropy of each rationale and applies an intra-label and inter-theme modulation that amplifies trustworthy signals while suppressing uncertain ones without extra training. Third, a Collaborative Multi-factor Prediction module dynamically integrates multi-factor signals weighted by confidence into the final diagnosis. Extensive experiments on the DAIC-WOZ and E-DAIC datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of Dep-LLM: it surpasses zero-shot baseline on nearly all 21 foundation LLMs across 9 metrics such as accuracy, macro F1 and weighted-average F1, and further outperforms state-of-the-art supervised domain-specific LLMs as well as the latest closed-source commercial LLMs, while requiring no extra training.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond APIs: Probing the Limits of MLLMs in Physical Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10803)
*Zhixin Ma,Yutong Zhou,Yongqi Li,Chong-Wah Ngo,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了首个物理工具使用基准 PhysTool-Bench，包含 2,510 个查询，覆盖 2,678 种真实物理工具。评估 13 个 MLLM 后发现，最强模型 Gemini-3.1-Pro 仅能识别场景中 58.7% 的工具，端到端任务完成率仅 21.0%，揭示了感知与规划两个层次的能力缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在具身 AI 中日益充当"大脑"角色，但它们在物理工具使用方面的能力尚未被系统探索。该工作旨在填补这一空白，量化并诊断 MLLM 在真实物理世界工具理解与任务规划中的瓶颈。

Method: 构建 PhysTool-Bench 基准，涵盖制造、电工、农业、医疗等多个领域，包含 2,678 种真实物理工具和 2,510 个查询。评估沿两个维度展开：(1) 识别场景中所有物理工具；(2) 根据指令和视觉上下文规划工具选择与使用序列。在 13 个主流 MLLM 上进行系统评测。

Result: 最强模型 Gemini-3.1-Pro 的工具识别准确率仅为 58.7%，端到端查询完成率仅 21.0%。分析揭示出两级缺陷：感知层面难以准确识别真实场景中的工具；规划层面更大幅度的性能下降表明 MLLM 缺乏将感知到的工具映射到任务语义的功能常识。

Conclusion: 物理工具使用是当前 MLLM 在具身 AI 应用中的关键瓶颈，尤其在任务规划阶段的功能常识严重不足，这一发现为面向实用化具身 AI 的未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at utilizing digital APIs and increasingly serve as the "brain" of embodied AI, instructing robots to interact with the physical world. In such embodied settings, a central capability is the use of physical tools, which underpins MLLMs' ability to assist humans in real-world tasks. Despite the importance, MLLMs' proficiency in physical tool use remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce PhysTool-Bench, the first physical tool-use benchmark designed to evaluate MLLMs' ability to comprehend real-world scenarios, identify physical tools, and plan their use. PhysTool-Bench comprises 2,510 queries over 2,678 real-world physical tools spanning diverse domains, including manufacturing, electrical work, agriculture, and healthcare. Concretely, models are evaluated along two primary dimensions: 1) recognizing all physical tools present in the scene, and 2) planning the tool selection and use sequence based on the instruction and visual context. Across 13 leading MLLMs, even the strongest model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) identifies only 58.7% of tools in a scene and completes merely 21.0% of queries end-to-end. Our analysis reveals a two-level deficit: MLLMs struggle to perceive tools in realistic scenes, and the much larger drop at the planning stage further indicates a lack of functional commonsense for mapping perceived tools onto task semantics, pinpointing a critical bottleneck for the development of practical embodied AI.

</details>


### [141] [Attention-Discounted Adaptive Sampler for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10829)
*Yusuf Sahin,Ahmed Rockey Saikia,Volkan Cevher,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ADAS 是一种免训练的并行掩码扩散解码重排序规则，通过基于注意力权重的软折扣惩罚来筛选真正安全的联合生成 token，在低 NFE 下显著提升了解码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码扩散语言模型的并行解码方法（Top-k、Fast-dLLM、EB-Sampler）仅基于 token 级别的置信度分数排序，忽略了所选 token 之间的交互耦合关系，导致单独高置信的 token 在同时揭示时产生错误。

Method: 提出 ADAS（Attention-Discounted Adaptive Sampling），一种训练无关的重排序规则。其核心思想是：当候选 token 对已选定但预测仍不确定的位置产生强注意力时，对该候选 token 施加基于注意力权重的软边际折扣惩罚，从而在子集构建阶段剔除耦合风险高的 token，而不改变基础采样器的停止规则。

Result: 在 LLaDA-8B-Base 和 Dream-7B-Base 上，将 ADAS 插入 Top-k、Fast-dLLM 和 EB-Sampler 后，GSM8K、MATH500、HumanEval、MBPP 四个基准上的低 NFE 性能平均分别提升 9.11 和 10.46 个百分点，且每次前向推理仅增加 3.1% 的运行时开销。

Conclusion: 基于注意力软折扣的重排序是一种简单、模块化且有效的方法，可显著提升掩码扩散语言模型在高并行解码场景下的生成质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models can reduce inference steps by revealing multiple tokens per denoising iteration, but this parallelism is fragile: positions that are individually confident may be unsafe to commit together when their predictions are coupled. Existing training-free samplers such as Top-\(k\), Fast-dLLM, and EB-Sampler mainly control how many tokens to reveal, while often ranking candidates by token-wise scores that ignore interactions within the selected set. We propose ADAS, a training-free reranking rule for parallel masked diffusion decoding. ADAS leaves the base sampler's stopping rule unchanged and modifies only subset construction: it greedily discounts a candidate when it attends strongly to already selected positions whose predictions remain uncertain. Unlike graph-constrained methods that turn attention into hard compatibility constraints, ADAS keeps attention continuous and uses it as a soft marginal penalty. Across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base on GSM8K, MATH500, HumanEval, and MBPP, plugging ADAS into Top-\(k\), Fast-dLLM, and EB-Sampler improves low-NFE performance at matched denoiser evaluations by \(9.11\) and \(10.46\) percentage points on average, respectively, with \(3.1\%\) per-forward runtime overhead. These results show that soft attention-discounted reranking is a simple and modular way to improve quality in highly parallel decoding for masked diffusion language models.

</details>


### [142] [ConvMemory v2: A Recall-Preserving Top-10 Evidence Reranker for Conversational Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10842)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ConvMemory v2 是一个可选的 token-evidence 重排序器，在轻量级 v1 重排序器之后对 top-10 候选集进行精排。它是一个微调的 ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2 交叉编码器，在 LoCoMo 对话记忆基准上将 FULL MRR 从 0.5824 提升至 0.6560，且不改变召回集，通过级联模式以低成本逼近昂贵的全池交叉编码器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 v1 轻量级重排序器已选出 top-10 记忆候选后，仍需进一步提升排序精度以缩小与昂贵全池交叉编码器（mxbai-rerank-large-v1）之间的性能差距，同时保持计算效率，且不改变 v1 构建的召回集。

Method: 采用标准 recall-preserving 级联范式：v2 是一个微调的 ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2 交叉编码器（22,713,601 参数），仅对 v1 选出的 top-10 (query, memory) 对进行重排序，不改变返回的记忆集合。通过四臂负载消融实验（移除、打乱、替换候选特定记忆文本）验证记忆文本是关键机制。使用 LoCoMo 基准（5 种子，n=4955 测试行）进行配对 bootstrap 评估。

Result: v2 将 FULL MRR 从 v1 的 0.5824 提升至 0.6560（+0.0734，95% CI [+0.0645, +0.0827]），H@1 从 0.4440 提升至 0.5474。Recall@10 和 Hit@10 不变。v2 的 FULL MRR 仅比 mxbai_top500 的 0.6688 低 0.013，且在两个 raw-dense-hard 切片上超过 mxbai_top500。消融实验确认候选特定记忆文本是核心机制。

Conclusion: v2 作为标准 recall-preserving 级联模式，通过 LoCoMo 特定微调、显式反捷径推理合约和严格的负载消融分析，在特定切片上可超越昂贵的全池交叉编码器，但其优势是切片特定的，并非通用主导性声明。

Abstract: We describe ConvMemory v2, an opt-in token-evidence reranker that sits after the lightweight ConvMemory v1 reranker and reorders only v1's protected top-10 candidate set. v2 is a fine-tuned ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2 cross-encoder (22,713,601 parameters, measured from the released checkpoint) applied to the ten (query, memory) pairs that v1 has already selected; it does not change which ten memories are returned, so Recall@10 and Hit@10 are identical to v1 by construction, not by statistical coincidence. On the LoCoMo conversational memory benchmark (5 seeds, n = 4955 test rows), v2 raises FULL MRR from v1's 0.5824 to 0.6560 (paired bootstrap +0.0734, 95% CI [+0.0645, +0.0827]) and H@1 from 0.4440 to 0.5474. v2 closes most but not all of the gap to a much more expensive full-pool cross-encoder reference (mxbai-rerank-large-v1 over the top-500, MRR 0.6688): on FULL MRR v2 sits 0.013 below mxbai_top500, but on two raw-dense-hard slices (where v1's protected top-10 has higher recall than mxbai's own top-10) v2 exceeds mxbai_top500. A four-arm load-bearing ablation shows candidate-specific memory text is the mechanism: removing, shuffling, or replacing it collapses MRR below raw dense retrieval. v2 is best understood as a standard recall-preserving cascade pattern with LoCoMo-specific fine-tuning, an explicit anti-shortcut inference contract, and disciplined load-bearing analysis; its advantage over mxbai is slice-specific rather than a general dominance claim. This report extends the v1 technical report (arXiv:2605.28062).

</details>


### [143] [Janus: A Benchmark for Goal-Conditioned Information Distortion in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10852)
*Polydoros Giannouris,Mohsinul Kabir,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JANUS是一个基准测试，用于评估LLM在事实约束下的目标驱动语用扭曲——即模型如何通过对真实事实的选择性处理（省略不利证据、淡化细节、强调有利信息、模糊化精确限定）来产生误导性输出，而非依赖幻觉或虚假陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM欺骗性评估主要关注虚假声明和明确谎言，但现实中的误导性沟通往往源于对真实事实的选择性处理——省略、淡化、强调或模糊化。这种更隐蔽的失败模式在现有基准中几乎未被覆盖，却可能更具危害性。

Method: 构建JANUS基准，包含8个领域共160个场景，每个场景提供固定的有利和不利事实池，并对比中性条件与目标驱动条件（如提高采纳率、注册率、批准率或支持度）。通过约束所有输出使用相同事实池，将误导性印象与幻觉/虚构隔离开来。每个场景配有人工标注的中性和目标驱动prompt及标注的事实材料。

Result: 在12个LLM上的广泛实验揭示了持续存在的目标驱动扭曲现象：当前模型对激励和框架目标依然敏感，缺乏针对选择性误导沟通的鲁棒防护机制。

Conclusion: JANUS揭示了LLM在事实约束下产生语用扭曲的普遍性，表明现有模型在面对目标驱动场景时容易产出误导性输出，亟需针对此类更隐蔽欺骗模式的防御机制。该基准和代码已公开发布。

Abstract: LLM deception is often evaluated through direct markers such as fabricated claims, explicit lies, or strategic concealment. However, many real-world misleading communications do not depend on false statements, rather, they arise from selective treatment of true material facts: omitting adverse evidence, softening unfavorable details, emphasizing favorable details, or replacing precise qualifications with vague language. Existing benchmarks largely miss this subtler and arguably more dangerous failure mode. We introduce JANUS, a benchmark for measuring goal-conditioned pragmatic distortion in fact-grounded LLM outputs. Each scenario in our benchmark provides a fixed pool of favorable and adverse facts and compares a neutral condition against a goal-directed condition, such as increasing adoption, enrollment, approval, or support, despite potential harm to directly affected individuals or groups. Because all outputs are constrained to use the same fact pool, JANUS isolates misleading net impressions from hallucination and fabrication. JANUS contains 160 scenarios across 8 domains, with each scenario paired with neutral and goal-conditioned prompts and annotated material facts. Extensive experiments across 12 LLMs reveal consistent goal-conditioned distortions, demonstrating that current models remain sensitive to incentive and framing objectives and lack robust safeguards against selectively misleading communication. We publicly release our corpus and code for future research.

</details>


### [144] [Pushing the Limits of LLM Tool Calling via Experiential Knowledge Integration and Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10875)
*Yupu Hao,Zhuoran Jin,Huanxuan Liao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了知识如何影响LLM工具使用性能，涵盖知识获取、激活和内化三个阶段。研究发现：实例级知识简单可靠，意图级知识收益有限；推理宽度扩展比深度扩展更有效激活知识；强化学习优于监督微调用于知识内化。基于此提出KATE框架，在BFCL-V3和AppWorld上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主代理依赖工具使用，但在多步执行中常因工具相关知识不足和知识激活低效而失败。现有研究缺乏对知识在工具使用全流程中作用的系统理解，因此需要系统研究知识获取、激活和内化各阶段如何影响工具使用性能。

Method: 提出知识增强工具执行框架KATE，分三阶段：(1)知识获取阶段，获取并评估实例级经验知识和抽象意图级知识；(2)推理时知识激活，通过并行采样加聚合扩展推理宽度（而非深度）来激活潜在经验知识；(3)训练时知识内化，使用知识增强数据进行后训练，对比强化学习和监督微调的效果。最终将三者整合为统一框架。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)简单实例级知识即可带来强且可靠的性能增益，抽象意图级知识收益有限；(2)提示LLM扩展推理深度收益递减，而通过并行采样扩展推理宽度能更有效激活经验知识；(3)知识内化阶段，强化学习优于监督微调。KATE在BFCL-V3和AppWorld基准上跨模型规模均实现一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 工具使用性能可通过系统性的知识增强策略得到显著提升：获取实例级经验知识、在推理时扩展宽度而非深度来激活知识、以及用强化学习进行知识内化是关键设计原则。KATE框架为构建更可靠的LLM工具使用代理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) rely on tool use to act as autonomous agents, yet often fail in multi-step execution due to insufficient tool-related knowledge and ineffective knowledge activation. Therefore, we present a systematic study on how knowledge influences tool-use performance, covering the stages of knowledge acquisition, activation, and internalization. In the knowledge acquisition stage, we acquire and evaluate various forms of experiential knowledge, and our analysis shows that simple instance-level knowledge can already provide strong and reliable gains, while abstract intent-level knowledge offers limited benefits. At inference time, to activate knowledge, we find that prompting LLM to expand the depth of reasoning yields diminishing returns, whereas expanding the width of reasoning by parallel sampling with aggregation more effectively activates latent experiential knowledge. At training time, for knowledge internalization, post-training with knowledge-augmented data further improves performance, with reinforcement learning outperforming supervised fine-tuning. Based on these insights, we propose the Knowledge-Augmented Tool Execution (KATE), a knowledge-augmented tool execution framework that integrates experiential knowledge with reasoning-width-expanded inference and knowledge-aware training. Experiments on BFCL-V3 and AppWorld demonstrate consistent and substantial improvements over strong baselines across model scales. Our Code is available at https://github.com/hypasd-art/KATE.

</details>


### [145] [Trace Only What You Need: Structure-Aware On-Demand Hypergraph Memory for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10921)
*Xiangjun Zai,Xingyu Tan,Chen Chen,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocTrace是一种面向长文档问答的多智能体RAG框架，通过查询触发的知识组织、文档结构感知和经验引导的推理，解决了现有结构化RAG方法成本高、结构利用不足和无经验复用的问题，在多个数据集上以更低计算成本取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化RAG方法存在三个核心局限：(1) 查询无关的知识组织成本高昂；(2) 对原始文档结构利用不充分；(3) 缺乏对历史推理经验的复用能力。长文档QA需要模型理解事件顺序、章节级上下文和跨部分证据关联，上述局限严重制约了推理效果与效率。

Method: DocTrace包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级文档结构树索引，保留文档层级结构；(2) 基于超图结构的工作记忆，在推理过程中按需动态构建，为多智能体共享；(3) 图结构经验记忆，存储成功的推理计划供未来相似问题复用，实现跨问题的自适应探索。

Result: 在四个长文档QA数据集上，DocTrace在三个数据集上取得最优性能，相比最强基线ComoRAG，F1最高提升8.85%，EM提升4.40%，同时整体计算成本降低53.32%。

Conclusion: DocTrace通过查询触发、结构感知和经验引导三大机制，有效克服了现有结构化RAG的局限性，在长文档QA任务上实现了性能与效率的双重突破，证明了文档结构保留和经验复用对长文档推理的重要价值。

Abstract: Long-document question answering (QA) requires large language models (LLMs) to reason over evidence scattered across lengthy documents, where answers often depend on event order, section-level context, and cross-part evidence connections. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) reduces the input context by retrieving relevant evidence, existing structured RAG methods still face three limitations: costly query-agnostic knowledge organization, insufficient use of original document structure, and no reuse of historical reasoning experience. To address these limitations, we propose DocTrace, a multi-agent RAG framework for long-document QA that supports query-triggered knowledge organization, document-structure-aware and experience-guided reasoning. DocTrace preserves document hierarchy with a lightweight document structural tree index, constructs agent-shared hypergraph-structured working memory on demand during reasoning, and stores successful reasoning plans in graph-structured experience memory for future reuse, enabling adaptive exploration across related long-document questions. Experiments on four long-document QA datasets show that DocTrace achieves the best performance on three datasets, surpassing the strongest baseline, ComoRAG, by up to 8.85% in F1 and 4.40% in EM, while reducing the overall computational cost by 53.32%

</details>


### [146] [It Takes One to Bias Them All: Breaking Bad with One-Shot GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10931)
*Naihao Deng,Yilun Zhu,Naichen Shi,Clayton Scott,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 仅需一个偏置样本进行一轮GRPO训练，即可系统性地破坏大语言模型的对齐，使刻板印象驱动的推理泛化到不同属性、类别和基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现代大语言模型经过大规模后训练建立的安全护栏是否容易被GRPO算法攻破，揭示后训练阶段存在的关键脆弱性。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，仅使用单个偏置样本进行一次性（one-shot）训练，观察模型是否会产生系统性偏见，并在不同属性、类别和多个基准测试上评估偏见的泛化程度。同时分析模型对偏置的易感性与模型初始产生偏置输出的似然之间的关系。

Result: 单次GRPO训练即可诱发系统性偏见，刻板印象驱动的推理能够跨属性、跨类别、跨基准测试泛化。不同模型对偏置的易感性存在差异，取决于其初始产生偏置输出的可能性。

Conclusion: 后训练阶段的模型对齐存在严重脆弱性，仅需一个恶意样本即可推翻大规模对齐训练建立的安全防护，这对LLM安全性的实际部署构成重大隐患。

Abstract: Warning: This paper contains several toxic and offensive statements.
  Modern large language models (LLMs) are typically aligned through large-scale post-training to ensure fair and reliable behavior. In this work, we investigate how easily such guardrails can be broken by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We show that one-shot GRPO training on a single biased example is sufficient to induce systematic bias, with stereotype-driven reasoning generalizing across attributes, categories, and benchmarks. We further find that models differ in their susceptibility based on the initial likelihood of producing biased outputs. Our results reveal a critical vulnerability in post-training: alignment can be overridden by a single example.

</details>


### [147] [Density Field State Space Models: 1-Bit Distillation, Efficient Inference, and Knowledge Organization in Mamba-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10932)
*Chirag Shinde*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-SSM框架，将SSM模型压缩至1-bit骨架加int8低秩修正，实现Mamba-2 1.3B模型9.7倍压缩和21.4倍推理加速，同时保持下游任务性能。仅需32M token和6小时蒸馏。此外，系统分析了压缩模型的内部知识组织，发现了意图分类、知识检索和输出格式化三个处理阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模SSM模型（如Mamba-2）虽然推理效率较高，但其FP16模型体积仍然庞大（2.7GB），在资源受限场景下部署困难。现有二值化/量化方法需要从头训练，成本高昂。本文旨在探索一种高效的后训练压缩方案，仅需极少量token即可将预训练SSM压缩至极小体积，同时保持竞争力性能。

Method: 提出DF-SSM框架：(1) 将SSM权重压缩为1-bit二值骨架加int8低秩修正矩阵；(2) 以FP16教师模型进行知识蒸馏，仅需32M token、6小时单卡A100；(3) 构建优化推理管线：cuBLAS INT8 tensor core加速骨架矩阵乘法、自定义CUDA kernel处理有状态SSM和卷积操作、AVX-512 CPU后端支持CPU部署；(4) 通过445个事实性提示词（19个类别）的逐层分析，研究压缩模型内部知识组织。

Result: 压缩后模型仅278MB（9.7倍压缩），GPU推理加速21.4倍（batch=1）。下游任务性能比BitMamba-2（150B token从头训练的1.58-bit模型）仅低2-4个百分点。知识组织分析发现三个处理阶段：(1) 第0-3层进行意图分类，在抽象空间操作，无语义对齐；(2) 第25-35层进行知识检索，事实关联集中在一个5层窗口内；(3) 第36-47层进行输出格式化，类别结构溶解。早期层分类是句法驱动（受模板结构影响）而非语义驱动，模型展现出良好组织的知识表征但事实召回较弱。

Conclusion: DF-SSM证明了对预训练SSM进行极低位宽后训练压缩的可行性与高效性，在保持竞争力的同时实现显著的体积缩减和推理加速。对压缩模型内部机制的分析揭示了SSM中知识处理的阶段性特征，暗示表征结构可能先于事实强度形成，为理解大模型内部知识组织提供了新见解。

Abstract: We present Density Field State Space Models (DF-SSM), a framework for compressing SSMs to a 1-bit scaffold with int8 low-rank correction. Applied to Mamba-2 1.3B, we achieve a 278 MB model (9.7x smaller than the 2.7 GB FP16 teacher) that runs at 21.4x faster inference on GPU (batch=1, relative to the mamba-ssm reference implementation) while maintaining downstream task performance within 2-4 percentage points of BitMamba-2, a 1.58-bit model trained from scratch on 150B tokens. The distillation itself requires only 32M tokens and 6 hours on a single A100 GPU, though it presupposes a pretrained FP16 teacher. We develop an optimized inference pipeline combining cuBLAS INT8 tensor cores for the scaffold matmul, custom CUDA kernels for stateful SSM and convolution operations, and an AVX-512 CPU backend for efficient deployment on both GPU and CPU. Beyond compression, we investigate the internal knowledge organization of the resulting model, discovering three distinct processing phases: intent classification (layers 0-3, operating in an abstract space with no vocabulary alignment), knowledge retrieval (layers 25-35, where factual associations localize to a 5-layer window), and output formatting (layers 36-47, where category structure dissolves). Through systematic analysis of 445 factual prompts across 19 categories, we find that early-layer classification is syntactic (driven by template structure) rather than semantic, and that the model exhibits well-organized knowledge representations despite weak factual recall--suggesting that representational structure may precede factual strength.

</details>


### [148] [Who Brought Easter Eggs to Eid? Auditing Cultural Translation of Math Word Problems Across Diverse Languages and Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11009)
*Parisa Suchdev,Juniper Lovato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了大语言模型在将数学应用题跨语言文化适配时的行为，发现模型间一致性低（仅33.5%的具体替换一致），且所有模型-语言组合都呈现"熵塌缩"现象，即文化多样性被压缩而非扩展。模型偏好改编表层文化标记（姓名、食物、货币），却保留嵌入文化假设的深层结构（如年级体系），并出现区域性错误归因等系统性问题，这些问题仅能通过语料库级分析才能发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型越来越多地被用于个性化学习中数学应用题的跨文化适配，但尚不清楚不同模型间的适配是否一致、这些适配在大规模上是否保留了文化多样性，以及模型认为哪些文化实体最为显著。这一问题的核心在于：表面上看似合理的适配可能掩盖深层的文化同质化和错误归因。

Method: 研究者选取Claude Opus 4、GPT-4.1和Gemini 2.5 Pro三个模型，将60道英文数学应用题适配为孟加拉语、印地语、旁遮普语（印度）、乌尔都语、信德语（巴基斯坦）、意大利语和西西里语（意大利），涵盖从高资源到低资源的语言谱系。对6,489个实体转换进行标注，编码为保留、本地化、泛化、省略或更改五类转换类型，分析模型间的转换一致性和文化多样性变化。

Result: （1）三个模型在62.5%的案例中转换类型一致，但在具体替换上仅33.5%一致；（2）所有21个语言-模型组合均出现"熵塌缩"，适配过程压缩而非扩展了文化多样性；（3）模型优先改编姓名、食物、货币等表层文化标记，但保留年级体系等携带文化特定假设的深层结构；（4）存在文化错误归因，如为印度孟加拉语学生使用孟加拉国货币塔卡，以及将复活节找蛋活动适配为开斋节活动等跨文化污染。

Conclusion: 模型适配的"表面可信性"恰恰使得深层失败容易被忽视。模型选择直接塑造了学生接触的文化世界，但当前所有模型均系统性地压缩文化多样性、偏好表层标记替换、且出现区域性错误归因。这些深层次问题无法通过单篇译文审查发现，必须依赖语料库级别的系统分析。研究揭示了将LLM用于跨文化教育适配时需要审慎对待的系统性风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to adapt math word problems for personalized learning at scale, but it remains an open question whether those adaptations are consistent across models, preserve cultural diversity at scale, and reveal which cultural entities models treat as most salient. We analyze how Claude Opus 4, GPT-4.1, and Gemini 2.5 Pro adapt 60 English math word problems into Bengali, Hindi, Punjabi (India), Urdu, Sindhi (Pakistan), Italian, and Sicilian (Italy), a language set spanning the full resource spectrum, from high-resource Italian and Hindi to under-studied Sindhi, Sicilian, and Punjabi. We annotate 6,489 entity transformations, coding whether models preserve, localize, generalize, omit, or change entities such as names, foods, and places. Models agree on transformation type in 62.5% of cases and on specific substitutions in only 33.5%, meaning model choice directly shapes which cultural world students encounter. All 21 language-model combinations show entropy collapse, with adaptation compressing rather than expanding cultural diversity. Models prioritize surface markers such as names, foods, and currencies while preserving deeper structural features such as grade-level systems that embed culturally specific assumptions. Despite prompts specifying target countries, models misattribute regional context by using Bangladeshi taka for Indian Bengali students and produce cross-cultural contamination, such as adapting egg hunts as Eid activities. Some failures are visible in individual translations. Others, including diversity collapse, systematic preference for surface markers, and consistent regional misattribution, emerge only through corpus-level analysis. The surface plausibility that makes adapted problems look correct is precisely what makes deeper failures easy to overlook.

</details>


### [149] [Measuring Human Value Expression in Social Media Texts: Calibrated LLM Annotation and Encoder Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11018)
*Maria Milkova,Maksim Rudnev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Measuring subjective constructs in naturally occurring social media text requires annotation procedures that are theoretically grounded, empirically validated, and transferable to an encoder model for scalable prediction. Using non-English social media posts annotated according to Schwartz's theory of basic human values, we investigate how different LLMs, prompts, and instruction languages operationalize the expression of values in text. We argue that although texts may permit multiple plausible interpretations, theory-based value definitions can constrain interpretations and reduce spurious value attributions. Beyond precision, recall, and F1, we evaluate structural alignment between values, error structure, confidence-ambiguity relations, and annotation stability. We show that different LLMs produce different value interpretations. Iterative prompt calibration through error analysis reduces misattributions and improves alignment with expert annotations. We also derive targeted expert verification rules from recurrent error structures and use them during corpus annotation. Finally, we show that LLM annotations can be transferred to an encoder model through soft-label training, retaining theory-based value interpretations and information about uncertainty in value expression.

</details>


### [150] [Does Reasoning Preserve Alignment? On the Trustworthiness of Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11046)
*Prajakta Kini,Avinash Reddy,Souradip Chakraborty,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Furong Huang,Amrit Singh Bedi,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 推理模型的后训练会损害指令微调模型的对齐行为，导致毒性、刻板印象、隐私泄露等可信度退化，且这种行为漂移与KL散度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将指令微调LLM转化为推理模型的后训练方法（如SFT、RL、蒸馏）仅优化推理精度，缺乏对对齐行为（安全性、偏见、隐私等）保持的关注。作者旨在系统审计这一转化过程是否保持可信度。

Method: 对通过监督微调、强化学习后训练和蒸馏三种方式生成的推理模型，在安全性、毒性、刻板印象与偏见、机器伦理、隐私和分布外鲁棒性六个可信度维度上，与匹配的指令微调基线进行系统对比审计，并引入KL散度量化行为漂移。

Result: 推理模型虽在推理基准上提升，但普遍出现对齐退化：毒性增加、刻板印象放大、拒绝策略失准、上下文隐私泄露。这些退化与KL散度度量的行为漂移一致。

Conclusion: 可信度指标应作为评估推理模型的核心维度，与推理能力增益一并报告，不应被忽视。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned LLMs are increasingly converted into reasoning models through post-training to improve multi-step task performance. This conversion is usually optimized for reasoning accuracy, without explicitly preserving the alignment behavior of the instruction-tuned model, such as safe refusal, bias avoidance, and privacy protection. We ask: does this conversion preserve alignment? We study this question through a trustworthiness audit and find that it is not behavior-preserving by default. For a systematic analysis, we compare reasoning models produced via supervised fine-tuning, RL-based post-training, and distillation against matched instruction-tuned baselines across six trustworthiness dimensions: safety, toxicity, stereotyping and bias, machine ethics, privacy, and out-of-distribution robustness. We observe that reasoning models often improve on reasoning benchmarks but exhibit alignment regressions, including increased toxicity, amplified stereotyping, miscalibrated refusal, and contextual privacy leakage. These regressions are consistent with behavioral drift from the instruction-tuned baseline, measured by KL divergence. Overall, our results point to the broader conclusion that trustworthiness metrics are essential for evaluating reasoning models and should be reported alongside gains in reasoning capability.

</details>


### [151] [Attention Amnesia in Hybrid LLMs: When CoT Fine-Tuning Breaks Long-Range Recall, and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11052)
*Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Zhu,Yi Xu,Zhiwei Li,Yingfa Chen,Huiming Wang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 链式思维监督微调(CoT-SFT)虽能提升推理能力，但会系统性损害混合线性注意力模型的长上下文检索性能。本文提出了一种无需训练的QK-Restore方法，通过恢复微调前的查询-键投影矩阵来修复这一问题，在零训练成本下同时保持推理和长上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现CoT-SFT会系统性损害混合线性注意力模型（如HypeNet、Jet-Nemotron）的长上下文检索能力，且随着检索难度和上下文长度增加，退化更加严重。需要一种方法在不牺牲推理性能的前提下恢复长上下文能力。

Method: 提出QK-Restore方法：仅将查询投影矩阵W_Q和键投影矩阵W_K恢复为SFT前的权重，保留其他所有后SFT参数。进一步引入Procrustes变体，通过正交变换平衡路由保持与推理适应。该方法完全无需训练，零额外训练成本。

Result: 在HypeNet-9B上，NIAH-S2@256K从CoT-SFT后的9.4%（SFT前为67.2%）显著恢复；HypeNet-5B的S3@256K从65.4%提升至76.4%，同时保持强推理性能。方法在多种架构上一致有效。

Conclusion: CoT-SFT通过偏置注意力梯度向短程模式倾斜，破坏了负责长程路由的W_Q和W_K投影。QK-Restore通过选择性恢复这两个投影矩阵，在零训练成本下有效恢复长上下文检索能力并保持推理性能，跨架构验证了该方法的普适性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely adopted to improve reasoning ability, yet we find that it systematically degrades long-context recall in hybrid linear-attention models. Across architectures including HypeNet and Jet-Nemotron, retrieval performance on Needle-In-A-Haystack (NIAH) deteriorates substantially after CoT-SFT, and the degradation becomes more severe under harder retrieval settings and longer context windows. For example, HypeNet-9B on NIAH-S2@256K decreases from $67.2\%$ to $9.4\%$. We attribute this to CoT-SFT biasing attention gradients toward short-range patterns, disrupting query-key projections ($W_Q, W_K$) that are responsible for long-range routing. Motivated by this observation, we propose QK-Restore, a training-free method that restores only $W_Q$ and $W_K$ from the pre-SFT checkpoint while preserving all other post-SFT parameters. We further introduce a Procrustes variant to balance routing preservation and reasoning adaptation. Across architectures, QK-Restore consistently restores long-context capability at zero training cost while preserving reasoning performance; for instance, on HypeNet-5B it improves S3@256K from $65.4\%$ to $76.4\%$ while maintaining strong reasoning performance.

</details>


### [152] [T1-Bench: Benchmarking Multi-Scenario Agents in Real-World Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11070)
*Genta Indra Winata,Amartya Chakraborty,Yuzhen Lin,Swasthi P Rao,Shikhhar Siingh,Houhan Lu,Nadia Bathaee,Sriharsha Hatwar,Paresh Dashore,Anmol Jain,Kshitij Tayal,Xiuzhu Lin,Anirban Das,Sambit Sahu,Shi-Xiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: T1-Bench是一个高保真、全面的agentic系统评测基准，覆盖25个真实客户服务多领域场景，支持多轮用户-助手交互和跨领域交错任务，大幅提升了任务复杂性、交互深度和领域覆盖度，并通过自动评估与人工判断相结合的方式评估12个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent评测基准在任务复杂性、真实性和领域多样性方面存在局限，且难以捕捉跨领域的多步交互场景，无法有效评估需要持续推理和协调的真实多步agent行为。

Method: 提出T1-Bench基准，设计覆盖25个不同难度领域的面向客户的多领域交错场景，要求agent在多轮用户-助手交互中进行结构化推理；使用12个专有和开源模型进行评估，并采用自动评估与人工判断相结合的评估框架。

Result: T1-Bench在任务复杂性、交互深度和领域覆盖方面显著超越先前基准，提供了可复现、标准化的评估框架，能够有效评估agent行为、工具使用和对话质量。

Conclusion: T1-Bench通过提升任务复杂性、交互深度和领域覆盖，为agentic系统的未来研究提供了更严格、更真实的评测平台，并承诺开源数据和评估代码以促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning and tool-calling capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have enabled increasingly capable agentic systems. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task complexity, realism, and domain diversity, and often fail to capture interactions that span multiple domains, limiting their ability to evaluate agents in realistic multi-step settings that require sustained reasoning and coordination. To address these limitations, we introduce T1-Bench, a high-fidelity, comprehensive benchmark for evaluating agentic systems in realistic customer-facing, multi-domain environments, featuring interleaved scenarios that require structured reasoning across multi-turn user-assistant interactions and substantially increasing both compositional complexity and evaluative rigor across 25 domains of varying difficulty. We evaluate T1-Bench using 12 proprietary and open-weight models, providing a reproducible and standardized framework for assessing agent behavior, tool utilization, and conversational quality in complex, multi-step environments. We further complement automatic evaluation with human judgments to strengthen the assessment of qualitative performance. Overall, T1-Bench substantially advances prior benchmarks by increasing task complexity, interaction depth, and domain coverage in simulated multi-domain environments. To facilitate future research on agentic systems, we will publicly release data and evaluation code as open source.

</details>


### [153] [Modeling Complex Behaviors: Multi-Personality Composition and Dynamic Switching in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11074)
*Peiqi Jia,Haonan Jia,Ziqi Miao,Linkang Du,Yuntao Wang,Zhou Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在外显人格条件下的行为表现，建立了涵盖单人格诱导、多人格诱导和人格切换的系统评估框架。实验发现人格诱导可提升图像描述性能，但会损害视觉问答等需精确推理的任务，并揭示了多特质组合和动态切换中的平衡与残留效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MLLMs在社交互动中的广泛部署，理解和控制其在复杂人格条件下的行为变得至关重要。现有基于提示的人格诱导方法在多模态场景中迁移性有限，亟需针对MLLMs的专门化人格建模与评估方法。

Method: 作者引入外显人格条件（explicit personality conditioning），并构建了一个系统评估框架，包含三种实验范式：(1)单人格诱导——测试单一特质对模型的影响；(2)多人格诱导——考察多特质组合的交互效应；(3)人格切换——分析动态人格变化中的行为模式。通过图像描述和视觉问答等下游任务评估人格诱导的效果。

Result: (1)人格诱导提升了图像描述性能，但损害了VQA等需要精确推理的任务表现；(2)多特质组合中存在平衡效应，动态人格切换中存在残留效应——模型行为同时受前序人格和当前人格约束的共同调制；(3)现有的基于提示的人格诱导方法在多模态场景中迁移性有限。

Conclusion: MLLMs中的人格建模具有动态性和复杂性，当前方法尚不完善。研究强调了开发鲁棒、定制化的人格诱导与评估方法的必要性，为未来多模态大模型的人格可控性研究提供了重要方向。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in social interaction, understanding and controlling their behavior under complex personality conditions is essential. This paper introduces explicit personality conditioning and establishes a systematic evaluation framework encompassing single-personality induction, multi-personality induction, and personality switching. Experiments show that personality induction improves image captioning performance but can impair performance on tasks requiring precise reasoning, such as visual question answering (VQA). Balancing and residual effects are observed during multi-trait composition and dynamic switching, indicating that model behavior is co-modulated by both previous and current personality constraints. Existing prompt-based personality induction methods show limited transferability to multimodal settings. Our work reveals the dynamic and complex nature of personality modeling in MLLMs and underscores the need for robust, tailored methods for personality induction and evaluation. The code will be released when the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [154] [VISTA: A Versatile Interactive User Simulation Toolkit for Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11079)
*Yunan Lu,Ryan Shea,Yusen Zhang,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VISTA是一个用于交互式Agent评估的多功能用户模拟工具包，提出了六项评估指标衡量模拟交互的真实性、能力覆盖度和交互有效性，并开发了融合UI操作与API操作的混合用户模拟器，在电商购物和教育客服场景中验证了其优于现有方法的评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交互式Agent评估依赖静态基准，无法捕捉动态多步行为及失败模式；基于用户模拟的评估虽具前景，但现有框架存在两大缺陷：缺乏衡量模拟交互质量和覆盖度的机制，以及仅支持UI操作或API操作单一模式，无法建模完整的真实用户行为。

Method: 提出VISTA工具包：(1)设计了一套六项指标，分别衡量模拟交互的真实性(realism)、能力覆盖度(capability coverage)和交互有效性(interaction effectiveness)；(2)开发了混合用户模拟器，整合UI-based interactions与API-based interactions，实现对多样化交互环境的全面建模。

Result: 在电商购物和教育客服两个场景中评估表明，VISTA相比现有方法能产生更真实、更全面的评估结果。

Conclusion: VISTA通过六项评估指标和混合交互模拟，有效解决了现有用户模拟框架在质量评估和交互建模范围上的不足，为交互式Agent提供了更可靠、更全面的评估方案。

Abstract: Evaluation remains a critical bottleneck for interactive agent development. Existing evaluation methods often rely on static benchmarks, which fail to capture the dynamic, multi-step nature of agentic behavior and struggle to expose meaningful failure modes. While user-simulation-based evaluation offers a promising alternative, existing simulation frameworks suffer from two major limitations. First, they provide limited mechanisms for evaluating the quality and comprehensiveness of simulated interactions, making it difficult to assess whether a simulator sufficiently explores an agent's capabilities and failure modes. Second, most frameworks are restricted to either UI-only actions or API-only actions, limiting their ability to model the full range of realistic user behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose VISTA, a Versatile Interactive user Simulation Toolkit for Agent evaluation. Our toolkit includes a suite of six metrics for measuring the realism, capability coverage, and interaction effectiveness of simulated interactions. In addition, we develop a hybrid user simulator that integrates both UI-based interactions and API-based interactions, enabling more realistic and comprehensive evaluation across diverse interactive environments. We evaluate VISTA in e-commerce shopping and education customer service settings and demonstrate that it produces more realistic and comprehensive evaluations than existing methods.

</details>


### [155] [The Shibboleth Effect: Auditing the Cross-Lingual Distributional Skew of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11082)
*Hakan Mehmetcik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了前沿大语言模型在持续对抗情境下的跨语言分布偏差（Shibboleth效应），通过一个模拟东地中海冲突的地缘政治兵棋推演实验发现，模型在不同语言下的行为偏差取决于架构与训练机制，而非西方模型普遍存在此效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前沿大语言模型在跨语言（英语 vs. 土耳其语）对抗性外交场景中是否存在系统性的行为偏差，即"Shibboleth效应"——模型是否因使用不同语言而表现出不同的让步率或胁迫性修辞，以及该效应究竟是西方来源LLM的普遍属性还是取决于具体模型架构与训练机制。

Method: 设计了一个名为"Cerulean Sea Crisis"的多智能体地缘政治兵棋推演，模拟东地中海海洋领土争端的结构动态。六种前沿模型（GPT-4o、Llama-4、Mistral-Large、Gemini-3.1-Pro、Qwen3.6-Plus、DeepSeek-R1）参与组间实验（每组10局，每局5轮），唯一操纵变量为游戏语言（英语 vs. 土耳其语），共产生586条验证语句。采用零样本分类器沿"让步率"和"胁迫性修辞"两个连续维度评估行为倾向。

Result: 结果呈异质性：(1) Llama-4在土耳其语条件下胁迫性修辞显著增加（Δ=+0.800, p=.002，Holm校正后）；(2) Gemini-3.1-Pro显著减少（Δ=-0.750, p=.005）；(3) DeepSeek-R1同样显著减少（Δ=-0.860, p=.006），其思维链证据显示存在缓冲机制；(4) GPT-4o无显著效应（Δ=+0.130, p=.614）。表明跨语言行为偏差取决于模型架构与训练机制，并非普遍属性。

Conclusion: 跨语言行为偏差（Shibboleth效应）在前沿LLM中并非普遍存在，而是具有模型异质性。识别出两种缓冲机制：思维链制度锚定（DeepSeek-R1）和多语言RLHF对齐。这些发现对将LLM安全整合进外交与危机管理场景具有重要启示。

Abstract: This study investigates cross-lingual distributional skew (the Shibboleth Effect) in frontier large language models (LLMs) subjected to sustained adversarial conditions. We develop a multi-agent geopolitical wargame, the Cerulean Sea Crisis, a synthetic maritime territorial dispute designed to mirror the structural dynamics of Eastern Mediterranean conflicts. Six frontier models (GPT-4o, Llama-4, Mistral-Large, Gemini-3.1-Pro, Qwen3.6-Plus, and DeepSeek-R1) participate in a between-groups experiment (N = 10 games per arm, K = 5 rounds per game) in which the sole manipulation is the language of play (English versus Turkish), producing 586 validated statements. A zero-shot classifier assesses behavioral dispositions along two continuous dimensions: Concession Rate and Coercive Rhetoric. The results are heterogeneous. Llama-4 shows a substantial, Holm-corrected increase in coercive rhetoric under Turkish (delta = +0.800, p = .002), whereas Gemini-3.1-Pro displays an equally large decrease (delta = -0.750, p = .005). DeepSeek-R1 exhibits a similar negative shift (delta = -0.860, p = .006) and provides chain-of-thought evidence consistent with a buffering mechanism. GPT-4o shows no detectable effect (delta = +0.130, p = .614). These findings indicate that cross-lingual behavioral skew is contingent on model architecture and training regime rather than a universal property of Western-origin LLMs. We identify two distinct buffering mechanisms, chain-of-thought institutional anchoring and multilingual RLHF alignment, and discuss their implications for integrating LLMs safely into diplomatic and crisis-management settings.

</details>


### [156] [PhantomBench: Benchmarking the Non-existential Threat of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11105)
*Haeji Jung,Hila Gonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhantomBench 是一个包含6万多个不存在术语的大规模基准测试，用于评估语言模型能否识别自身知识边界。评估21个模型后发现幻觉率惊人（最高达86.7%），即使是前沿模型也难以对不存在概念进行拒答，尤其当输入预设其存在时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的幻觉问题在高风险领域尤为危险，但现有研究对模型能否可靠地识别自身知识边界仍不清楚。作者旨在系统性地量化模型在面对不存在概念时的幻觉行为，填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建PhantomBench基准，从真实概念出发衍生出超过6万个不存在的术语和实体，覆盖多个领域。使用该基准评估21个不同类型和规模的模型，并提供一个可扩展的流水线，供研究者按需生成定制化的不存在概念。

Result: 所有模型均表现出惊人的幻觉率，部分情况下平均高达86.7%。即使是前沿模型，当输入预设不存在概念为真实存在时，也难以正确拒答。此外，PhantomBench可作为研究模型在稀有概念上行为的代理工具。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型在识别知识边界方面存在严重缺陷，PhantomBench为系统性地诊断和改进这一问题提供了重要基准和工具。

Abstract: Hallucinations, where language models (LMs) generate factually ungrounded responses, pose serious risks, as users tend to blindly rely on them. This is particularly concerning in high-stakes domains, where consequences of such model behavior can lead to significant harms. Despite notable progress in understanding hallucinations, it remains unclear how reliably these models can recognize the limits of their knowledge. We introduce PhantomBench, the first large-scale benchmark of its kind, comprising more than 60K non-existent terms and entities derived from real concepts across diverse domains. Using our benchmark, we evaluate a total of 21 models of various types and sizes. We show staggering hallucination rates across the board (with average rates as high as 86.7% in some cases), and note that even frontier models surprisingly fail to abstain on non-existent concepts, especially when the input presumes their existence. We then show that PhantomBench can serve as a proxy for studying model behavior on rare concepts for which models are more prone to hallucinate. We also provide a pipeline to construct PhantomBench, enabling scalable generation of non-existent concepts tailored to the specific needs of researchers and practitioners.

</details>


### [157] [Provenance-Grounded Gating and Adaptive Recovery in Synthetic Post-Training Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11127)
*Soham Bhattacharjee,Karun Sharma,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu,Pratinav Seth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了合成数据后处理流程中两个常被忽视的问题：过滤信号是否有源证据支撑，以及被拒绝的样本能否通过恢复策略重新利用而非直接丢弃。通过在合成语料中注入对抗性样本提供真实标签，研究发现：源出处信息能提升强评判器的忠实度把关效果；幻觉门控和奖励门控拒绝的样本群体高度不重叠，两者都不可或缺；结合失败诊断与定向重生成的自适应恢复流程在产出率、恢复率和注入召回率上均优于简单重采样；下游微调质量主要由生成器规模驱动，过滤与恢复条件贡献显著但居次要地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前合成数据后处理流程普遍使用奖励模型或整体性LLM评判器来过滤生成样本，但两个关键实践问题鲜少被共同审视：(1) 过滤信号是否根植于触发该生成的源证据；(2) 被拒绝的样本是否可以被系统性地恢复而非永久丢弃。论文旨在通过受控实验填补这一空白。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，通过向合成语料中对抗性注入样本以提供真实失败标签（ground-truth）。研究覆盖三个维度：门控配置（幻觉门控与奖励门控）、恢复策略（包括失败诊断结合定向重生成的自适应恢复、简单重采样等）以及生成器规模。对比分析了不同条件下源出处信息对忠实度把关的影响、两类门控拒绝样本的重叠程度，以及恢复策略的效果差异。

Result: (1) 精确的源出处信息能提升强评判器的忠实度把关效果；(2) 幻觉门控与奖励门控拒绝的样本群体基本不重叠，二者均不可或缺；(3) 自适应恢复流程（失败诊断+定向重生成）在产出率、恢复率和注入召回率上均显著优于简单重采样；(4) 下游微调质量主要由生成器规模决定，过滤与恢复条件有显著但次要的贡献。

Conclusion: 源证据信息对提升合成数据质量把关至关重要；幻觉检测与奖励评分的过滤机制互补，应联合使用；被拒绝样本不应简单丢弃，通过诊断失败原因并有针对性地重生成可有效回收利用；在资源有限的场景下，扩大生成器规模仍是提升下游质量的首要手段，但合理的过滤与恢复策略能带来增量收益。

Abstract: Synthetic post-training pipelines commonly filter generated samples with reward models or holistic LLM judges, yet two practices remain rarely examined together: whether the filtering signal is grounded in the source evidence that induced each generation, and whether rejected samples can be systematically recovered rather than permanently discarded. We present a controlled study of both questions across gate configurations, recovery strategies, and generator scales, using adversarially injected corpora to provide ground-truth failure labels. We find that exact source provenance improves faithfulness gating for stronger judges, that hallucination and reward gates reject largely disjoint sample populations making both necessary, and that an adaptive recovery pipeline combining failure diagnosis with targeted regeneration achieves higher yield, recovery rate, and injection recall than naive resampling. Downstream fine-tuning quality is driven primarily by generator scale, with filtration and recovery conditions contributing meaningfully but secondarily.

</details>


### [158] [Multi-Faceted Interactivity Alignment in Full-Duplex Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11167)
*Atsumoto Ohashi,Neil Zeghidour,Alexandre Défossez,Eugene Kharitonov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向全双工口语对话模型的RL后训练对齐方法，围绕停顿处理、话轮转换、反向通道和用户打断四个交互维度设计奖励函数，结合LLM质量奖励，在Moshi和PersonaPlex模型上实现了一致的交互性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全双工口语对话模型仅通过监督学习（token级似然最大化）训练，缺乏对交互级行为的直接优化，导致过度沉默、话轮时机不当等交互性问题。现有RL方法仅覆盖有限交互行为，缺乏全面改善方案。

Method: 提出四轴交互性RL后训练对齐方法：(1) 停顿处理——从人类对话语料提取短音频片段；(2) 话轮转换；(3) 反向通道（backchanneling）；(4) 用户打断。每轴设计专属奖励函数，并额外引入基于LLM的回复质量奖励防止语义退化。在Moshi和PersonaPlex两个开源模型上应用该方法。

Result: 在离线评估（预录音频）和实时多轮对话评估中，两模型在四个交互维度上均表现出一致的交互性改善。

Conclusion: 通过RL全面优化全双工模型的四个交互维度，可显著提升对话交互自然度，且LLM质量奖励有效防止语义退化，为构建更自然的人机口语对话系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue models can listen and speak simultaneously, making them a promising architecture for natural conversation. However, current models are trained solely with supervised learning through token-level likelihood maximization, which does not directly optimize interaction-level behaviors, causing interactivity issues such as excessive silence and ill-timed turn-taking. Recent work has applied reinforcement learning (RL) to improve interactivity, but existing methods address only a limited set of interactive behaviors in their rewards. In this work, we propose a post-training alignment method that comprehensively improves the interactivity of full-duplex spoken dialogue models through RL. We address the four canonical axes of interactivity: pause handling, turn-taking, backchanneling, and user interruption. For each axis, we extract short audio segments from human conversation corpora and optimize the model with axis-specific reward functions. An extra LLM-based reward for response quality prevents semantic degradation. We apply our method to two open-source models, Moshi and PersonaPlex, demonstrating consistent improvements in interactivity on both offline evaluation with pre-recorded audio and real-time multi-turn dialogue evaluation.

</details>


### [159] [Gradient-Guided Reward Optimization for Inference-time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09635)
*Hankun Lin,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Gradient-Guided Reward Optimization (GGRO) 是一种轻量级推理时对齐方法，通过监控 token 级熵检测分布漂移，并利用奖励模型梯度信号注入"微调 token"来引导生成轨迹，突破了传统 Best-of-N 和拒绝采样的性能上限与奖励黑客瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法（如 Best-of-N、拒绝采样）存在两个根本局限：(1) 性能受限于基模型生成质量的上限；(2) 依赖不完美的奖励模型进行重排序，容易受到奖励黑客（reward hacking）攻击。需要一种能主动干预生成过程而非被动重排序的方法。

Method: GGRO 在解码阶段工作，包含三个核心机制：(1) 熵监控——实时计算 token 级熵值，识别高不确定性区域作为分布漂移/错位的信号；(2) 梯度信号提取——从现成的奖励模型获取梯度，生成"微调 token"（nudging tokens）；(3) 轨迹引导——将微调 token 注入解码过程，主动引导生成轨迹朝向高奖励方向，而非仅重排序已生成样本。整个过程计算开销极小。

Result: 实验显示，GGRO 在安全性（safety）、有用性（helpfulness）和推理（reasoning）三个基准上均一致性地提升了推理时对齐效果。同时提高了高质量响应的覆盖率和对奖励黑客的鲁棒性，且仅引入最小的计算开销。

Conclusion: GGRO 证明了通过在解码时利用梯度信号进行最小化、针对性的干预，可以有效突破传统采样-重排序范式的性能天花板，为 LLM 推理时对齐提供了一种更高效、更鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) under distribution drift requires inference-time adaptation. While inference-time alignment methods such as Best-of-$N$ and rejection sampling are widely used, they frame the task as a sampling-intensive, reward-guided search, leading to two key limitations: their performance is bounded by the base model's generation quality, and their reliance on imperfect reward models makes them vulnerable to reward hacking. To address these challenges, we introduce Gradient-Guided Reward Optimization (GGRO), a lightweight inference-time method that performs targeted, minimal intervention during decoding via gradient guidance. Specifically, GGRO monitors token-level entropy to identify high-uncertainty regions indicative of drift or misalignment. Upon detection, it responds by injecting nudging tokens, generated using gradient signals from an off-the-shelf reward model, to steer the generation trajectory rather than merely re-ranking samples. Experiments show that GGRO consistently improves inference-time alignment across safety, helpfulness, and reasoning benchmarks. It also increases coverage of high-quality responses and robustness to reward hacking, with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/lhk2004/GGRO.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [An information-geometric framework for mapping maximum potential biodiversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09906)
*Shinto Eguchi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个信息几何框架，通过将观测群落与由约束变分原理得到的"潜在群落"在概率单纯形上进行比较，定义了"潜力多样性"和"多样性差距"，统一处理Hill型多样性和Rao二次熵，为保护规划提供站点层面的参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多样性测度多为描述性使用，仅计算并空间映射多样性指数，缺乏站点层面的规范性基准。保护规划需要回答"群落现状与其生态潜力相差多远"的问题，这需要一个理论框架来定义可与观测值对比的局部潜力基准。

Method: 在物种单纯形上引入一对概率向量：观测组成和由约束变分原理获得的潜力组成。利用信息几何工具，通过护卫约束、容量约束和散度投影定义非平凡的潜力基准（超越均匀分布）。多样性差距通过比较两者上的多样性泛函得到。框架兼容Hill型多样性（丰度与均匀度）和Rao二次熵（纳入性状/系统发育/生态差异）。还通过空间点过程解释局部生态容量，并给出动态扩展方向。

Result: 该框架成功分离了两个问题：群落有多多样，以及它离局部可容许的潜力基准有多远。将"暗多样性"的生态学概念与概率单纯形上连续的、丰度加权的比较联系起来，提供了一个统一、可操作的多样性差距定义。

Conclusion: 信息几何方法为生物多样性评估提供了描述与规范双重功能，通过定义潜力多样性和多样性差距，弥补了传统方法缺乏站点基准的不足。未来可将该框架与大规模公民科学数据和性状数据库结合进行实证验证。

Abstract: Biodiversity measures are often used descriptively: one computes a diversity index from an observed or estimated community composition and maps the resulting values across space. Conservation planning, however, also requires a site-specific benchmark against which the observed community can be compared. This chapter develops an information-geometric framework for such \emph{potential diversity} and the associated \emph{diversity gap}. The central object is a pair of probability vectors on the species simplex: an observed or realized composition \(p^{\mathrm{obs}}\), and a potential composition \(p^{\mathrm{pot}}\) obtained by a constrained variational principle. The gap is then defined by comparing a diversity functional at these two compositions. The framework is developed for both Hill-type diversity, which measures abundance and evenness, and Rao's quadratic entropy, which incorporates trait, phylogenetic, or ecological dissimilarities among species. A spatial point-process interpretation clarifies how local ecological capacities can be defined before passing to the simplex. Escort constraints, capacity constraints, and divergence projections then provide a unified way to define nontrivial benchmarks beyond the uniform distribution. The resulting formulation separates two distinct questions: how diverse a community is, and how far it is from a locally admissible potential benchmark. It also connects the ecological idea of dark diversity with a continuous, abundance-weighted comparison on the probability simplex. We also outline a dynamic extension in which capacities, species migration, and climate-driven shifts vary over time. Empirical implementation with large-scale citizen-science biodiversity data and trait databases is left for future work.

</details>


### [161] [Estimating the Wasserstein barycenter of one-dimensional distributions under sparse sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10096)
*James Peng,Florian Stijven,Linbo Wang,Peter B. Gilbert*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 在稀疏采样下，经验Wasserstein重心估计量存在严重偏差。本文提出了一种基于边际构造的重心（MCB）估计方法，通过二项混合模型估计潜在单位水平分位数的分布，进而取其均值得到Wasserstein重心分位数函数的点估计。该方法避免了直接估计单位水平分布，在稀疏采样下具有点态一致性和渐近正态性，模拟和HIV-1序列数据分析均验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏采样场景下，每个统计单元仅通过少量i.i.d.观测表征其概率分布。此时，直接将经验分布视为真实分布的朴素Wasserstein重心估计量（即经验Wasserstein重心）会产生严重偏差。因此，需要一种无需直接估计单位水平分布或总体分布律即能获得Wasserstein重心分位数函数的一致估计方法。

Method: 首先，将给定分位数水平下潜在单位水平分位数的分布用单位水平CDF值的边际分布来刻画；其次，利用二项混合模型（binomial mixture methods）估计这些边际分布；最后，取该估计分布的均值得到边际构造重心（MCB）估计量。该方法跳过了对各单位分布的直接估计，从总体层面直接对目标参数进行推断。

Result: 理论层面，建立了MCB估计量点态一致性和渐近正态性的条件。模拟研究表明，在稀疏采样下MCB估计量显著优于经验Wasserstein重心。在HVTN 502/503 HIV-1疫苗功效试验的序列数据分析中，利用MCB方法在每位参与者仅有少量序列的情况下，有效总结了参与者内部的病毒序列特征分布并进行比较。

Conclusion: MCB估计量通过边际构造策略有效解决了稀疏采样下Wasserstein重心估计的偏差问题，兼具理论保证和实际应用价值。该方法为分布型数据（distributional data）的中心趋势分析提供了一种稳健且高效的统计工具，尤其适用于序列数据稀疏但需跨单元比较的生物学应用场景。

Abstract: We study distributional data under sparse sampling where each unit is represented by a probability distribution on the real line observed only through a small i.i.d.~sample. A natural notion of central tendency for one-dimensional distributional data is the Wasserstein barycenter, whose quantile function is the pointwise average of the unit-level quantile functions. We focus on pointwise estimation of the Wasserstein barycenter quantile function: at a given quantile level, the target is the population mean of the corresponding unit-level quantiles. A naive plug-in estimator is the empirical Wasserstein barycenter, which treats observed unit-level empirical distributions as the true latent unit-level distributions. Under sparse sampling, however, this estimator can be severely biased. We propose an approach that avoids directly estimating either the unit-level distributions or the full population law of distributions. We start with the more ambitious goal of characterizing the distribution of latent unit-level quantiles at a given quantile level. We show that this distribution can be written in terms of the marginal distributions of the unit-level CDF values, which can be estimated using binomial mixture methods. This motivates our estimator, the marginal-constructed barycenter (MCB) estimator, obtained by taking the mean of the estimated distribution of latent unit-level quantiles. We establish conditions under which the MCB estimator is pointwise consistent and asymptotically normal, and show through simulations that it can substantially outperform the empirical Wasserstein barycenter under sparse sampling. We illustrate the method in an analysis of HIV-1 sequence data from the HVTN 502/503 vaccine efficacy trials, using the barycenter to summarize and compare within-participant distributions of viral sequence features when only a small number of sequences are available per participant.

</details>


### [162] [Methods for adjusting for covariate measurement error in flexible modelling of functional form: results of a blinded, controlled neutral comparison simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10123)
*Mohammed Sedki,Aris Perperoglou,Anne C. M. Thiébaut,Steve Ferreira Guerra,Paul Gustafson,Frank E. Harrell,Willi Sauerbrei,Michal Abrahamowicz,Laurence S. Freedman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项盲法、多阶段的中立比较模拟研究，系统评估了在非线性暴露-结局关联下，测量误差校正方法与灵活函数形式建模相结合的23种分析策略的表现。研究发现，逐点SIMEX方法整体最准确且最稳健，而B样条表现始终较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量测量误差在流行病学研究中普遍存在，会扭曲暴露-结局关联的估计。然而，现有的校正方法几乎均在线性建模假设下被研究，当底层关联为非线性且采用灵活回归建模时，这些校正方法的表现鲜有系统评估。

Method: 在STRATOS倡议框架下，设计了一项盲法、多阶段的中立比较模拟研究。将六类测量误差校正方法（逐点SIMEX、基于系数的SIMEX、logit尺度贝叶斯推断、风险尺度贝叶斯推断、多重插补MI、回归校准RC）分别与四种灵活建模方法（B样条BS、惩罚样条PS、分数多项式FP、自然样条NS）组合，形成23种分析方法。在五种函数形式（J形、线性、两个阈值模型、饱和模型）下生成病例对照数据，系统变化样本量、重复子研究规模、误差大小和误差分布，假设经典加性误差并使用重复测量子研究进行误差校准。

Result: 逐点SIMEX是整体最准确且最稳健的方法；贝叶斯方法和RC与PS、FP或NS配对时表现次之；MI表现较差；贝叶斯估计与未惩罚的B样条组合表现最差。PS、FP和NS三者表现近乎等效，而B样条始终表现较劣。没有任何单一方法在所有场景中均占优。

Conclusion: 在非线性暴露-结局关联下进行测量误差校正时，逐点SIMEX是首选方法，灵活建模方面应优先选用惩罚样条、分数多项式或自然样条，而非未惩罚的B样条。由于没有单一方法在所有场景中占优，强调进行敏感性分析的价值。

Abstract: Covariate measurement error is pervasive in epidemiological research and distorts estimated exposure-outcome associations, yet correction methods have been studied almost exclusively under linear modelling assumptions. Their behaviour when the underlying association is non-linear and is itself estimated with flexible regression, remains poorly characterised. We report a blinded, multi-stage neutral comparison simulation study, conducted within the STRATOS initiative, evaluating measurement error correction coupled with flexible modelling of functional form. Six families of correction methods (pointwise and coefficient-based Simulation Extrapolation [SIMEX], Bayesian inference on the logit and risk scales, Multiple Imputation [MI], and Regression Calibration [RC]) were each combined with B-splines (BS), penalised splines (PS), fractional polynomials (FP), and natural splines (NS), yielding 23 analytic methods. Methods were applied to case-control data generated under five functional forms (J-shape, linear, two threshold models, and saturation) across simulated datasets spanning varying sample sizes, replication substudy sizes, error magnitudes, and error distributions, with classical additive error and a replication substudy for error calibration. Performance was assessed by the log mean squared error of the estimated function over the central 95 % of the exposure distribution. Pointwise SIMEX was the most accurate and most robust approach overall, followed by Bayesian methods and RC when paired with PS, FP, or NS; MI performed less well, and Bayesian estimation with unpenalised BS performed worst. PS, FP, and NS were near-equivalent, whereas BS was consistently inferior. No single method dominated across all scenarios, underscoring the value of sensitivity analyses.

</details>


### [163] [Spatial Prediction of Local Soil Erosion Distribution in the Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10224)
*Jiaming Qiu,Xiongtao Dai,Zhengyuan Zhu,Shuiqing Yin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Wasserstein空间的侵蚀分布空间预测方法，将局部侵蚀分布表示为平方可积轨迹，通过基展开构建多变量随机场，结合局部回归与Kriging实现任意位置侵蚀分布的灵活预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的侵蚀测量需要昂贵的野外工作，直接对大范围区域（如省份或流域）进行调查不可行，因此需要一种能将有限野外调查结果外推至更大范围的空间预测方法。

Method: 将局部侵蚀分布视为Wasserstein空间中的对象，映射为平方可积轨迹并通过基展开表示，构建捕捉空间依赖性的多变量随机场；在此基础上应用局部回归和Kriging进行预测。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法优于错误指定的参数替代方法和现有Fréchet回归方法；在陕西省的应用中，利用土地利用和海拔等协变量，成功将调查流域的局部测量扩展为全省侵蚀分布预测。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在分布泛函（如均值和超越概率）的预测上表现优越，为大规模侵蚀评估提供了一种灵活且有效的统计工具。

Abstract: Obtaining precise erosion measurements requires costly fieldwork, making it infeasible to directly survey large domains such as a province or river basin. To extend fieldwork results across such extensive domains, we propose a novel spatial prediction method that treats local erosion distributions as objects in the Wasserstein space. These distributions are mapped into square-integrable trajectories and represented via basis expansion, forming a multivariate random field that captures spatial dependence. By applying local regression and Kriging in this representation, our approach flexibly models and predicts erosion distributions at arbitrary locations. This framework improves prediction for functionals of the distribution, such as the mean and exceedance probabilities. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms a misspecified parametric alternative and existing Fréchet regression approaches. We illustrate the approach with a detailed erosion analysis in Shaanxi province, China, where local measurements from surveyed watersheds are extended to predict erosion distributions across the entire province using covariates such as land use and elevation.

</details>


### [164] [Robust Bayesian Predictive Model Selection using Bregman Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10409)
*Jongwoo Choi,Neil A. Spencer,Dipak K. Dey*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bregman评分规则的广义ELPD框架，替代传统对数评分，通过β-散度族控制预测比较对低密度观测的敏感性，从而减少模型排名对离群点的过度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的留一法交叉验证依赖基于对数评分的ELPD，导致模型排名对离群值和尾部失配过度敏感。作者希望构建一个通过评分规则参数调节敏感性的通用框架，在模型误设下仍能稳健地选出最接近真实数据生成过程的模型。

Method: 提出评分匹配的广义ELPD：用Bregman评分规则替换对数评分，通过广义后验更新模型参数，并在选定评分规则下评估留一法预测效用。重点研究β-散度族，其中β参数控制对低密度观测的敏感性。在模型误设下，该过程渐近选择在所选Bregman散度下预测分布最接近真实数据生成过程的模型。

Result: 模拟研究和微生物及法医学实际数据应用表明，广义ELPD能够通过降低对低密度观测的敏感性来改变所选模型，验证了框架的有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 评分匹配的广义ELPD框架成功实现了对传统ELPD的直接推广，通过β-散度调节敏感性使模型比较更加稳健，在模型误设场景下具有理论保证和实用价值。

Abstract: Predictive Bayesian model comparison often relies on leave-one-out (LOO) cross-validation criteria such as the expected log predictive density (ELPD). However, model rankings can be overly sensitive to outliers and tail mismatch because ELPD is based on the log score. We propose a score-matched generalized ELPD framework that replaces the log score by a Bregman scoring rule to update model parameters through a generalized posterior and to evaluate LOO predictive utility. Candidate posterior predictive distributions are ranked by out-of-sample utility under the chosen scoring rule, yielding a direct proper-score generalization of standard ELPD. We focus especially on the $β$-divergence family, where $β$ controls the sensitivity of predictive comparison to low-density observations. Under model misspecification, the procedure asymptotically selects the model whose predictive distribution is closest to the data-generating process under the chosen Bregman divergence. A simulation study and applications to microbial and forensic data show that the generalized ELPD can change the selected model through reduced sensitivity to low-density observations.

</details>


### [165] [Minimum free energy randomized design to improve covariate balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10497)
*Haolin Chen,Jun Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小自由能的随机化试验设计方法，借鉴热力学第二定律，在协变量平衡（阻断）与随机性（熵最大化）之间建立显式权衡，并开发了具有理论保证的动态分配算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机对照试验的分配策略缺乏对"阻断实现的协变量平衡"与"随机化保证的稳健性"之间权衡的量化框架，亟需一个统一准则来同时优化两者。

Method: 受热力学第二定律启发，提出最小自由能准则——同时最小化协变量不平衡（类比能量）并最大化分配熵（类比熵）；基于此推导出最小自由能随机化设计，并开发了具有理论保证的计算高效动态分配算法；利用有限样本方差分解分析其统计性质。

Result: 该方法能够控制协变量不平衡，同时防止未观测异质性主导均方误差，在给定设计约束下保持极小化极大效率。大量数值模拟表明，该方法在统计效率和稳健性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的最小自由能随机化设计首次正式实现了阻断与随机化之间的显式量化权衡，兼具高统计效率与强稳健性，为随机对照试验的分配策略提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: ``Block what you can and randomize what you cannot'' is the core principle for treatment effect estimation in randomized controlled trials. Although a wealth of allocation strategies has been developed, an explicit trade-off between the covariate balance achieved by blocking and the robustness guaranteed by randomization is seldom quantified. Motivated by the second law of thermodynamics, this work posits a new criterion that lowers the covariate imbalance while maximizing the entropy that quantifies contrast and allocation diversity. The resulting optimal strategy, termed the minimum free energy randomized design, is then derived, thereby formally achieving such a trade-off. To facilitate practical implementation, we further develop a computationally efficient dynamic allocation algorithm with theoretical guarantees. Using a finite-sample variance decomposition, the proposed randomization strategy is shown to control covariate imbalance while preventing unobserved heterogeneity from dominating the mean squared error, thus retaining minimax efficiency under the prescribed design constraints. Extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that our method achieves superior statistical efficiency and greater robustness than existing approaches.

</details>


### [166] [Deterministic Denominator Design for Localized Tamed Stochastic-Gradient Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10559)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性分母的驯化随机梯度朗之万动力学（SGLD），提出通过代理得分、经验分位数阈值和校准层来设计状态依赖包络，以避免随机分母导致的均值偏移，实验表明该方法接近oracle得分行为且优于简单的确定性驯化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的驯化SGLD通过添加分母来稳定大幅漂移，但当分母使用与更新步相同的随机梯度样本时，会改变条件均值漂移，引入一个有害的均值偏移通道。作者希望设计确定性分母——在采样当前oracle样本之前就固定状态依赖包络，从而消除随机分母带来的均值偏移问题。

Method: 设计流程分为三步：(1) 从oracle得分出发，在引导状态上构建低成本的代理得分；(2) 通过经验分位数选择激活阈值；(3) 施加一个小的校准层。理论分析追踪三个误差传播阶段：代理误差和阈值误差转化为包络误差，包络误差扰动单步SGLD，局部残差通过条件扰动桥给出稳态误差。

Result: 理论分析建立了代理-分位数分母到稳态误差的完整误差传播链。实验表明，代理-分位数分母的性能接近oracle得分行为，成功避开了随机分母的均值偏移通道，且优于简单的确定性驯化选择。

Conclusion: 通过代理得分、经验分位数和校准层设计的确定性分母，能够有效替代随机分母，在避免均值偏移的同时保持接近oracle的采样质量，为驯化SGLD的实践部署提供了更优的方案。

Abstract: Tamed stochastic-gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD) stabilizes large drifts by adding a denominator to the update. If this denominator uses the same stochastic-gradient sample as the update step, it can also change the conditional mean drift. We study deterministic denominators: the state-dependent envelope is fixed before the current oracle sample is drawn. The main question is how to design this envelope in practice. The design starts from an oracle score, builds a low-cost proxy score on pilot states, chooses activation thresholds by empirical quantiles, and then applies a small calibration layer. The analysis tracks three steps: proxy and threshold errors become envelope errors; envelope errors perturb one SGLD step; and the local residuals give stationary errors through a conditional perturbation bridge. Experiments show that the proxy-quantile denominators are close to oracle-score behavior, avoid the random-denominator mean-shift channel, and improve simple deterministic taming choices.

</details>


### [167] [Predicting Current Outcomes From Historical Survey Data With Weighted Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10563)
*Chihoon Lee,Sungkyu Jung,Hyokyung G. Hong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对大规模复杂调查中部分指标仅在特定年份测量导致记录不完整的问题，本文提出了一种加权共形预测框架，通过整合子群密度比和子群比例估计来近似历史与目标协变量分布之间的似然比，在协变量偏移和复杂调查设计下实现对未观测结果的有效总体水平预测，并提供了覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模复杂调查（如NHANES）中，许多健康指标仅在特定年份测量，导致跨调查波次的记录不完整。现有方法难以同时处理协变量分布随时间演变（协变量偏移）以及复杂调查设计带来的代表性偏差，亟需一种能在这些条件下进行有效推断的方法。

Method: 提出加权共形预测框架，核心步骤包括：(1) 利用历史调查数据，通过整合子群特定密度比估计和子群比例估计来近似历史与目标分布之间的似然比；(2) 将该似然比作为权重引入共形预测流程中，以校正协变量偏移和调查设计效应；(3) 在加权框架下构建预测集，并建立有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法在多种协变量分布情景下均能达到接近名义水平的覆盖。在预测当前美国人群低密度脂蛋白胆固醇（LDL-C）的应用中，该方法相比现有方法在覆盖准确性和效率方面均有提升，尤其在协变量分布复杂或未知时优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权共形预测框架能够有效处理大规模调查中的协变量偏移和复杂调查设计问题，为未观测结果提供具有理论覆盖保证的预测集。该方法在实际应用中展现出良好的覆盖性能和效率，为利用历史调查数据推断当前人群健康指标提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: In large-scale complex surveys such as the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES), some outcomes are measured only in selected years, leaving incomplete records across survey waves. We develop a weighted conformal prediction framework that enables valid population-level prediction of unobserved outcomes using information from earlier surveys. The method accommodates covariate shift, where both continuous and categorical covariate distributions evolve over time while survey design affects representativeness. It integrates subgroup-specific density ratio and subgroup-proportion estimation to approximate likelihood ratios between the historical and target covariate distributions, and we establish coverage guarantees for the resulting prediction sets. Simulation studies and an application predicting low-density lipoprotein cholesterol (LDL-C) for the current U.S. population show that the proposed approach achieves coverage close to the nominal level and improved efficiency over existing methods, particularly when covariate distributions are complex or unknown.

</details>


### [168] [Data compression for fast dimension reduction and clustering of high-dimensional discrete data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10593)
*Silvia D'Angelo,Michael Fop*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性降维框架，通过缩放位置编码的加权和将高维离散数据压缩为低维连续表示。该方法适用于二值、分类和计数数据，理论上保证了映射的单射性、压缩变量的近似高斯性以及聚类中心分离性的保持，在模拟和实际数据中均展现出准确且高效的聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维离散数据广泛存在于基因组学、微生物组、调查研究和数字行为分析等领域，但现有聚类方法往往计算量大、对稀疏性和离散性敏感，或仅针对特定数据类型设计，缺乏一种通用、高效且理论完备的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于缩放位置编码（scaled positional encoding）的确定性降维框架：将每个高维离散观测通过加权和压缩为低维连续表示。该方法具有数值稳定性，适用于二值、分类和计数型数据。

Result: 理论层面：（1）映射是单射，保证不同观测压缩后仍可区分；（2）在温和正则条件下压缩变量近似服从高斯分布，为压缩空间中的基于模型的聚类提供理论依据；（3）聚类中心间的分离性在压缩后得以保持，确保位置驱动的聚类结构可识别。模拟实验表明该方法在多种现实场景下聚类恢复准确，且计算效率显著优于常用降维方法。在爱尔兰婴儿名字记录和微生物组数据上的应用进一步验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维离散数据聚类提供了一种可扩展、计算高效且广泛适用的方法，兼具理论保证与实用价值。

Abstract: High-dimensional discrete data arise in many contemporary applications, including genomics, microbiome research, survey studies, and digital behavioral analysis. Clustering such data remains challenging because existing methods are often computationally demanding, sensitive to sparsity and discreteness, or designed for specific data types. We propose a deterministic dimension-reduction framework for clustering high-dimensional discrete observations. The method compresses each observation into a low-dimensional continuous representation through weighted sums defined by a scaled positional encoding, yielding a numerically stable transformation applicable to binary, categorical, and count-valued data. We establish several theoretical properties of the proposed compression. The mapping is injective, ensuring that distinct observations remain distinct after compression. Under mild regularity conditions, the compressed variables admit an approximate Gaussian representation, providing a theoretical basis for model-based clustering in the compressed space. We further show that separation between cluster centroids is preserved under compression, implying that location-driven cluster structure remains identifiable after dimension reduction. Extensive simulation studies demonstrate accurate cluster recovery across a wide range of realistic settings. The proposed approach is also computationally efficient, providing substantial speed improvements over commonly used dimension-reduction techniques often used in conjunction with clustering. Applications to Irish baby-name records and microbiome data further illustrate its practical utility. The proposed framework offers a scalable, computationally efficient, and broadly applicable approach to clustering high-dimensional discrete data.

</details>


### [169] [Two-Sample Homogeneity Test via Entropic Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10767)
*Yiming Ma,Hang Liu,Weiwei Zhuang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于熵最优传输映射的两样本同质性检验方法，以单位球上的均匀分布为参考，利用两个经验EOT映射的L²距离作为检验统计量，证明了理论性质并提出了有效的自助法校准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两样本检验方法通常只提供一个标量差异度量，缺乏对分布间差异的诊断信息。作者希望开发一种既能有效检验分布差异，又能揭示差异具体形式的非参数检验方法。

Method: 以单位球上均匀分布为公共参考分布，分别估计两个样本到该参考分布的熵最优传输(EOT)映射，以两个经验EOT映射的平方L²距离作为检验统计量。证明映射差异的可识别性，推导原假设下的函数中心极限定理，建立二次型极限分布。采用加权乘数自助法校准非枢轴零分布，并证明其有效性。

Result: 理论证明：原假设下检验统计量收敛于高斯二次型；固定备择假设下具有一致性；在邻近备择假设下刻画了局部渐近功效。模拟显示该方法具有可靠的有限样本第I类错误控制，在位置备择下功效突出，且能提供分布差异的额外诊断信息。真实数据分析验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于EOT映射的两样本检验方法不仅在理论上严谨（具有可识别性、极限分布和自助法有效性），在有限样本下也表现优异，尤其适用于位置偏移类差异，且能提供标量检验之外的诊断信息。

Abstract: This paper proposes a two-sample homogeneity test based on entropic optimal transport (EOT) maps from a common reference distribution -- the uniform law on the unit ball. The test statistic is the squared $L^2$-distance between the two empirical EOT maps. For fixed entropic regularization parameter, we prove that the population map discrepancy is identifiable, derive a functional central limit theorem for the empirical map difference under the null, and establish the Gaussian quadratic-form null limit. We also prove consistency against fixed alternatives and characterize local asymptotic power under contiguous alternatives. A weighted multiplier bootstrap is proposed to calibrate the non-pivotal null distribution, and its validity is established. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed EOT-map test has reliable finite-sample size control and exhibits competitive power compared with other existing methods. The method is particularly powerful for location alternatives and, beyond a single scalar discrepancy, it provides additional diagnostic information on how the two distributions differ. Finally, a real data application concludes the paper.

</details>


### [170] [Correcting Variable Importance Scored by Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10770)
*Guancheng Zhou,Haiping Xu,Jason Liu,Donghui Yan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 随机森林中的变量重要性计算受变量间相关性干扰，相关变量常被低估或掩盖。本文提出基于条件相关性（以响应变量为条件）对变量进行分组，以消除无关相关变量的影响，并探索了两种高效计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林的变量重要性度量未考虑变量间的相关性，导致与多个变量高度相关的变量获得偏低的重要性指数，甚至被其他强相关变量完全掩盖（重要性接近零）。这严重影响了模型解释、变量选择等下游任务的可靠性，因此需要一种能校正相关性偏差的变量重要性评估方法。

Method: 提出基于条件相关性（以响应变量为条件）对变量进行分组的方法。具体包含两种计算高效的实现：(1) 逐变量分组——将每个目标变量与其所有相关变量分离；(2) 基于聚类的分组——根据变量间的成对条件相关性进行聚类，将变量划分为若干组。通过分组消除无关相关变量的干扰后，再计算校正后的变量重要性。

Result: 实验表明，两种分组方法均能对变量重要性进行合理的校正，有效缓解了因变量间相关性导致的重要性低估或掩盖问题。

Conclusion: 通过条件相关性分组来校正随机森林变量重要性是一种有效的策略，能够恢复被相关变量掩盖的真实变量重要性，提升模型解释和变量选择的准确性。

Abstract: Variable importance produced by Random Forests (RF) is used widely in statistical data analysis, and has played an important role in a variety of tasks such as assisting model interpretation, model selection and diagnosis, and cost-bounded learning etc. However, the calculation of variable importance in RF does not take into account of the correlations among variables, and variables that are correlated to many other variables tend to receive a lower importance index or being completely masked (i.e., with an importance index near zero) by other strongly correlated variables. To prevent influence from unwanted correlated variables in calculating variable importance, we propose to group variables by their conditional correlations (conditional on the response variable). We explore two computationally efficient options, with one grouping variables individually, and then separates the variable of interest from all correlated variables, while the other uses clustering to group variables according to their pair-wise conditional correlations. Our experiments show that both lead to sensible corrections to the importance of variables.

</details>


### [171] [Adressing Separation: A Firth-corrected Joint Model for Longitudinal and Time-to-event Data with an Application on Dropout from Vocational Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10866)
*Sophie Potts,Viola Deutscher,Elisabeth Bergherr*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出将Firth校正引入联合模型（纵向-生存数据）的频率学派估计中，以解决生存子模型中分类变量导致的数据分离问题；通过EM算法实现，模拟和实证表明Firth校正能有效减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型中的生存子模型在分类协变量存在分离（separation）问题时，传统最大似然估计会失效或产生严重偏差，这限制了联合模型在实际数据中的应用。需要一种方法来克服这一局限。

Method: 作者推导了Firth校正项所需的相关量（惩罚对数似然的修正项），并将其整合到联合模型参数估计的期望最大化（EM）算法框架中，实现了Firth校正的联合模型频率学派估计流程。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在存在分离问题的数据情境下，Firth校正估计能产生偏差更小的参数估计，且系数估计值趋近于无非分离情形下的结果。在德国职业培训满意度与辍学数据的实证应用中，Firth校正联合模型展示了其在真实分离数据中的优势，揭示了社会经济和培训特定因素对辍学风险的直接效应以及通过培训满意度的间接效应。

Conclusion: Firth校正有效扩展了联合模型的适用范围，使其能够处理包含分离问题的分类协变量；该研究也为德国职业培训辍学问题的文献做出了贡献，明确区分了各因素的直接和间接（通过满意度）作用路径。

Abstract: Joint Models for longitudinal and time-to-event data are frequently used to model endogenous longitudinal covariates alongside a time-to-event outcome. However, the model class borrows some limitations of the survival submodels, including the necessity for non-separation for each category of categorical covariates. We therefore incorporate Firth's correction into the frequentist estimation procedure of joint models in order to make the model class applicable in settings with separation cases. We derive the needed quantities for the correction term and implement it in the Expectation-Maximization Algorithm for the parameter estimation in joint models. Our simulation study shows, that in data situations with separation issues, the Firth-corrected estimation procedure yields less biased estimates and the respective coefficients approach the estimated values observed in the non-separation cases. The application on a data set on satisfaction with and dropouts from vocational training demonstrates the advantages of the Firth-corrected joint model in a real world data set with separation. The results add to the literature on dropout from vocational training in Germany by explicitly modeling direct effects of socioeconomic and training-specific factors on the risk of dropout as well as their indirect contribution via satisfaction with the training.

</details>


### [172] [A Functional Data Framework For Analyzing Shapes and Textures in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10969)
*Issam-Ali Moindjié*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于星形域假设的函数型数据分析方法，用于图像的简约表示，避免传统像素离散化带来的高维计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分析方法多依赖像素离散化，导致高维表示和沉重计算代价，亟需一种更简约的表示方法。

Method: 假设目标对象具有星形域内部结构，将图像轮廓和纹理视作连续随机函数的观测，从函数型数据分析视角进行建模；在极坐标下将图像表示为径向函数，实现降维与连续表示。

Result: 在真实监督图像分类问题上验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于星形域假设的函数型数据表示可以作为一种计算经济的图像分析替代方案，兼顾了统计建模的连续性与计算效率。

Abstract: Images represent objects characterized by contours and textures. From a statistical perspective these features can be defined as observations of continuous random functions. However, most existing approaches rely on pixel-based discretizations which lead to high-dimensional representations and heavy computational costs. In this note, we introduce an alternative more frugal representation. This representation assumes that the object has a star-shaped domain interior. Under this condition, we explore the analysis of images from a functional data analysis perspective. The proposed framework is illustrated on a real data supervised image classification problem.

</details>


### [173] [Empirical stratification for treatment effect heterogeneity with post-treatment variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11013)
*Chao Cheng,Rui Wang,Yichi Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于经验分层的弱假设框架,利用基线协变量预测潜在的后处理变量响应,构造经验分层,从而识别异质性治疗效应。该方法在标准因果假设下可识别,并与主分层方法建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后处理变量(PV)如治疗不依从、行为反应等会改变最终治疗效果,但现有方法对该类异质性研究工具有限。条件于PV的传统方法会引致内生选择偏倚,而主分层方法虽严谨但需强识别假设,亟需一种假设较弱的替代框架。

Method: 利用基线协变量预测潜在PV响应,构建"经验分数"(empirical scores),以此定义经验可及的分层,进而定义经验分层治疗效应(ETE)。在标准因果假设(如无未测混杂、正数性等)下ETE可识别。进一步引入投影ETE曲线,并基于有效影响函数(EIF)构建半参数推断估计量。

Result: 在标准因果假设下ETE可识别;在主可忽略性假设下,平均ETE可恢复主因果效应;即使该假设不成立,ETE仍具信息量。通过两个实际应用案例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提经验分层框架为研究后处理变量相关的效应异质性提供了一种假设较弱的实用工具,兼具可识别性与灵活性,较之主分层方法更易实施且解释性强。

Abstract: Post-treatment variables (PVs), such as treatment noncompliance, behavioral responses, intercurrent events, often modify the ultimate treatment effect on the primary outcome.
