However, existing methods provide limited tools for studying treatment effect heterogeneity with respect to PVs. Conventional heterogeneous treatment effect estimands condition on baseline covariates. However, similarly conditioning on the observed PV can induce endogenous selection bias for the treatment effect estimation. Principal stratification offers a rigorous framework for studying principal causal effects across principal strata, but principal strata are latent and their identification often requires stringent assumptions. This paper develops an assumption-lean empirical stratification framework for characterizing treatment effect heterogeneity with respect to PVs. We define empirical scores using the predicted potential PV responses based on baseline covariates, and use the empirical scores to construct empirically accessible subgroups. The resulting empirical-stratum treatment effects (ETEs) are identifiable under standard causal assumptions. We connect the proposed framework to principal stratification by showing that the average ETE recovers principal causal effects under the principal ignorability assumption, but remains informative under violations of this assumption. We further introduce projected ETE curves and develop efficient influence function-based estimators for the semiparametric inference. We illustrate the proposed framework with two real-world applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Business World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10044)
*Cecil Pang,Hiroki Sayama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出业务世界模型(BWM)的概念与架构，它是一种专为业务环境设计的世界模型，通过编码业务状态、动态、约束、目标和可行行动空间，使智能系统能够从高层战略目标出发进行自主规划、优化与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业AI应用多局限于自动化预定义任务，未能充分发挥AI在自主规划与战略执行层面的变革潜力。需要一个专门的框架来支撑从"基于指令的执行"向"目标驱动的规划与执行"的范式转变。

Method: 提出以业务语义为中心的BWM架构，核心包括：(1)将业务状态、动态和行动与关键业务实体关联的形式化定义；(2)融合语义数据表示、概率机器学习模型、确定性业务规则和显式行动空间，构建可执行的内部模拟器；(3)支持智能体模拟替代行动序列、估计未来业务结果并在不确定性下评估权衡。

Result: 建立了BWM的概念基础，展示了如何将语义数据、概率模型、确定性规则和行动空间等已有组件组织成一个面向业务计划的连贯可执行架构，支持反事实推理与规划。

Conclusion: BWM的核心贡献不在于提出全新的单一技术，而在于将现有组件系统性地整合为面向业务计划的内部模拟器，为从指令执行走向目标驱动的自主业务系统奠定了概念基础。

Abstract: Businesses are increasingly adopting AI-enabled tools to improve productivity, reduce costs, and enhance products and services. However, the transformative potential of AI extends beyond automating predefined tasks: it lies in enabling intelligent systems to plan, optimize, and execute business initiatives from high-level strategic objectives. This paper introduces the concept and architecture of a business world model (BWM), a world model specialized for business and organizational environments. Inspired by world models in artificial intelligence, cognitive science, and control theory, a BWM encodes business states, dynamics, constraints, objectives, and feasible action space to support autonomous decision-making. We propose a business-semantics-centric formulation in which business states, dynamics and actions are linked to key business entities. Within this framework, agents can simulate alternative action sequences, estimate their effects on future business outcomes, and evaluate trade-offs under uncertainty. The proposed architecture integrates semantic data representations, probabilistic machine learning models, deterministic business rules, and explicit action space into a coherent structure for planning and counterfactual reasoning. Although its individual components are not new, the contribution of BWM lies in organizing them as an executable internal simulator for business initiatives. This work establishes a conceptual foundation for autonomous business systems capable of moving from instruction-based execution toward goal-driven planning and execution.

</details>


### [175] [Deployment-Time Memorization in Foundation-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10062)
*Lei,Chen,Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao,Dalmo Cirne,Andy Olsen,Xu Chu,Zeke Miller,Alet Blanken,Amine Anoun,Jerry Ting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了部署时记忆（deployment-time memorization）这一概念，将智能体记忆建模为个性化效用与隐私风险之间的权衡前沿，通过个性化召回率（PR）、对抗提取率（AER）和遗忘残留分数（FRS）三个指标，系统扫描摘要激进程度、检索广度（k）和删除模式这三个设计维度，揭示压缩摘要可大幅降低提取风险但会引入删除保真度失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体正成为记住用户跨会话信息的长期运行系统，记忆化已从模型权重的静态属性转变为部署时的显式功能。现有工作要么关注参数化记忆，要么审计固定记忆配置，缺乏对记忆设计选择如何共同塑造个性化效用、提取风险和删除保真度的系统性分析。

Method: 在LongMemEval基准上，将智能体记忆形式化为隐私-效用前沿，定义PR（个性化召回率）和AER（对抗提取率）两个核心指标，并引入FRS（遗忘残留分数）量化已删除信息在派生记忆层中的可恢复性。系统扫描三个记忆设计维度：摘要激进程度、检索广度k值和删除模式（仅原始删除、全流水线清除、墓碑式删除）。

Result: 在Gemma 3 12B上，关键事实摘要使金丝雀提取降低76%，GPT-4o-mini上降低64%，同时几乎保持全部个性化召回率；一旦内容被压缩，增大k值不再恢复泄露。但相同压缩导致删除保真度失效：仅删除原始内容时，约20%的实例中派生摘要副本仍可恢复；仅全流水线清除或墓碑式删除才能将最差层级残留降至零。

Conclusion: 持久化智能体记忆必须作为一级记忆化机制进行评估——需要从智能体记住了什么、什么可被提取、以及什么能真正被擦除三个维度进行综合衡量。压缩摘要在隐私-效用前沿上表现优越，但会引入删除不彻底的结构性风险，需要完整删除管线的设计来弥补。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are increasingly long-lived systems that remember users across interactions, making memorization an explicit deployment-time function rather than solely a property of model weights. Existing work addresses parametric memorization or audits fixed memory configurations, but does not characterize how memory-design choices jointly shape personalization utility, extraction risk, and deletion fidelity. We study this surface as deployment-time memorization, formulating agent memory as a privacy-utility frontier measured by Personalization Recall (PR) and Adversarial Extraction Rate (AER), and sweeping three memory-design knobs: summarization aggressiveness, retrieval breadth (k), and deletion mode. We further introduce the Forgetting Residue Score (FRS) to quantify whether deleted information remains recoverable from derived memory tiers. On LongMemEval, key-fact summarization reduces canary extraction by 76% on Gemma 3 12B and 64% on GPT-4o-mini while preserving nearly all personalization recall; critically, once content is compressed away, increasing k no longer restores leakage. The same compression, however, induces a deletion-fidelity failure: raw-only deletion leaves derived summary copies recoverable in approximately 20% of instances, and only full-pipeline purge or tombstone redaction drives worst-tier residue to zero. Together, these results establish that persistent agent memory must be evaluated as a first-class memorization mechanism -- assessed by what it helps agents recall, what it makes extractable, and what it can truly erase.

</details>


### [176] [Exploratory Responsiveness and Adaptive Rigidity under AI-Assisted Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10086)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个AI辅助优化下的探索性适应理论框架，核心论点是：AI系统的长期适应效应关键取决于预测性辅助如何与系统自身的探索性响应能力相互作用。作者通过一个动力系统框架，刻画了认知、制度和技术系统在崎岖认知景观上的演化，识别出两种对立的机制：收敛性预测机制下AI替代探索导致适应性僵化，以及探索增强机制下AI放大探索性搜索。适应性响应能力本身决定了AI是替代还是增强探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI系统对认知、制度和技术系统长期适应性演化的影响，揭示一个关键悖论——AI预测辅助可能提升局部效率，但也可能削弱系统面对变化时的探索性响应能力，导致"局部高效但全局僵化"的适应陷阱。现有文献缺乏关注AI如何通过改变探索性响应来塑造长期适应动态的理论框架。

Method: 采用动力系统建模方法，将认知、制度和技术系统的演化置于"崎岖认知景观"上，该景观具有多重局部强化配置特征。模型的核心状态变量是"适应性响应能力"——衡量系统在变化条件下穿越陌生概念和制度轨迹的能力。通过分析"收敛性预测机制"与"探索增强机制"两种AI辅助模式对适应性响应能力的不同影响，推导出亚稳态捕获、滞后效应、过早收敛和探索-崩溃等动力学现象。

Result: （1）在收敛性预测机制下，AI替代探索性参与，降低适应性响应能力，引发亚稳态捕获、滞后效应、过早收敛和探索-崩溃动力学；（2）存在对立的探索增强机制，AI可放大探索性搜索和适应性移动性；（3）"有效替代参数"是响应能力依赖的：弱探索惯性的系统更易受探索替代影响，而高适应性响应的系统可利用AI扩展探索移动性；（4）AI的长期适应效应不仅取决于AI能力本身，还取决于制度结构、发展脉络和人机交互架构。

Conclusion: AI对系统长期适应性的影响是条件依赖且路径敏感的。核心洞见在于替代效应与增强效应之间的张力由系统自身的适应性响应能力所中介。这提示AI治理与设计不应仅关注AI能力提升，更需关注如何构建能够维持甚至增强人类/制度探索性响应能力的人机协作架构，以避免"效率陷阱"导致的长期适应性崩溃。

Abstract: This paper develops a theory of exploratory adaptation under AI-assisted optimization. The central argument is that the long-run adaptive effects of AI systems depend critically on how predictive assistance interacts with exploratory responsiveness itself. We formalize this mechanism using a dynamical framework in which cognitive, institutional, and technological systems evolve over rugged epistemic landscapes characterized by multiple locally reinforced configurations. A central state variable in the model is adaptive responsiveness, which measures the capacity of a system to traverse unfamiliar conceptual and institutional trajectories under changing conditions. Under convergent predictive regimes, AI systems substitute for exploratory engagement, reducing adaptive responsiveness and generating metastable trapping, hysteresis, premature convergence, and exploration-collapse dynamics in which systems become locally efficient but globally rigid. The framework also identifies contrasting exploration-enhancing regimes in which AI systems amplify exploratory search, conceptual traversal, and adaptive mobility. The effective substitution parameter is therefore responsiveness-dependent: systems possessing weak exploratory routines are more vulnerable to exploratory substitution, whereas systems already possessing high adaptive responsiveness may use AI assistance to expand exploratory mobility across rugged landscapes. The long-run adaptive effects of AI consequently depend not only on AI capability itself, but also on institutional structure, developmental context, and the architecture of human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [177] [Predictive Assistance and the Temporal Dynamics of Exploratory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10094)
*Balaraju Battu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何动力学框架，将预测性AI辅助建模为一种"外源性探索压缩"过程。该过程在自发性探索尚未充分展开之前就稳定了注意力轨迹，从而导致探索响应性衰减、曲率积累与滞后效应，以及关键期依赖的发育结果——早期干预可能不可逆地收窄策略空间的探索范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典认知理论将问题解决视为通过结构化问题空间的探索性搜索，而预测性AI系统引入了一种新机制：答案在内部搜索展开之前就被提供，即"先稳定、后探索"。作者试图从理论上理解这种外部预测稳定化如何重塑探索性认知的几何结构，以及其对学习、适应和认知发展的深远影响。

Method: 构建了一个几何动力学框架，注意力（attention）在一个由稳定化漂移、内源性探索扰动和响应性门控学习共同塑造的策略景观上演化。预测性辅助被形式化为"外源性探索压缩"——在自发性探索拓宽策略空间可及区域之前，先压缩并稳定注意力的运动轨迹。通过该框架分析系统的动力学行为，包括响应性衰减、曲率动力学和发育时序效应。

Result: 得出三个主要结果：（1）持续的预测性稳定化即使在探索变异仍存在的情况下，也会通过削弱内源性扰动的有效影响力来降低探索响应性；（2）曲率在稳定化期间积累、在辅助撤除后非对称松弛，产生滞后效应和探索机动性延迟恢复；（3）发育结果关键依赖于稳定化的时机——早期干预在广泛的表征多样化发生之前就收窄了未来的探索遍历范围。

Conclusion: 该框架对探索熵、过早收敛和预测性稳定化后的延迟恢复等现象给出了可检验的实证预测。更广泛而言，预测性系统可能不仅影响问题解决的效率，更可能从根本上重塑探索性认知本身的几何结构。

Abstract: Classical theories of cognition describe problem solving as exploratory search through structured problem spaces in which repeated interaction gradually compresses search into efficient representational structures. Predictive artificial intelligence systems introduce a distinct regime in which stabilization may occur before exploratory diversification unfolds, supplying solutions and decision trajectories prior to internally generated search. This paper develops a geometric dynamical framework in which attention evolves over a landscape of strategies shaped by stabilizing drift, endogenous exploratory perturbation, and responsiveness-gated learning. Predictive assistance is modeled as a process of exogenous exploratory compression that stabilizes trajectories before self-generated exploration broadens the accessible regions of strategy space. The framework yields three main results. First, sustained predictive stabilization reduces exploratory responsiveness by attenuating the effective influence of intrinsic perturbations even when exploratory variability remains present. Second, curvature accumulates and relaxes asymmetrically, producing hysteresis and delayed recovery of exploratory mobility after assistance withdrawal. Third, developmental outcomes depend critically on the timing of stabilization, with early intervention narrowing future exploratory traversal before broad representational diversification has occurred. The framework generates empirically testable predictions concerning exploratory entropy, premature convergence, and delayed recovery following predictive stabilization. More broadly, the results suggest that predictive systems may reshape the geometry of exploratory cognition itself.

</details>


### [178] [From Senses to Decisions: The Information Flow of Auditory and Visual Perception in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10147)
*Wish Suharitdamrong,Muhammad Awais,Xiatian Zhu,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统揭示了音视频大语言模型（AVLLMs）内部音频与视觉信息的流动路径：在音视频视频输入下，信息沿序列通路流动，各模态贡献与任务需求成比例；在多交错音视频项输入下，信息流转向并行分流。此外，一旦信息传递给LLM后，音视频等token可被丢弃而对预测影响极小，甚至略有提升，从而实现更高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频大语言模型（MLLMs）虽能"听"和"看"，但音频和视觉信号在网络内部如何传输、路由并整合以形成最终答案，目前仍缺乏系统理解。随着AVLLMs在实际应用中的日益普及，揭示其内部信息流动机制对于可解释性、模型设计和推理效率的提升至关重要。

Method: 作者以Qwen2.5-Omni和Video-SALMONN2 Plus（3B和7B两个尺度）为研究对象，针对两种输入配置——（1）音视频视频（audio-visual video）和（2）多交错音视频项（multiple interleaved audio-visual items），追踪AVLLMs内部音频与视觉token的路由、利用和整合方式。通过分析信息流动路径，并实验验证不同token类型在信息传递后可丢弃对预测的影响。

Result: （1）对于音视频视频，AVLLMs遵循VLMs和VideoLLMs已建立的序列信息流路径，音频和视觉贡献沿此路径按任务对各模态的依赖比例流动；（2）在多交错音视频项设置中，信息流转向不同的并行流；（3）音视频及其他类型token在信息传递给LLM后可被丢弃，对模型预测影响极小甚至略有提升，该结论在多个任务和数据集上具有泛化性，可支持更高效推理。

Conclusion: 该研究首次完整描绘了AVLLMs内部如何协调声音与视觉信息的图景，为音视频及更广泛MLLMs的可解释性、模型设计和推理效率的下一波进展奠定了基础，并提出了关于这些流动结构为何涌现的假说。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can listen and see, but how do audio and visual signals actually travel through the network to shape an answer? Despite their growing role in research and real-world applications, the internal pathways through which audio and visual tokens influence the final prediction remain poorly understood. In this study, we examine audio-visual information flow inside Audio-Visual Large Language Models (AVLLMs), tracing how AVLLMs route, utilize, and integrate audio and visual information across two input configurations, audio-visual video and multiple interleaved audio-visual items. We find that for audio-visual video, AVLLMs follow the sequential information flow pathway established for VLMs and VideoLLMs, with audio and visual contribution flowing along this pathway in proportion to the task's reliance on each modality. In settings with multiple interleaved audio-visual items, this routing shifts to different parallel streams. Furthermore, we demonstrate that audio-visual and other token types can be discarded once their information is transferred to LLM, with minimal impact on the model's prediction or even slight improvement, generalizing across multiple tasks and datasets, enabling more efficient inference. These findings hold across multiple models and scales, Qwen2.5-Omni and Video-SALMONN2 Plus at 3B and 7B scales, leading to hypotheses on why these flow structures emerge. Together, these results deliver the first coherent picture of how AVLLMs orchestrate sound and sight inside the network and lay the groundwork for the next wave of interpretability, design, and efficiency advances in audio-visual and broader MLLMs.

</details>


### [179] [Less Context, Better Agents: Efficient Context Engineering for Long-Horizon Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10209)
*Abhilasha Lodha,Mahsa Pahlavikhah Varnosfaderani,Abir Chakraborty,Abhinav Mithal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将大型语言模型部署为企业自主代理时，冗长的工具响应会导致上下文溢出、状态陈旧和高推理成本。本文在微软Dynamics 365财务与运营的酒店费用自动化项化任务中，对比四种上下文工程策略，发现"最近5次工具调用+自动摘要"方案最优：完成率达91.6%，金额项化率99.64%，同时token消耗和运行时间大幅低于全历史保留方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统中部署LLM代理时，工具返回的冗长响应会造成三大问题：上下文窗口溢出、基于过期状态的错误决策（stale-state errors）以及高昂的推理成本。当前缺乏系统性的上下文工程策略对比研究来指导如何在企业工具调用工作流中高效管理上下文。

Method: 基于Microsoft Dynamics 365 Finance and Operations的真实环境，使用Model Context Protocol (MCP)工具，构建50个酒店费用任务基准；评估GPT-5的四种配置：(1)无用户模型、(2)全量对话历史、(3)仅保留最近5次工具调用/响应对、(4)带自动摘要的裁剪策略；每种配置独立运行5次取均值；辅以置信区间、效应量分析、不同裁剪/摘要窗口灵敏度分析、失败分析、三类费用类型结果及Claude Sonnet 4.5跨模型验证。

Result: 无用户模型基线完成率仅8.0%；全上下文保留完成率提升至71.0%，但消耗1,480,996 token和14.56小时；最近5次裁剪完成率79.0%，token降至535,274，时间降至5.39小时；摘要方案最优：完成率91.6%、金额项化率99.64%，仅用553,374 token和5.79小时。

Conclusion: 在该类企业工具调用工作流中，选择性保留近期工具交互并辅以紧凑摘要，在可靠性和效率上均优于全历史保留策略，为上下文工程在企业LLM代理部署中提供了明确的实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as autonomous agents for enterprise workflows face a key challenge: verbose tool responses from enterprise systems can cause context overflow, stale-state errors, and high inference cost. We study this problem in automated expense itemization in Microsoft Dynamics 365 Finance and Operations using Model Context Protocol tools. We evaluate four GPT-5 configurations on a 50-task hotel expense benchmark: no user model, full conversation history, context pruned to the last 5 tool call/response pairs, and pruning with automated summarization. Results are averaged across 5 independent runs, with the user model held constant for the context-engineering comparison. The no-user-model baseline achieves only 8.0% complete itemization. Full-context retention improves completion to 71.0%, but consumes 1,480,996 tokens and 14.56 hours per benchmark. Pruning to the last 5 tool calls improves completion to 79.0% while reducing token use to 535,274 and runtime to 5.39 hours. Adding summarization achieves the best result: 91.6% complete itemization and 99.64% average amount itemized, with 553,374 tokens and 5.79 hours. We further report confidence intervals, effect-size analysis, sensitivity over pruning and summary windows, failure analysis, results across five expense types grouped into three categories, and cross-model evidence with Claude Sonnet 4.5. These results show that, for this class of enterprise tool-use workflow, selective retention of recent tool interactions plus compact summarization can improve both reliability and efficiency compared with full-history retention.

</details>


### [180] [Minimalist Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10237)
*Leonardo Trujillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出极小主义遗传编程（MGP），用源自语言学极小主义纲领的二元合并算子MERGE替代传统GP的进化搜索，将程序归纳重新定义为语法推导任务，在符号回归基准上显著优于标准GP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GP将程序归纳视为进化搜索问题，存在膨胀（bloat）等固有问题。作者认为可以借鉴乔姆斯基极小主义纲领中"句法是连接心智系统的最优方案"这一思想，将程序归纳重新表述为语法推导过程，从根本上规避进化搜索带来的弊端。

Method: 提出MGP算法，核心是用极小主义中的二元集合形成算子MERGE，以马尔可夫过程增量式地构建复杂语法结构。算法首先发现符号表达式的原子构建块，然后通过MERGE算子逐步组合这些构建块，形成层次化的语法树模型。

Result: 在已知对标准GP因膨胀问题而难以求解的符号回归任务上，当选择了合适的原子语法对象词库时，MGP能够一致地生成精确的真实模型，而标准GP在相同任务上表现挣扎。

Conclusion: 极小主义语言学理论为程序归纳问题提供了有价值的视角，MGP展现的潜力表明这一方向值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Genetic programming (GP) is based on two important insights. First, that any learning task can fundamentally be posed as a program induction problem, where the goal is to construct a symbolic hierarchical model that is expressed as a syntax tree. Second, to pose this task as a search problem, and use evolution to locate the desired model. Since it was proposed, GP has produced notable results in a wide range of tasks and problem domains. This work presents an alternative view by modifying the second core insight of GP, posing the problem as a syntactic derivation task instead. In particular, this paper presents Minimalist Genetic Programming (MGP), an algorithm that like GP is biologically inspired, but instead of evolution it takes inspiration from the Minimalist Program to human language, in which syntax is understood as an optimal solution to the problem of linking two other mental systems. In minimalism, the core computational process is a binary set formation operator called $MERGE$, than can be used to incrementally construct complex syntactic structures using a simple Markovian process. MGP is able to discover the core building blocks of the symbolic expressions, and to incrementally combined them using $MERGE$. The proposed system is benchmarked on symbolic regression tasks that are known to be difficult to solve with standard GP systems because of the propensity for bloat. Results show that when a proper lexicon of atomic syntactic objects are chosen, MGP is able to consistently produce the exact ground truth model on a set of symbolic regression where standard GP struggles to do the same. The insights provided by minimalism are shown to be relevant to the problem of program induction, and should be explored further based on the potential exhibited by MGP in this work.

</details>


### [181] [Regimes: An Auditable, Held-Out-Gated Improvement Loop Demonstrated on LongMemEval with ActiveGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10241)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件溯源（event-sourced）的智能体运行时ActiveGraph，将受控的自主改进循环变为一等工作流。通过Regimes改进循环，对LongMemEval-S上的失败进行诊断、修复和门控验证，在多个held-out分片上实现了准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主改进循环难以信任，因为改进过程通常是外挂脚手架：失败无日志记录、诊断无法复现、promote-or-discard决策存储在外部数据库而非智能体自身历史中。需要一种将受控改进内化为智能体一等工作流的机制。

Method: 基于ActiveGraph事件溯源运行时：智能体状态是仅追加事件日志的确定性投影，失败被记录，运行可精确复现，候选补丁作用于类型化管道接缝（typed pipeline seams），门控可审计，每次promote/discard本身也是事件。Regimes循环：诊断失败评估→在管道点提出修复→经静态检查、沙箱执行、样本内评估和held-out验证后才promote。该循环是目标无关的（target-agnostic），通过通用接口运行于不同任务。

Result: 在LongMemEval-S上，主导失败模式是reconciliation（调和）而非retrieval（检索）：证据已在上下文中但阅读器回答错误。五个held-out分片中，Regimes发现的reader-prompt修复在四个分片上将最终held-out准确率提升+0.05至+0.10，一个分片提升+0.01；两个分片单独显著。聚合计数仅具描述性，因为分片共享500题的题库。

Conclusion: 持久贡献包括：ActiveGraph作为可审计基底使受控改进循环可行；held-out门控改进循环；将失败按regime分类路由到管道位置的分类法（其相对于无路由基线的边际价值是主要开放问题）；以及prompt作为发现探针的假设。

Abstract: Autonomous improvement loops are hard to trust because the improvement process is usually external scaffolding bolted onto the agent: failures go unlogged, diagnoses cannot be replayed, and promote-or-discard decisions land in a side database rather than the agent's own history. We show that an event-sourced agent runtime removes that friction and turns controlled improvement into a first-class workflow. When the agent's state is a deterministic projection of an append-only event log, failures are recorded, a run replays exactly from its log, candidate patches scope to typed pipeline seams, gates are auditable, and every promotion or discard is itself an event. We demonstrate this with Regimes, a loop on the ActiveGraph runtime that diagnoses failed evaluations, proposes a repair at a pipeline point, and promotes it only after static checks, sandbox execution, in-sample evaluation, and held-out validation. The loop is target-agnostic: the same control flow runs against different tasks through a common interface. On LongMemEval-S the dominant failure is not retrieval but reconciliation: the evidence is already in the assembled context, yet the reader answers incorrectly. Across five seeded held-out splits, Regimes discovers reader-prompt repairs that improve final held-out accuracy by +0.05 to +0.10 in four splits and +0.01 in one over-promotion split; two splits are individually significant (seed 5 unadjusted for its sequential promotion structure), and the pooled count is descriptive only, since the splits share one 500-question pool. The durable contributions are ActiveGraph as an auditable substrate that makes controlled improvement loops tractable, the held-out-gated loop it supports, the failure-regime taxonomy routing each failure to a pipeline location (whose marginal value over an unrouted baseline is the primary open question), and the prompt-as-discovery-probe hypothesis.

</details>


### [182] [RealMath-Eval: Why SOTA Judges Struggle with Real Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10254)
*Yiteng Mao,Kenan Xu,Yijia Lyu,Wenhao Li,Jianlong Chen,Xiangfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RealMath-Eval基准测试，包含224份真实高中生数学考试作答，发现当前LLM在评估真实学生推理时表现不佳（MSE≈2.96），与评估合成数据（MSE≈1.17）之间存在显著的"评估鸿沟"。分析表明，合成错误呈现结构坍缩，而人类错误空间更具多样性，且人类推理的信息论惊奇度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在求解数学题上接近完美，但鲜有研究考察其评估真实学生多样化推理过程的能力。现有评估管线过度依赖合成数据，可能无法捕捉真实学生推理的丰富性。

Method: 构建RealMath-Eval基准（224份真实高中考试作答，经严格标注）；将LLM评判器在真实数据与合成LLM生成解答上的表现进行对比；通过语义嵌入分析错误空间结构；利用生成概率探测推理步骤的信息论惊奇度；测试表层风格迁移对弥合鸿沟的效果。

Result: 最先进LLM评判器在真实学生作答上MSE高达2.96，而在合成数据上仅为1.17。合成错误坍缩为可预测的低维线性子空间，人类错误空间更加多样化。人类推理步骤的生成概率显著更低（信息论惊奇度更高），表明这些推理转换对当前模型而言更具分布外特性。表层风格迁移无法弥合这一鸿沟。

Conclusion: 当前依赖合成数据的LLM评估管线可能不足以捕捉真实学生数学推理的多样性，揭示了从"求解"到"评估"的能力迁移存在根本性挑战，呼吁开发更加真实、多样化的评估基准与方法。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved near-perfect performance in \emph{solving} high-school mathematics, their ability to \emph{evaluate} the diverse reasoning processes of real human students remains under-examined. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{RealMath-Eval}, a rigorously annotated benchmark of 224 real-world exam responses from high schools. Our initial evaluation reveals that even state-of-the-art LLM judges struggle significantly on this task, exhibiting a high Mean Squared Error ($\sim$2.96) against expert human grading. To probe a plausible explanation, we contrast this performance with a control setting where the same judges evaluate synthetic LLM-generated solutions. We identify a stark ``Evaluation Gap'': judges are considerably more accurate and consistent on synthetic text (MSE $\sim$1.17) but struggle to generalize to authentic student reasoning. Through semantic embedding analysis, we find that synthetic errors suffer from a ``structural collapse'' into predictable, low-dimensional linear subspaces, whereas human errors form a more diverse error space. Furthermore, generative probability probes suggest that human reasoning involves significantly higher information-theoretic surprisal, indicating that student reasoning transitions are more out-of-distribution for current models. Finally, we find that surface-level style transfer fails to close this gap. Our findings suggest that current LLM evaluation pipelines relying heavily on synthetic data may not adequately capture the diversity of authentic student mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [183] [Supervised Fine-tuning with Synthetic Rationale Data Hurts Real-World Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10279)
*Buxin Su,Bingxuan Li,Cheng Qian,Yiwei Wang,Jin Jin,Bingxin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模对照实验发现，在阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆症（ADRD）预测任务中，基于推理链的监督微调非但未提升性能，反而持续显著损害预测表现，根本原因在于叙事合理性（narrative plausibility）与判别优化（discriminative optimization）之间的结构性冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前普遍假设认为，使用合成推理链数据进行监督微调能教会模型"为何预测"，从而提升临床预测性能。但该假设缺乏严格验证，尤其在高风险临床预测场景下，推理链监督的实际效果尚不明确。

Method: 作者基于纵向健康史数据，以五年期ADRD预测为任务，设计了504种配置的大规模对照实验。实验覆盖不同模型家族、数据规模及推理导向基座模型，对比仅标签微调与推理链监督微调的性能差异。同时进行人类专家评估以验证推理链质量，并通过少样本实验检验相同推理链在推理时作为示例的效果。

Result: 推理链SFT在所有504种配置中一致且显著地降低了预测性能；性能下降无法归因于推理链质量差（专家确认其医学准确且忠实于患者特异性证据）；相同推理链作为推理时的few-shot示例可提升性能，但作为训练目标时却有害。

Conclusion: 失败根因是叙事合理性与判别优化之间的结构性冲突：模型在生成连贯推理时所学到的模式，与直接优化判别准确率所需的模式存在根本矛盾。这提示需更精确地理解推理链监督何时有效、何时无效，以指导高风险临床预测中语言模型的负责任开发。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning with synthetic rationale data is widely assumed to improve language model performance on clinical prediction tasks by teaching models not just what to predict but why. We test this assumption on five-year Alzheimer's disease and related dementias (ADRD) prediction from longitudinal health histories. Across a large-scale controlled experiment of 504 configurations, we find that rationale-based SFT consistently and substantially hurts prediction performance relative to label-only fine-tuning. The degradation persists across model families and data scales, and is not resolved by using a reasoning-oriented base model. Crucially, the failure is not explained by poor rationale quality: human expert annotation confirms that the generated rationales are medically accurate and faithfully grounded in patient-specific evidence, and few-shot experiments show that the same rationales improve performance when used as inference-time demonstrations rather than training targets. We identify the root cause as a structural conflict between narrative plausibility and discriminative optimization. We hope our work paves the path toward a more precise understanding of when and how rationale-based supervision helps and when it does not, guiding the responsible development of language models for high-stakes clinical prediction.

</details>


### [184] [Sim2Schedule: A Simulator-Guided LLM Framework for Autonomous Open-Pit Mine Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10286)
*Mustavi Ibne Masum,Thiago Eustaquio Alves de Oliveira,Mahzabeen Emu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模拟器驱动的大语言模型（LLM）露天矿调度框架，LLM作为自主决策Agent，在定制模拟器约束下零样本生成排产方案，无需云端推理或微调。实验表明，该框架可恢复MILP最优净现值（NPV）的94%-99%，且计算时间线性增长，为大规模型工业调度提供了可扩展的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MILP方法虽能提供数学最优解，但计算复杂度呈指数级增长且无法实时适应动态工业环境，限制了其在露天矿调度中的实际部署。因此需要一种兼具优化质量与计算可扩展性的替代方案。

Method: 提出模拟器驱动的LLM调度框架：(1) LLM作为自主决策Agent，每一步由定制模拟器引导；(2) 模拟器将地质力学优先级、采掘-加工耦合关系及动态产能约束直接编码进动作生成机制；(3) 完全零样本运行，在封闭数据安全环境中生成完整的、可解释的采掘与加工调度；(4) 同时提出一种融入实际运营与地质力学约束的新型MILP公式作为性能基准。

Result: 在不同规模和时段的矿山实例上评估，LLM框架恢复了MILP最优NPV的94%-99%，且计算时间呈线性增长，而非MILP的指数增长。

Conclusion: 模拟器约束的LLM Agent可作为经典优化的实用且可扩展的替代方案，适用于复杂操作约束下的长周期工业调度场景。

Abstract: Open-pit mine scheduling is a critical process for maximizing economic return under complex geotechnical and operational constraints. While Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) provides mathematically optimal baselines, its exponential computational complexity and inability to adapt in real time limit its practical deployment in dynamic industrial environments. This work introduces a simulator-driven Large Language Model (LLM) scheduling framework in which the LLM acts as an autonomous decision-making agent, guided at each step by a custom simulator that encodes geotechnical precedence, extraction-processing coupling, and dynamic capacity constraints directly into the action generation mechanism. Operating entirely zero-shot within a closed, data-secure environment, the framework produces complete, interpretable extraction and processing schedules without cloud-based inference, domain-specific fine-tuning, or retraining. To provide a trustworthy performance benchmark, a novel MILP formulation is developed that incorporates realistic operational and geotechnical constraints. Evaluated across mining instances of varying scale and time periods, the LLM-based framework recovers between 94\% and 99\% of the MILP optimal NPV while scaling linearly in computation time. These results position simulator-constrained LLM agents as a practical and scalable alternative to classical optimization for long-horizon industrial scheduling under complex operational constraints.

</details>


### [185] [From Context-Aware to Conflict-Aware: Generalizing Contrastive Decoding for Knowledge Conflict in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10298)
*Runze Jiang,Taiqiang Wu,Yan Wang,Bingyu Zhu,Longtao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突感知解码范式，通过自适应机制路由在参数先验与检索上下文之间动态分配权重，解决了传统上下文感知解码在上下文错误时覆盖正确先验的可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比解码方法遵循"上下文感知"范式，单向放大上下文而覆盖参数先验，当检索到的外部上下文存在错误时，会错误地覆盖模型原本正确的参数化知识。需要一种能根据冲突信号动态判断上下文是否可信的新范式。

Method: （1）将先验与上下文logits的仿射组合推广为幂族（power family），揭示其固有的"区域不对称性"：外推在上下文错误时无限放大错误，内插在上下文正确时纠正不足；（2）提出TriState-Bench评估协议，校准每模型先验知识，衡量纠正、抵抗、一致三种冲突状态；（3）提出自适应区域路由（ARR），在每一步根据冲突信号在插值和外推区域间动态路由。

Result: ARR将抵抗（Resistance）的EM指标从不足6提升至16-33，同时不牺牲纠正（Correction）和一致（Agreement）性能。

Conclusion: 冲突感知范式通过动态路由机制有效解决了区域不对称性问题，在上下文错误和正确两种情况下均表现出鲁棒性，显著优于现有的上下文感知解码方法。

Abstract: When large language models generate from retrieved or augmented contexts, conflicts between external context and parametric priors remain a central reliability bottleneck. Existing contrastive decoding methods follow a \emph{context-aware} paradigm that unilaterally amplifies context over parametric priors, overwriting correct priors when the context is erroneous. We generalize this to the \textbf{conflict-aware} paradigm that dynamically allocates authority between prior and context based on conflict signals, rather than presupposing context trustworthiness. We show that the affine combination of prior and context logits yields a \textbf{power family} with an inherent \textbf{regime asymmetry}: extrapolation amplifies errors unboundedly when the prior is correct, interpolation under-corrects when the context is correct, and no static regime covers both. Existing contrastive decoding methods are instances of this family, mostly extrapolative. To evaluate both conflict directions, we propose TriState-Bench, a model-aware evaluation protocol that calibrates per-model prior knowledge to measure three conflict states: correction, resistance, and agreement. To resolve the asymmetry, we propose Adaptive Regime Routing (ARR), which routes between regimes at each step, lifting resistance EM from below 6 to 16--33 without sacrificing correction or agreement. Our code is available at https://github.com/keith-Jiang/conflict-aware-decoding.

</details>


### [186] [What Spatial Memory Must Store: Occlusion as the Test for Language-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10299)
*Doeon Kwon,Junho Bang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了语言智能体"记忆宫殿"系统中几何信息与文本信息在记忆存储与召回中的角色分离。通过三项预注册实验，作者证明：(1) 空间邻近性不应简单线性混合到召回排序中，几何引导的加权方式显著优于默认混合；(2) 记忆召回与可见性必须解耦——回溯是遮挡盲的，而可见性需要基于几何的射线检测；(3) 提出的DDA可见性谓词在实时系统中得到验证，成功消除误可见问题。核心贡献在于将空间记忆的存储内容与读取方式进行测量与隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体的"记忆宫殿"系统默认将空间邻近性与近因性、重要性线性混合用于记忆召回，但这一直觉从未被严格检验。作者旨在实证验证几何信息在记忆召回中的实际作用，以及"记忆回溯"与"可见性感知"这两个常被混淆的概念是否应当分离。

Method: 研究采用三项预注册实验：(1) 冻结的召回实验，对比默认线性混合与几何引导加权策略在Hit@5上的表现；(2) 基于射线-体素DDA（数字微分分析器）的可见性谓词，利用智能体已有的视线射线检测目标是否被遮挡，并与纯文本和纯视锥方法对比；(3) 实时系统验证（SPMEM-OCC-LIVE-v1），在8个脚本化世界、96个遮挡目标上进行自动化评分，并采用精确McNemar检验进行统计推断。

Result: 实验一：默认混合策略的ΔHit@5为-0.0375（Wilcoxon p=0.306），与位置盲基线无差异；几何引导策略为+0.3208（p<10⁻¹⁵），显著胜出。实验二：文本和纯视锥对849个墙后目标命中率均为0.000，而视锥+DDA达到0.982（精确McNemar p<10⁻⁶）；坐标召回对近似重复位置的分辨能力为1.000 vs 余弦空值0.533。实验三：误可见率从1.000降至0.000（pooled exact McNemar p=2.5×10⁻²⁹），同时发现并修复了一个中继锚点缺陷。

Conclusion: 几何信息在空间查询主导的召回中必须处于主导地位；记忆召回与可见性感知是两个独立且需要分离的功能——记忆存储的是"什么在哪里"，而可见性判断需要基于实时几何的遮挡计算。遮挡需要几何虽近乎同义反复，但贡献在于对这两个概念进行了精确的测量与隔离，明确了空间记忆系统应存储什么以及如何读取。

Abstract: Language-agent "memory palace" systems anchor each memory to a world coordinate, on the intuition that geometry adds something text cannot. We make that intuition testable and report three results. First, the memory-palace default of folding spatial proximity into a linear blend beside recency and importance does not help and can hurt: in a pre-registered recall experiment the shipped blend fails its own frozen test (mean Delta-Hit@5 -0.0375, Wilcoxon p=0.306), sitting at a position-blind baseline, while a geometry-led weighting wins decisively (+0.3208, p<10^-15): geometry must lead recall when the query regime is spatial. Second, memory recall and visibility must be separated: recall is occlusion-blind by design (you correctly remember the next room behind a wall), while visibility is a perception predicate over stored geometry that the live system never computed. A one-line ray-versus-voxel digital differential analyzer (DDA), re-pointed from the gaze ray the agent already casts, supplies it: text and the live FoV cone both score 0.000 on 849 behind-wall targets while cone-plus-DDA reaches 0.982 (exact McNemar p<10^-6); coordinate recall separately resolves near-duplicate locations a cosine null cannot (1.000 vs 0.533, n=150). Third, the visibility predicate is confirmed live under a git-committed pre-registration (SPMEM-OCC-LIVE-v1: eight scripted worlds, automated oracle scoring, 96 behind-wall targets, false-visible 1.000->0.000, pooled exact McNemar p=2.5x10^-29), a run that surfaced and fixed a real relay anchor defect. We concede that occlusion-needs-geometry is near-tautological; the contribution is the measurement and isolation, separating what spatial memory must store from how it is read. These pilots power a frozen confirmatory study (SPMEM-ZERO-REAL-PREREG-v1); the full human-authored multi-world study with blind raters remains future work.

</details>


### [187] [Mobility Anomaly Generation using LLM-Driven Behavior with Kinematic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10314)
*Yueyang Liu,Joon-Seok Kim,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对人类轨迹异常检测领域缺乏带标注真实数据集的核心瓶颈，提出了一种端到端的生成框架，利用大语言模型智能体在基线仿真轨迹上注入语义行为异常，并通过地图约束路由重建和上下文感知空间噪声模型，大规模合成具有真实感的异常轨迹数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类轨迹异常研究受限于带标注的真实数据集极度匮乏。根本原因在于：(1) 异常事件本身统计稀有，传统观测方法不可行；(2) 大规模移动数据采集面临高昂成本和严格隐私法规的双重瓶颈。现有的真实与仿真轨迹数据集仅捕获正常移动模式，缺乏异常标注，严重制约了空间数据挖掘的实证研究。

Method: 提出端到端生成框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于LLM智能体的语义异常注入——在基线仿真轨迹上系统性地注入两类行为异常：分布外的不规则签到（irregular out-of-distribution check-ins）和跳过的例行访问（skipped routine visits）；(2) 地图约束路由重建——对LLM智能体修改后的停留点之间的物理转移进行重新计算，确保空间有效性；(3) 上下文感知空间噪声模型——以环境和位置特定变量为参数，模拟异构GPS传感器退化，缩小仿真到真实的差距。

Result: 该框架能够大规模合成具有标注真实值的、物理可行的、包含语义行为异常的人类轨迹数据，有效弥补了仿真移动数据与复杂真实世界物理约束之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM智能体、地图约束路由与上下文感知噪声建模相结合，该框架为人类轨迹异常检测领域提供了一个可扩展的、带标注真实值的基准数据集生成方案，有望推动该领域的实证研究突破数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Although the study of human trajectory anomalies is critical for advancing spatial data mining, empirical research remains severely hindered by a pervasive lack of ground-truth datasets. Despite the availability of several real-world and simulated human trajectory collections, these datasets exclusively capture normal mobility patterns and lack annotated anomalies. This specific scarcity is fundamentally driven by the inherent statistical rarity of anomalous events, precluding the feasibility of conventional observational methods. Compounding this challenge, the systematic acquisition of large-scale mobility data is strictly bottlenecked by prohibitive costs and stringent privacy regulations. To overcome these fundamental limitations and establish a reliable human trajectory anomalies dataset with annotated ground truth, we introduce a novel, end-to-end generative framework designed to synthesize realistic trajectory anomalies at scale. Our architecture bridges the gap between purely synthetic mobility data and complex real-world physical constraints by operating directly on baseline simulated trajectories. We employ Large Language Model (LLM) agents to systematically inject semantically meaningful behavioral anomalies such as irregular out-of-distribution check-ins and skipped routine visits. To ensure rigorous spatial validity, the system leverages map-constrained routing reconstruction to recalculate the physical transitions between these LLM agent-modified staypoints. Moreover, to narrow the simulation-to-reality gap, we augment the resulting trajectories with a context-aware spatial noise model, parameterized by environmental and location-specific variables, to accurately emulate heterogeneous GPS sensor degradation.

</details>


### [188] [Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Visual Feedback: Bridging Code and Visual Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10334)
*Haoyu Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual-SDPO，一种面向代码生成视觉产物（图表、网页、幻灯片）的视觉反馈自蒸馏策略优化框架。核心创新在于将渲染后的视觉缺陷作为特权信息，通过教师模型蒸馏给学生模型，并引入视觉定位的代码信用加权机制，使监督信号精准聚焦于导致缺陷的代码语句。在多个基准上，该方法相比零样本基线提升超10个绝对点，相比GRPO提升至少2.4个点，且无推理阶段额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成大模型在生成图表、网页、幻灯片等视觉产物时，采用"先写代码再渲染"的非可微流程，代码虽可执行但渲染结果常出现元素重叠、文本裁剪、对齐错误、对比度不足、溢出等视觉缺陷。现有方法缺乏对渲染视觉反馈的有效利用，作者希望将渲染后的视觉反馈蒸馏回代码生成过程，且监督信号能精准定位到产生缺陷的具体代码行。

Method: 提出Visual-SDPO框架，包含两大核心机制：(1) 视觉反馈自蒸馏——将渲染后的视觉反馈作为教师模型的"特权上下文"，通过权重共享的教师-学生架构，将视觉质量信息蒸馏给代码生成学生模型；(2) 视觉定位代码信用加权(Visual-Grounded Code Credit Weighting)——检测渲染图像中的视觉缺陷，反向追溯至负责该受影响元素的代码语句，并在这些语句上放大蒸馏信号。此外，序列级GRPO项作为补充，奖励可执行且视觉质量高的生成结果，而执行失败的样本通过将执行错误作为特权信息传递给教师，仍可沿自蒸馏路径进行学习。统一使用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct作为骨干模型。

Result: 在ChartMimic（图表生成）、Design2Code（UI生成）和AeSlides（幻灯片生成）三个基准上，Visual-SDPO相比零样本基线在主要指标上提升超10个绝对点，相比GRPO提升至少2.4个点，且训练步数更少，推理阶段无额外计算开销。

Conclusion: Visual-SDPO通过将渲染视觉反馈作为特权信息进行自蒸馏，并结合视觉定位的细粒度信用分配，有效解决了代码生成LLM在视觉产物生成中的渲染缺陷问题。该方法通用性强，可统一应用于图表、UI和幻灯片等多种视觉代码生成任务，在性能显著提升的同时保持了推理效率。

Abstract: Code-generating large language models (LLMs) increasingly produce visual artifacts such as charts, web pages, and slides by writing programs that are executed by non-differentiable renderers, committing to code before observing the render. As a result, otherwise executable code often yields artifacts with visually salient defects, including overlapping elements, clipped text, broken alignment, low contrast, and overflow. We study visual-feedback self-distillation for code-generated visual artifacts. We propose Visual-SDPO, a self-distillation policy-optimization framework that treats rendered visual feedback as privileged context for a weight-sharing teacher and distills this feedback into a coding student. To make supervision spatially targeted rather than uniform, we introduce Visual-Grounded Code Credit Weighting, which traces each detected defect back to the code statements responsible for the affected elements and amplifies the distillation signal on those statements. A sequence-level GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) term complements the dense token-level objective by rewarding executable, visually high-quality rollouts, while failed executions remain learnable through the self-distillation path by passing execution errors as privileged context to the teacher. We instantiate Visual-SDPO for chart, web/UI, and slide generation with a unified Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone. Across chart-to-code, UI-to-code, and slide-generation benchmarks (ChartMimic, Design2Code, and AeSlides), Visual-SDPO improves over the zero-shot base by more than 10 absolute points in the primary metric and over GRPO by at least 2.4 points, with fewer training steps and no added inference-time cost.

</details>


### [189] [Reasoning or Memorization? Direction-Aware Diversity Exploration in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10346)
*Jiangnan Xia,Yucheng Shi,Yu Yang,Kishan Panaganti,Zhenwen Liang,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiRL 提出一种方向感知的强化学习框架，通过从模型表征中提取推理-记忆内部方向，构建方向加权梯度特征来区分和奖励真正的推理探索、抑制记忆化捷径探索，并集成到 GRPO 中，在数学和通用推理基准上显著优于现有探索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 探索方法仅鼓励语义或梯度空间的多样性，但无法区分多样性来源于真正的推理过程还是记忆化模式的变体。不加区分地奖励两者会导致探索偏向记忆化而非真正的推理能力提升。

Method: 提出 DiRL（Direction-Aware RL）框架：(1) 从模型表征中提取推理-记忆化的内部方向；(2) 基于该方向构建方向加权的梯度特征来刻画每次 rollout 的更新特性；(3) 重塑奖励函数，放大与推理方向对齐的探索、抑制与记忆化方向对齐的变化；(4) 无缝集成到 GRPO 中。

Result: 在数学推理和通用推理基准上的广泛实验表明，DiRL 相比多种现有探索方法均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过方向感知的奖励塑形区分推理与记忆化探索，DiRL 能有效引导策略朝向真正的推理能力提升，为 RL-based 推理增强提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a key paradigm for eliciting reasoning abilities in large language models, where exploration is crucial for discovering effective solution trajectories. Existing exploration methods typically encourage diversity in semantic or gradient spaces, without distinguishing what drives this diversity. A trajectory may appear novel because it follows a new reasoning process, or because it varies memorized patterns and shortcuts. Rewarding both cases equally may steer exploration toward memorization rather than genuine reasoning improvement. In this paper, we propose DiRL, a Direction-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework that anchors exploration to an internal reasoning-memorization direction of the policy. Specifically, DiRL extracts this direction from model representations, constructs direction-weighted gradient features to characterize rollout updates, and shapes rewards to amplify reasoning-aligned exploration while suppressing memorization-aligned variations. DiRL integrates seamlessly into standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Extensive experiments on mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of DiRL, showing significant improvements over various existing exploration methods.

</details>


### [190] [ReflectiChain: Epistemic Grounding in LLM-Driven World Models for Supply Chain Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10359)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REFLECTICHAIN 提出了一个生成式供应链世界模型（SC-WM），将异构供应网络编码为具备物理守恒的6维图-隐空间，并通过双环学习分离认知不确定性与随机不确定性，在半导体供应链基准测试中显著提升了推理一致性、抗扰动能力和反脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前供应链AI智能体面临根本性的认知鸿沟：大语言模型（LLM）能够理解策略文本但缺乏物理世界的操作根基，而强化学习（RL）擅长优化物流却对非结构化约束语义盲视。二者各自存在短板，无法单独胜任复杂供应链环境中的决策任务。

Method: 作者提出 REFLECTICHAIN 框架，核心包含两大组件：（1）生成式供应链世界模型（SC-WM），将异构供应网络映射到一个具有物理守恒约束的6维图-隐空间；（2）双环学习机制，将认知不确定性（通过KL置信域约束的策略自适应）与随机不确定性（随机隐空间推演）显式分离。评估采用 Semi-Sim 基准，该基准包含10节点半导体供应链、SIR风险传播模型、6类扰动类型和10种策略约束模板。

Result: 在 Semi-Sim 基准上，REFLECTICHAIN 的推理一致性得分（Rationale Consistency Score）提升33.0%（p < 0.0001，Cohen's d = 2.78），在对抗性冲击下维持82.3%的可操作性，并在中等压力条件下表现出反脆弱行为（+40.2%的增益）。研究还识别出三种操作性认知机制：不确定性分离、知识边界检测和经验贝叶斯策略更新。

Conclusion: REFLECTICHAIN 通过显式建模并分离两类不确定性，有效弥合了LLM与RL在供应链决策中的能力鸿沟，其反脆弱特性表明该方法不仅在正常条件下表现优异，更能从适度扰动中获益。文章同时讨论了五类局限性，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: AI agents in supply chains face a fundamental epistemic gap: large language models (LLMs) interpret policies but lack physical grounding, while reinforcement learning (RL) optimizes flows but is semantically blind to unstructured constraints. We introduce REFLECTICHAIN, bridging this gap through a Generative Supply Chain World Model (SC-WM) - encoding heterogeneous supply networks into a 6-dim graph-latent space with physical conservation - and Double-Loop Learning that separates epistemic uncertainty (KL-trust-region-bounded policy adaptation) from aleatoric uncertainty (stochastic latent rollouts). On Semi-Sim, a 10-node semiconductor benchmark with SIR risk propagation, 6 perturbation types, and 10 policy constraint templates, REFLECTICHAIN improves Rationale Consistency Score by 33.0% (p < 0.0001, d = 2.78), maintains 82.3% operability under adversarial shocks, and exhibits anti-fragile behavior (+40.2% gain under moderate pressure). We identify three operational epistemic mechanisms - uncertainty separation, knowledge-boundary detection, and empirical Bayesian policy updating - and discuss five limitation categories.

</details>


### [191] [Belief-Space Control for Personalized Cancer Treatment via Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10376)
*Deniz Sargun,H. Bugra Tulay,C. Emre Koksal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将癌症治疗建模为信念空间中的主动推理规划问题，利用预期自由能目标统一目标导向控制与信息获取，并在真实临床数据上验证了在测量预算约束下同时实现患者分类和高治疗效果的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症治疗本质上是一个序贯决策问题，具有部分可观测性、潜在的患者异质性以及医疗测量预算的显式约束。传统强化学习方法控制状态轨迹，但癌症治疗会永久性改变患者的转移动态，使得状态演化方式发生变化，因此需要一种能够同时处理信息获取（患者分型）和目标导向治疗的新框架。

Method: 采用主动推理（Active Inference）框架，将癌症治疗建模为信念空间规划问题；推导出预期自由能（Expected Free-Energy）目标函数，在测量预算约束下统一了目标导向控制（治疗效果最大化）与信息获取（患者分类）；使用AACR Project GENIE Biopharma Collaborative数据集中的真实临床癌症数据进行实现和验证。

Result: 在真实临床数据上的实验结果表明，该方法能够在真实的测量和治疗约束条件下，同时实现准确的患者分类和高治疗效果。

Conclusion: 主动推理框架为癌症治疗序贯决策提供了一种有效的方法，能够在有限测量预算下平衡信息探索与治疗利用，为个性化癌症治疗的临床决策支持开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Cancer treatment is at the core a sequential decision-making problem with partial observability, latent patient heterogeneity, and explicit constraints on the budget for medical measurements. Unlike standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches that control state trajectories, cancer treatments permanently modify patients' transition dynamics, changing how states evolve over time. We model cancer treatment as a belief-space planning problem using active inference, deriving an expected free-energy objective that unifies goal-directed control and information acquisition under measurement budgets without. We implement this framework using real clinical cancer data from the AACR Project GENIE Biopharma Collaborative dataset. Results on clinical data demonstrate a simultaneous patient categorization and high treatment efficacy, under real measurement and treatment constraints.

</details>


### [192] [Beyond Static Evaluation: Co-Evolutionary Mechanisms for LLM-Driven Strategy Evolution in Adversarial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10389)
*Haoran Li,Zengle Ge,Ziyang Zhang,Xiaomin Yuan,Yui Lo,Qianhui Liu,Bocheng An,Dongke Rong,Jiaqun Liu,Annan Li,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FAMOU 框架通过评估器协同进化、分层深度评估和弱点压力三种机制，解决了对抗性多智能体游戏中因策略改进导致评估失效的问题，在 MCTF 2026 海战夺旗任务上超越基线方法，并发现了全新的战术算法结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动的代码进化方法在对抗性多智能体游戏中面临核心挑战：随着策略不断改进，固定的评估器变得不可靠，导致进化停滞。需要解决这一评估-进化脱节问题。

Method: 提出三种机制：(1) 评估器协同进化——将发现的冠军策略纳入对手池；(2) 分层深度评估——用统计可靠的评估替代噪声较大的少数几局评分；(3) 弱点压力——动态加权最难对付的对手以突破性能瓶颈。三者集成于 FAMOU 框架，基于与 OpenEvolve 和 ShinkaEvolve 相同的基础模型代码进化范式。

Result: 在 MCTF 2026 3v3 海上夺旗任务中，FAMOU 在两种骨干 LLM 下均优于基线：综合得分最高 (0.526)，对未见对手泛化胜率最佳 (61.7%)。消融实验验证了各机制的有效性。LLM 变异过程生成了种子策略中不存在的战术结构（前瞻搜索、自适应拦截）。在 AAMAS 2026 MCTF 竞赛中获硬件轮循赛第1名、仿真赛第3名。

Conclusion: 代码级进化可以在对抗场景中产生非平凡的算法创新，评估器协同进化是持续改进的关键，且进化出的策略具备真实世界可迁移性。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-driven code evolution have enabled automated discovery by iteratively generating and improving programs. However, applying these methods to adversarial multi-agent games introduces a fundamental challenge: the evaluation landscape shifts as strategies improve, causing fixed evaluators to become unreliable and evolution to stagnate. We propose three mechanisms to address this challenge: evaluator co-evolution, which incorporates discovered champions into the opponent pool; hierarchical deep evaluation, which replaces noisy few-game scores with statistically reliable assessments; and weakness pressure, which dynamically up-weights the most difficult opponents to break through plateaus. We implement these mechanisms within FAMOU, a framework built upon the same foundation-model code-evolution paradigm as OpenEvolve and ShinkaEvolve. On the MCTF 2026 3v3 maritime capture-the-flag task, FAMOU consistently outperforms both baselines under two backbone LLMs, achieving the highest combined score (0.526) and the best generalization to unseen opponents (61.7% win rate), while ablations confirm that each mechanism contributes to performance. Notably, the LLM mutation process generates tactical structures entirely absent from the seed strategies -- including lookahead search and adaptive interception -- demonstrating that code-level evolution can produce nontrivial algorithmic innovations in adversarial settings. The FAMOU-evolved strategy further achieved 1st place in the hardware round-robin and 3rd in simulation at the AAMAS 2026 MCTF Competition, validating its real-world transferability. The optimized implementation and corresponding evaluation codes developed through our evolutionary process are available at: https://github.com/1xiangliu1/FAMOU-CoEvo

</details>


### [193] [Instruction Finetuning DeepSeek-R1-8B Model Using LoRA and NEFTune](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10392)
*Wu Yuerong,Mingni Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将DeepSeek-R1-8B大模型结合LoRA和NEFTune技术应用于金融命名实体识别，在7类实体上达到0.912的micro-F1，优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在金融NER任务中常出现实体误分类或忽略领域特定模式的问题，需要探索如何高效地将开源LLM适配到金融领域，提升实体识别精度。

Method: 使用1693条标注样本构建指令微调数据集；在DeepSeek-R1-8B的Transformer层插入轻量级LoRA矩阵进行参数高效微调；训练时通过NEFTune向嵌入向量添加均匀噪声以增强泛化能力。

Result: LoRA微调后的DeepSeek-R1-8B在7类实体（Company、Date、Location、Money、Person、Product、Quantity）上micro-F1达0.901；叠加NEFTune后进一步提升至0.912，超越Llama3-8B、Qwen3-8B、Baichuan2-7B、T5和BERT-Base等基线模型。

Conclusion: DeepSeek-R1-8B配合LoRA+NEFTune是一种高效的金融NER方案，NEFTune引入的噪声正则化能有效提升模型泛化性能。

Abstract: Financial named-entity recognition (NER) is essential for translating unstructured financial reports and news into structured knowledge graphs. However, general-purpose large language models (LLMs) often misclassify financial entities or ignore domain-specific patterns. This paper investigates the use of DeepSeek-R1-8B, a recent open-source large language model, combined with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) and Noisy Embedding Fine-Tuning (NEFTune) for financial NER. Each annotated sentence in our corpus of 1693 samples is converted into an instruction-input-output triple. We insert lightweight LoRA matrices into the Transformer layers and apply NEFTune to improve generalisation by adding uniform noise to embedding vectors during training. Experiments show that the LoRA-adapted DeepSeek-R1-8B achieves a micro-F1 of 0.901 on seven entity types (Company, Date, Location, Money, Person, Product and Quantity), and adding NEFTune further boosts the micro-F1 to 0.912, outperforming Llama3-8B, Qwen3-8B, Baichuan2-7B, T5 and BERT-Base baselines.

</details>


### [194] [STAGE-Claw: Automated State-based Agent Benchmarking for Realistic Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10394)
*Sirui Liang,Bohan Yu,Peiyu Wang,Shiguang Guo,Wenxing Hu,Pengfei Cao,Jian Zhao,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAGE-Claw 是一个自动化框架，用于在真实个人计算环境中构建和评估个人代理（personal agents）。它从任务提示自动生成包含环境、提示、真值和验证程序的基准任务，并通过最终系统状态的正确性（而非仅文本响应）来衡量代理表现。论文使用该框架创建了40个任务，评估了11个前沿模型，分析了得分、成本、工具调用可靠性和常见失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人代理评估基准依赖沙盒化制品、静态任务设计和粗粒度评分，难以扩展且无法可靠评估代理在真实场景中的表现。需要一个可扩展的、基于状态的自动化评估框架来解决这些限制。

Method: 提出 STAGE-Claw 框架：给定任务提示，自动创建并验证包含环境配置、任务提示、真值（ground truth）和验证程序的基准任务。代理在真实操作环境中执行任务，评估以最终系统状态（如文件、配置、应用状态）的正确性为核心指标，而非仅评估文本输出。支持自动化端到端的基准构建与评估流程。

Result: 使用 STAGE-Claw 构建了包含40个真实场景任务的基准，评估了11个前沿模型，输出了各模型的任务得分、运行成本、工具调用可靠性以及常见失败模式的分析。

Conclusion: STAGE-Claw 提供了一种可扩展的、基于状态的代理评估方法，能够在真实用户场景下可靠地衡量个人代理的能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to power personal agents for everyday applications, but evaluating these agents remains a challenge. Existing benchmarks still rely on sandboxed artifacts, static task design, and coarse scoring, which hinder scalability and limit progress toward reliable personal-agent evaluation. This paper introduces STAGE-Claw, an automated framework for building and evaluating realistic personal-agent scenarios in state-based personal-computing environments. Given a task hint, STAGE-Claw automatically creates and validates a realistic benchmark task with its environment, task prompts, ground truth, and related verification programs. Agents are then evaluated in realistic operating environments, where performance is measured by the correctness of the final system state rather than only the textual response. Using STAGE-Claw, this paper creates a benchmark with 40 challenging real scenario agent tasks, evaluates 11 frontier models, and analyzes their task scores, costs, tool-call reliability, and common failure patterns. Overall, STAGE-Claw offers a scalable, state-based way to evaluate agents in realistic user scenarios.

</details>


### [195] [A Unified Multi-Modal Framework for Intelligent Financial Systems: Integrating Reinforcement Learning, High-Frequency Trading, and Game-Theoretic Approaches with Cross-Modal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10412)
*Fanrong Liu,Zhang Yuwei,Mingni Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将PPO机器人投顾、高频交易时间序列预测、上下文学习投资咨询、博弈论银行竞争策略及跨模态情感分析五大金融AI技术进行融合，实验表明各项指标均有显著提升（15.6%-31.2%），并给出了收敛性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI研究各自孤立发展——机器人投顾、高频交易预测、投资咨询、银行博弈策略、情感分析等技术被独立开发，未能发挥它们之间的协同效应。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个能同时处理多领域金融挑战的统一智能系统。

Method: 提出统一框架，无缝集成五大技术模块：(1) 近端策略优化(PPO)用于机器人投顾系统；(2) 高级时间序列预测模型用于高频交易；(3) 上下文学习(In-Context Learning)机制用于动态投资咨询；(4) 博弈论方法用于竞争性银行场景建模；(5) 统一嵌入(Unified Embeddings)实现跨模态金融情感分析。文章还为该集成优化问题提供了收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在多个金融数据集和真实场景实验中，统一框架全面超越各领域专用系统：投资组合优化指标提升23.7%，高频交易预测误差降低31.2%，投资建议准确率提升18.9%，纳什均衡收敛速度提升27.4%，跨模态融合情感分析准确率提升15.6%。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅推进了金融AI的前沿水平，还为构建适应现代金融市场复杂互联性的综合智能系统提供了蓝图。统一框架的实践适用性已在多种金融机构场景中得到验证。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of financial technology demands sophisticated artificial intelligence systems capable of handling diverse challenges across multiple domains simultaneously. This paper presents a groundbreaking unified framework that seamlessly integrates Proximal Policy Optimization for robo-advisory systems, advanced time-series prediction models for high-frequency trading, in-context learning mechanisms for dynamic investment advisory, game-theoretic approaches for competitive banking scenarios, and unified embeddings for cross-modal financial sentiment analysis. Our comprehensive framework addresses the critical gap in existing literature where these technologies have been developed in isolation, failing to leverage their synergistic potential. Through extensive experimentation across multiple financial datasets and real-world scenarios, we demonstrate that our integrated approach achieves superior performance compared to specialized single-domain systems. Specifically, our framework shows a 23.7% improvement in portfolio optimization metrics, reduces prediction error in high-frequency trading by 31.2%, enhances investment recommendation accuracy by 18.9%, optimizes competitive banking strategies with a 27.4% increase in Nash equilibrium convergence speed, and improves sentiment analysis accuracy by 15.6% through cross-modal fusion. The theoretical foundation of our work establishes convergence guarantees for the integrated optimization problem, while our empirical results validate the practical applicability across diverse financial institutions. This research not only advances the state-of-the-art in financial AI but also provides a blueprint for developing comprehensive intelligent systems that can adapt to the complex, interconnected nature of modern financial markets.

</details>


### [196] [Soul Computing: A Theoretical Framework and Technical Architecture for Intelligent Agents with Independent Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10413)
*Jinshan Zhang,Xishi Zhou,Qiu Peng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统探讨了从前沿AI技术驱动的传统虚拟人到"灵魂计算"范式的变革逻辑，首次正式界定了狭义与广义灵魂计算的学术内涵，并提出灵魂计算系统必须在架构上构建"内涵性"核心，以实现AI从工具性到生命主体性的根本转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI与数字人交叉领域存在根本性的概念模糊：下一代智能体与传统虚拟人的本质差异、具有自我身份的数字实体的构建路径、以及核心技术伦理挑战均亟待澄清。

Method: 系统性地分析人类意识与记忆机制的演化模式，重新评估海量多模态数字碎片在逆向重建个体精神世界中的核心价值，并在此基础上对灵魂计算进行学术界定与边界划分。

Result: 首次正式划分了狭义与广义灵魂计算的学术内涵，明确了其与情感计算、历史重建和凡人计算（Mortal Computation）的本质区别；论证了灵魂计算系统须构建"内涵性"核心而非纯"外延性"功能载体。

Conclusion: 灵魂计算范式代表了AI从工具属性向生命主体性跃迁的根本逻辑转折，其核心在于架构层面的"内涵性"构建，而非仅仅作为功能性载体存在。

Abstract: Breakthroughs in large language models and multimodal generation technologies have propelled the digital reconstruction of human mental traits, emotional patterns, and long-term memory from science fiction toward engineering practice. Yet current research and industry practices at the intersection of AI and digital humans remain hampered by fundamental conceptual ambiguities: the essential differences between next-generation intelligent agents and traditional virtual humans, the construction pathways for digital entities possessing self-identity, and the core technical and ethical challenges confronting this domain all demand urgent clarification. This paper systematically examines the transformative logic underlying the transition from traditional virtual humans to the ``Soul Computing'' paradigm, driven by frontier AI technologies. We first analyze the evolutionary patterns of human consciousness and memory mechanisms, reassessing the core value of massive multimodal digital fragments in the reverse reconstruction of individual mental worlds. On this basis, we formally delineate the academic connotations of narrow and broad Soul Computing for the first time, clarifying its academic boundaries and essential distinctions from Affective Computing, Historical Reconstruction, and Mortal Computation. We argue that Soul Computing systems must architecturally construct an ``Intensional'' core rather than serving as purely ``Extensional'' functional carriers, thereby enabling the fundamental transition of AI from toolhood to living agency.

</details>


### [197] [Trace2Policy: From Expert Behavior Traces to Self-Evolving Decision Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10457)
*Junli Zha,Jinbo Wang,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Policy 通过EISR（错误驱动的迭代技能精炼）机制，系统化地从企业专家的隐性决策规则中恢复并优化可读规则文档。研究表明，在此类合规敏感、偏斜基率的决策任务中，规则质量而非模型能力是主导性能杠杆。经过8轮EISR精炼的规则编译为确定性Python后达到79.6%准确率，且推理时无需LLM调用。在物流企业22天生产部署中，编译管道优于纯LLM基线，且LLM回退会单调降低准确率。Auto-EISR变体以每轮5-10美元成本复现此精炼过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业专家在审计、合规、合同审查中隐式应用的决策规则难以系统化提取和优化。现有LLM蒸馏方法在偏斜基率、合规敏感任务上性能停滞（~70%），亟需一种以规则质量为核心优化目标的方法来突破瓶颈。

Method: 提出Trace2Policy框架，核心为EISR（错误驱动的迭代技能精炼）机制：每轮在验证集上执行规则，按根因将错误聚类为MISSING、WRONG、CONFLICT三类，应用针对性补丁，仅通过回归检验的修改方可提交。最终精炼规则可编译为确定性Python代码实现零LLM推理。另提出Auto-EISR变体使用LLM自动执行精炼过程。

Result: 在五款LLM上，单次蒸馏停滞在~70%准确率；8轮EISR将规则提升至79.6%（编译为Python执行）。同一精炼内容，Python编译形式比LLM prompt形式高9.8pp。在大型物流承运商22天部署中（3349个审计案例），编译管道超越纯LLM基线（72.7%），LLM回退单调降低准确率。Auto-EISR以每轮$5-10成本复现精炼，并在LegalBench和BPIC 2012等四个公开基准上实现迁移。

Conclusion: 在合规敏感、偏斜基率的决策任务中，规则质量是比模型能力更关键的性能杠杆。EISR机制能够以低成本、可解释的方式系统化恢复和优化专家规则，编译为确定性代码后不仅提升准确率，且消除了推理时的LLM依赖和性能退化风险。

Abstract: Decision rules that enterprise experts apply tacitly -- in auditing, compliance, and contract review -- can be systematically recovered and improved through iterative error analysis. We present \textbf{Trace2Policy}, whose core mechanism -- \textbf{EISR} (\textbf{E}rror-driven \textbf{I}terative \textbf{S}kill \textbf{R}efinement) -- maintains a human-readable rule document as its optimization target: each round executes the rules on a validation set, clusters errors by root cause into MISSING, WRONG, or CONFLICT types, applies targeted patches, and commits only those that pass a regression gate. \textbf{For this class of compliance-sensitive, skewed-base-rate decision tasks, we identify rule quality -- not model capability -- as the dominant performance lever}: across five LLMs, one-shot distillation plateaus near $\sim$70\% on the deployed pool, while eight EISR rounds lift the same rules to 79.6\% when compiled into deterministic Python -- zero LLM calls at inference. \textbf{Execution form compounds the gain: in production, the same EISR-refined content runs 9.8~pp higher as compiled Python than as an LLM prompt, a form-and-engineering bundle the 22-day deployment matured together.} Deployed for 22 days at a major logistics carrier (3,349 audit cases), the compiled pipeline outperforms the pure-LLM baseline it replaced (72.7\%); on these calibrated, skewed-base-rate workloads, re-enabling LLM fallback monotonically degrades accuracy. An LLM-driven variant, \textbf{Auto-EISR}, reproduces this refinement at \$5--\$10 per cycle versus $\sim$70 expert-hours, and transfers to four public benchmarks spanning legal reasoning (LegalBench) and process-mining decisions (BPIC 2012) without re-engineering.

</details>


### [198] [ComBench: A Benchmark for Rigorous Proof Reasoning and Constructive Realization in Olympiad-Level Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10479)
*Shunkai Zhang,Haoran Zhang,Yun Luo,Qianjia Cheng,Haodi Lei,Yizhuo Li,Runzhe Zhan,Zhilin Wang,Bangjie Xu,Yucheng Su,Xinmiao Han,Xiaoye Qu,Dongrui Liu,Zhouchen Lin,Yu Qiao,Ning Ding,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ComBench是一个包含100道奥赛级别组合数学题的基准，分为分析型和构造型两类，结合证明评分与构造验证，评估大语言模型的组合推理能力。最强模型仅达65.4%平均分，分析推理与构造实现是两种不同的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最强前沿模型在奥赛组合数学上表现不均衡，暴露出创造性数学推理的短板。现有基准缺乏对组合数学中严谨证明与显式构造两种核心能力的系统性诊断，因此需要专门设计基准来评估和区分这些能力。

Method: 构建ComBench基准，包含100道人工标注的竞赛级组合数学题，分为分析型（侧重严谨论证）和构造型（需显式构造及正确性证明）两类。评估方案采用评分标准驱动的证明评分与确定性构造验证相结合的方法，并以Overall Avg.和Best@4作为核心指标，在多个开源与闭源前沿模型上进行实验。

Result: 最强模型整体平均分仅65.4%，Best@4为75.3%，基准远未饱和。严谨证明推理与构造实现能力存在分化：Kimi-K2.6在分析型证明评分上落后于GPT-5.5，但在构造型Best@4上超越GPT-5.5。存在性与构造性问题在所有代表性前沿模型中始终是最难的题型。

Conclusion: ComBench揭示了当前大语言模型在奥赛组合数学上的能力瓶颈，证明推理与构造实现是两种可分离的、发展不均衡的核心能力，为未来模型在离散数学与创造性推理方面的改进提供了诊断依据。

Abstract: Combinatorics is central to Olympiad-level mathematical problem solving, requiring deep discrete reasoning, creative constructions, and rigorous structural insight. Recent evidence suggests that even today's strongest frontier models remain uneven on Olympiad combinatorics, revealing a gap in creative mathematical reasoning. We introduce ComBench, an Olympiad-level combinatorics benchmark for evaluating and diagnosing the combinatorial reasoning capabilities of large language models. ComBench contains 100 human-annotated competition-level problems organized around two complementary settings: analysis-centric problems, which primarily require rigorous mathematical arguments, and construction-centric problems, which require explicit constructions in addition to correctness justifications. The evaluation protocol combines rubric-guided proof grading with deterministic construction verification, exposing cases where proof quality and construction validity diverge. Experiments on frontier open- and closed-source models show that ComBench is far from saturated: the strongest model reaches 65.4% overall Avg. and 75.3% overall Best@4. We further find that Rigorous Proof Reasoning and Constructive Realization are distinct capabilities: Kimi-K2.6 trails GPT-5.5 on analysis-centric proof grading but surpasses it on construction-centric Best@4, while Existence and Construction problems remain consistently hardest across representative frontier models.

</details>


### [199] [A complementary study on PlanGPT: Evaluation with defined Performance Metrics and comparison with a planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10489)
*Youssef Abdelkader,Humbert Fiorino,Damien Pellier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于LLM的规划器PlanGPT进行了复现与补充评估，通过计划成本和生成时间两项指标与传统规划器对比，发现PlanGPT的性能仅相当于贪心搜索水平，对其实际有效性提出质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证以PlanGPT为代表的LLM进行自动化规划是否真正有效且值得关注。作者质疑PlanGPT官方论文中关于计划覆盖率的结果，并希望对其进行更全面的性能评估。

Method: 复现PlanGPT的相关实验，并引入两个新的评估指标——计划成本（Plan Cost）和计划生成时间（Plan Generation Time），将PlanGPT与传统的自动化规划器在相同计划和相同指标下进行对比。

Result: PlanGPT的表现并不优于贪心搜索策略，其规划能力有限。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的规划方法（以PlanGPT为例）在自动化规划任务中并未展现出超越传统简单策略（如贪心搜索）的优势，其规划的 pertinence 和 worthwhile 存疑。

Abstract: Automated Planning is a subfield of Artificial Intelligence (AI) where the main objective is generating a sequence of actions, known as a plan, that helps us reach a goal state from an initial state. A planning problem is defined by a set of objects, an initial state and a desired goal state. The objective is to compute a plan that'll lead us from the inital state to the goal state. Programs that generate plans are called planners.
  In this paper, we did a complementary study to the state-of-the-art LLM called PlanGPT which was released last year. We redid some experiments to verify whether planning with LLMs is \textbf{pertinent} and \textbf{worthwhile}. We also check whether the results obtained in the official PlanGPT paper for plan coverage were correct, and we also performed a more comprehensive study on PlanGPT's performance: in our paper PlanGPT's performance was evaluated using two metrics: Plan Cost and Plan Generation Time. The results of planGPT were compared to those produced by a traditional planner for the same plans and same metrics. We discovered that PlanGPT is no better than a Greedy search strategy.

</details>


### [200] [A Reliable Fault Diagnosis Method Based on Belief Rule Base Consider Robustness Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10500)
*Mingyuan Liu,Dan Yin,Zongzong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于置信规则库（BRB）的故障诊断方法，系统性地进行鲁棒性分析，并设计三种鲁棒性约束策略来优化模型鲁棒性。在柴油机和轴承故障诊断实验中，该方法同时提升了诊断精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器读数广泛用于故障诊断，但其可靠性直接影响诊断结果。现有故障诊断模型面临鲁棒性评估与鲁棒性优化两大问题，需要一种既能保证诊断精度又具备良好鲁棒性的方法。

Method: （1）对BRB故障诊断模型进行系统性的鲁棒性分析；（2）提出三种鲁棒性约束策略用于优化BRB模型的鲁棒性；（3）在WD615柴油机和凯斯西储大学轴承数据集上进行故障诊断验证。

Result: 在WD615柴油机和CWRU轴承两个实例上的实验表明，所提出的模型在准确率和鲁棒性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 基于BRB并考虑鲁棒性分析的故障诊断方法能够有效解决鲁棒性评估与优化问题，在保证诊断精度的同时增强模型对传感器读数不确定性的抵抗能力，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: In equipment operation, the implementation of fault diagnosis is essential to ensure the continuity and safety of production equipment, improve operational efficiency and reduce maintenance costs. Since sensor readings are widely used for fault diagnosis, their reliability directly affects the results of fault diagnosis. A new fault diagnosis method is proposed to address the two problems of robustness assessment and robustness optimization of fault diagnosis models. For this purpose, a reliable fault diagnosis method based on a belief rule base (BRB) considering robustness analysis is proposed. Firstly, the robustness analysis of the BRB model is carried out systematically. Secondly, three robustness constraint strategies are proposed to optimize the robustness of the BRB fault diagnosis model. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed model is verified by taking the fault diagnosis of WD615 diesel engine and Case Western Reserve University bearings as an example, and the experiments show that the proposed model improves both accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [201] [Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation without Paired Data: Theoretical Foundation and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10504)
*Trong Khiem Tran,Anh Duc Chu,Quang Hung Pham,Phi Le Nguyen,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需配对多模态数据的跨模态知识蒸馏框架，通过分布对齐（而非样本对齐）来实现教师模型到学生模型的知识迁移，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态知识蒸馏方法通常依赖语义对齐的配对多模态数据，但获取此类配对数据成本高昂且不切实际。因此需要一种在无配对数据条件下也能有效进行跨模态知识迁移的方法。

Method: 首先建立教师模型与学生模型之间的跨模态分布关系，揭示有效蒸馏的两个核心要素：特征对齐（表征分布层面）和标签对齐（预测分布层面）。在此基础上提出基于分布对齐而非个体样本对齐的跨模态知识蒸馏框架，并提供理论保证。

Result: 在多种多模态基准上的大量实验表明，该框架在无配对数据和有配对数据两种设置下均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过分布对齐实现跨模态知识蒸馏是可行且高效的，突破了传统方法对配对数据的依赖，拓展了跨模态知识蒸馏的应用场景。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation (CMKD) studies how a (large) teacher model trained on one type of data (e.g., images) can guide a (smaller) student model building on another type of data (e.g., text/audio). Existing CMKD methods often require paired multi-modal data with aligned semantics, but obtaining such paired data are often costly and impractical. To mitigate this limitation, we develop a new CMKD framework for the more challenging setting where paired data are unavailable. In particular, we establish a cross-modal distributional relationship between teacher and student models, which reveals two fundamental quantities governing effective distillation: feature alignment and label alignment. These quantities characterize semantic discrepancy between modalities at the levels of representation and prediction distributions, respectively. Motivated by this insight, we propose a principled framework, with theoretical guarantees, that enables effective cross-modal knowledge distillation by aligning distributions rather than individual samples. Extensive experiments across a wide range of multimodal benchmarks show that our framework is highly effective in both unpaired and paired data settings, improving significantly over prior work.

</details>


### [202] [HIPIF: Hierarchical Planning and Information Folding for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10507)
*Juncheng Diao,Zhicong Lu,Peiguang Li,Yongwei Zhou,Changyuan Tian,Qingbin Li,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HIPIF 通过分层规划与信息折叠机制，将长周期任务显式分解为子目标，并折叠已完成子目标的历史以降低长上下文干扰，同时结合分层反思与子目标导向过程奖励来稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长周期多轮任务中性能下降，根源在于长上下文干扰——持续增长的对话历史削弱了模型跟踪全局任务状态和后续推理决策的能力，而现有方法（细粒度信用分配、分层强化学习）并未直接解决这一问题。

Method: 提出HIPIF框架：（1）端到端训练LLM智能体围绕显式子目标组织长周期执行；（2）折叠已完成子目标的历史信息以减少上下文干扰；（3）结合分层反思与子目标导向过程奖励，无需昂贵辅助模型或专家轨迹即可指导子目标的生成、转移与执行。

Result: 在三个公开的智能体基准测试上，HIPIF均验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: HIPIF通过模拟人类子目标分解与进度总结的认知方式，有效缓解了长上下文干扰问题，为长周期LLM智能体学习提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities as autonomous agents across a wide range of tasks, their performance often degrades in multi-turn long-horizon agentic tasks. Existing methods have made progress through fine-grained credit assignment to alleviate long-horizon sparse rewards and hierarchical reinforcement learning to decompose tasks and reduce long-term dependency. However, these methods still do not directly address long-context interference, in which continuously growing histories weaken the agent's ability to track the global task state and impair subsequent reasoning and decision-making. Inspired by the way humans handle complex tasks through subgoal decomposition and completed progress summarization, we propose Hierarchical Planning and Information Folding (HIPIF) for long-horizon LLM agent learning. HIPIF trains the agent end-to-end to organize long-horizon execution around explicit subgoals while folding completed subgoal histories to reduce long-context interference. Furthermore, to stabilize subgoal-based planning and execution, HIPIF combines hierarchical reflection and subgoal-oriented process rewards to guide subgoal generation, transition, and execution, without relying on costly auxiliary models or task-specific expert trajectories. Extensive experiments on three publicly available agentic benchmarks demonstrate the validity of our method.

</details>


### [203] [ActiveMem: Distributed Active Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10532)
*Yunhan Jiang,Wenbin Duan,Shasha Guo,Liang Pang,Xiaoqian Sun,Huawei Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveMem，一个受前额叶皮层与海马体分工启发的异构框架，将LLM智能体的记忆与核心推理过程解耦，通过高层规划器利用语义要点进行推理，同时由轻量分布式记忆系统并行累积和巩固信息，在BrowseComp-Plus和GAIA基准上以更低开销实现了最先进的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的记忆机制多为集中式，在扩展推理轨迹时面临上下文过载与信息不可逆丢失的权衡困境。受人类认知系统中前额叶皮层（执行控制）与海马体（记忆管理）功能互补的启发，作者认为这种权衡并非固有，而是源于集中式记忆组织，因此需要一种解耦的异构记忆架构。

Method: 提出ActiveMem异构框架：①将智能体记忆与核心推理过程解耦；②高层Planner基于蒸馏的语义要点（semantic gists）执行推理；③轻量级分布式记忆系统并行运行，在整个任务过程中主动累积和巩固这些语义要点。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus和GAIA数据集上，ActiveMem以显著降低的计算开销达到了最先进的准确率，验证了分布式主动记忆在长程推理任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 集中式记忆组织是导致长程推理中上下文过载与信息丢失权衡的根源，而受大脑认知架构启发的异构分布式记忆框架ActiveMem能够有效打破这一权衡，在性能与效率上均取得优异表现。

Abstract: Memory is essential for enabling large language model (LLM) agents to handle long-horizon reasoning tasks. Existing memory mechanisms are largely centralized, typically organizing retrieved information and interaction history within a single model context. This design imposes a fundamental trade-off: scaling reasoning trajectories risks context overload, whereas aggressive content pruning may result in irreversible information loss. Seeking a better trade-off, we draw inspiration from human cognitive systems, especially the functional complementarity between the prefrontal cortex (executive control) and the hippocampus (memory management), suggesting that such a trade-off need not be inherent, but may instead stem from centralized memory organization. To this end, we propose ActiveMem, a heterogeneous framework that decouples agent memory from the core reasoning process. Specifically, a high-level Planner utilizes distilled semantic gists to execute reasoning, while a lightweight, distributed memory system operates in parallel to actively accumulate and consolidate these gists throughout the task. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus and GAIA show that ActiveMem achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly reduced overhead, demonstrating the effectiveness of distributed active memory for long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [204] [One Token per Multimodal Evidence: Latent Memory for Resource-Constrained QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10572)
*Zhi Zheng,Ziqiao Meng,Hao Luan,Wei Liu,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Latent Memory范式，用单个高维潜在token替代原始文本/图像证据，在统一潜在空间中完成检索与生成，大幅降低token消耗（3×-10×），同时保持竞争力强的QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部记忆范式以原始文本和图像形式存储证据，检索后需将完整内容传给生成模型，导致极高的token消耗与存储压力，无法适用于资源受限场景。

Method: 设计Latent Memory范式：使用小型压缩器LLM/VLM将每条原始证据压缩为单个高维潜在token；检索时在统一潜在空间中进行查询嵌入与潜在token检索，检索到的潜在token直接提示预训练LLM/VLM生成答案。压缩器通过重建损失、对比损失和蒸馏损失三者联合端到端训练，使每个潜在token同时具备信息保真（可重建）、可检索性和生成可用性。

Result: 在7个纯文本QA基准（如HotpotQA）和多模态QA基准上，Latent Memory以比RAG基线少3×-10×的生成token消耗，取得有竞争力的QA性能；在WebQA上实现最强的图像grounded QA性能。

Conclusion: Latent Memory以统一的潜在空间操作范式，有效缓解了记忆密集型QA中的token消耗和存储瓶颈，为资源受限应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: External memory effectively grounds large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs)-based question answering (QA) in relevant multimodal evidence. However, existing memory paradigms represent each memory item in raw text and image forms, so retrieval-based systems must pass the retrieved text or images to the generation LLMs/VLMs, resulting in high token consumption and storage pressure, making it unaffordable for resource-constrained applications. We propose Latent Memory, a latent-space memory paradigm that replaces each raw text or image evidence item with a single high-dimensional latent token produced by a small compressor LLM/VLM. Rather than retrieving raw evidence for generation, Latent Memory operates in a unified latent representation space: the query is embedded into this space to retrieve relevant latent tokens, and the retrieved latent tokens are directly prompted to a pretrained LLM or VLM for answer generation. To make each latent token simultaneously informative for reconstruction, retrieval, and generation, we train the compressor with reconstruction, contrastive, and distillation objectives in a unified end-to-end manner. Latent Memory is evaluated on seven text-only QA benchmarks (e.g., HotpotQA) and multimodal QA benchmarks, where it achieves competitive QA performance compared to advanced RAG baselines while consuming 3x to 10x fewer generator tokens. It can also deliver the strongest image-grounded QA performance on WebQA. Code is available at https://github.com/zz1358m/Latent-Memory-Master.

</details>


### [205] [Learning What to Remember: Observability-Safe Memory Retention via Constrained Optimization for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10616)
*Qingcan Kang,Liu Mingyang,Shixiong Kai,Kaichao Liang,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将长程语言智能体的记忆保留问题形式化为一个受约束的随机优化问题，提出OSL-MR框架，在严格分离在线可观测特征与离线监督的条件下，结合证据学习器与Mixed-Score启发式先验，学习查询条件的证据价值策略，在LOCOMO和LongMemEval上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统将保留视为局部决策问题，未显式建模有限上下文窗口下的长期后果（包括错失惩罚、重新获取延迟和信息陈旧风险），且忽略了在线部署时实际可观测性约束与离线可用监督之间的不一致。

Method: OSL-MR框架：(1) 将记忆保留形式化为带预算可行性、证据效用和延迟成本的约束随机优化问题；(2) 强制执行在线可观测特征与离线可用监督（OAS）的严格分离；(3) 结合基于已实现证据监督训练的证据学习器与Mixed-Score启发式，后者既作为可部署的在线安全基线，又作为结构化归纳先验引导学习，最终从交互数据中学习查询条件的证据价值策略。

Result: 在LOCOMO和LongMemEval上，OSL-MR一致优于基于新近度的方法、Generative Agents风格评分及其他启发式基线，尤其在紧张记忆预算下优势明显；Mixed-Score先验在保持召回率的同时提升精确率；敏感性分析展示了跨广泛成本配置的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将记忆保留建模为考虑长期后果的约束优化问题，并严格对齐在线部署的可观测性约束，OSL-MR为长程语言智能体的记忆管理提供了一种更原则化且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents accumulate observations, reasoning traces, and retrieved facts that exceed their finite context windows, making memory retention a fundamental resource-allocation problem. Existing memory systems improve management through heuristic scoring, retrieval optimization, or learned compression, but largely treat retention as a local decision problem and do not explicitly model its long-term consequences under realistic observability constraints. To fill this gap, we formulate memory retention as a constrained stochastic optimization problem with explicit budget feasibility, evidence utility, and delayed costs including miss penalties, reacquisition delays, and stale-information risk. We then propose OSL-MR (Observability-Safe Learning for Memory Retention), a novel framework that enforces a strict separation between online-observable features and offline-available supervision (OAS). OSL-MR combines an evidence learner trained from realized evidence supervision with a Mixed-Score heuristic that serves both as a deployable online-safe baseline and as a structured inductive prior for learning. The resulting policy learns query-conditioned evidence value directly from interaction data while remaining deployable under the same observability constraints. Experiments on LOCOMO and LongMemEval show that OSL-MR consistently outperforms recency-based methods, Generative Agents-style scoring, and other heuristic baselines, particularly under tight memory budgets. The Mixed-Score prior further improves precision while preserving recall, and sensitivity analysis demonstrates robustness across a wide range of cost configurations.

</details>


### [206] [Infini Memory: Maintainable Topic Documents for Long-Term LLM Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10677)
*Suozhao Ji,Baodong Wu,Zehao Wang,Lei Xia,Qingping Li,Ruisong Wang,Wenbo Ding,Zhenhua Zhu,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infini Memory 提出一种以主题文档为核心的可维护持久记忆架构，通过缓冲区暂存、周期性整合和迭代式检索，使 LLM 智能体能够追踪变化的事实并跨会话聚合证据，在 MemoryAgentBench 上达到 64.7% 的综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体长期记忆系统通常将观察记录存储为孤立条目、摘要或索引片段，导致证据聚合困难、事实难以修正、记忆维护成本高。需要一个能够持续追踪变化事实、跨会话提供相关证据的可维护持久记忆方案。

Method: Infini Memory 将记忆组织为主题结构化的文本文档，每个主题文档作为语义单元收集相关证据、保留元数据并支持事实随时间修正。新观察先进入缓冲区暂存，再周期性整合为连贯的文本上下文。推理时采用智能体式检索流程，让 LLM 通过迭代工具调用逐步阅读记忆，而非单步检索。
