*Yiyuan She,Zhaojun Hu,Yifan Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于联邦学习的范围正则化方法，通过极值聚类自适应地将个性化特征权重推向极端值，以提升统计精度并诱导有利于量化、编码和资源效率的跨客户端规律性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中线性系统组件面临统计精度不足以及跨客户端缺乏规律性（不利于量化、编码和资源效率）的问题，需要一种能同时提升精度并诱导结构化稀疏/聚类模式的方案。

Method: 提出范围正则化（range regularization）方法：识别跨客户端共享权重的特征，并通过极值聚类（polar clustering）自适应地将个性化特征的权重聚类到极端值。理论分析上，针对该正则项的半范数性质和非可分解性，发展了新的非渐近分析证明技术。优化方面，利用局部强凸性的不同程度设计快速优化算法以降低迭代复杂度。

Result: 理论上建立了非渐近统计精度保证和模式忠实恢复的界；实验验证了所提方法在统计精度和资源效率上的有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: 范围正则化结合极值聚类能够有效提升联邦学习的统计精度，同时诱导出有利于压缩与通信效率的跨客户端权重规律性，理论和实验均支持该方法的优越性。

Abstract: This paper introduces range regularization for federated learning with linear systematic components to enhance statistical accuracy and induce cross-client regularity conducive to quantization, coding, and resource efficiency. Our approach identifies features with shared weights across different clients and adaptively clusters the weights of personalized features at extreme values, a process we refer to as polar clustering. Theoretical analysis of the associated estimators poses significant challenges due to the seminorm nature and non-decomposability of the regularizer. We develop new proof techniques for the nonasymptotic analysis of statistical accuracy and faithful pattern recovery. Moreover, a fast optimization algorithm that leverages varying degrees of local strong convexity is proposed to reduce iteration complexity. Experiments support the efficacy and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [241] [Generalized Conformal Predictive Systems Under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11044)
*Jef Jonkers,Johanna Ziegel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将广义共形预测系统(CPS)扩展至非可交换场景,通过观测特定的置换权重编码分布偏移,构建了移位感知的预测系统。同时引入权重不确定性盒,提供有限样本或渐近置信保证的鲁棒CPS包络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测系统依赖于数据的可交换性假设,但在协变量偏移、反馈驱动设计等现实场景中该假设常被违背。作者旨在将CPS扩展到非可交换设置,使预测分布在分布偏移下仍保持校准有效性。

Method: 核心方法包括:(1)通过观测特定的置换权重编码分布偏移,使得测试点在给定无序样本的条件下可视为从观测原子中的加权抽取;(2)引入权重不确定性盒(weight-uncertainty boxes)处理权重估计误差;(3)构建鲁棒CPS包络,提供有限样本或渐近的置信保证;(4)针对一致性测度CPS、共形分箱、共形保序分布回归三种具体方法推导了高效计算方案。

Result: 在协变量偏移和反馈驱动生物分子设计两类实验中,所提方法产出了校准的预测带——偏移越强,预测带越宽;样本量增大时预测带收紧,符合理论预期。

Conclusion: 本文成功将广义CPS推广至非可交换数据场景,通过加权置换和不确定性盒机制,在保持有限样本或渐近有效性的同时,实现了对分布偏移的自适应感知与鲁棒预测。

Abstract: Conformal predictive systems (CPS) output calibrated bands of CDFs under exchangeability. We extend generalized CPS to non-exchangeable settings by encoding distributional shifts through observation-specific permutation weights. This yields shift-aware predictive systems that remain valid whenever the test point is, conditionally on the unordered sample, a weighted draw from the observed atoms. Since such weights are typically estimated, we introduce weight-uncertainty boxes and construct robust CPS envelopes with finite-sample or asymptotic confidence guarantees. We derive efficient computation for conformity-measure CPS, conformal binning, and conformal isotonic distributional regression. Experiments under covariate shift and feedback-driven biomolecular design show calibrated predictive bands that widen under stronger shifts and tighten as sample size increases.

</details>


### [242] [Itô maps for any-step SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11156)
*Zhengkai Pan,Peter Potaptchik,Wenxi Yao,Michael S. Albergo,Jakiw Pidstrigach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Itô映射（Itô map），一种用于随机微分方程（SDE）的任意步随机流映射，能够从中间状态和布朗路径单步预测未来状态，为扩散模型的推理时控制提供可微分的后验采样估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步生成模型基于常微分方程（ODE）学习确定性流映射以实现加速采样，但如何为随机动力学定义精确的蒸馏过程仍是一个开放问题。作者旨在填补SDE背景下缺乏类似蒸馏框架的空白。

Method: 提出Itô映射（Itô map）：一种任意步随机流映射，接收中间状态和布朗运动路径作为输入，单次前向传播即可预测未来状态。该映射通过条件流匹配或蒸馏方式学习SDE的转移核，并为推理时控制提供可微分后验采样访问。

Result: 在合成基准和图像生成任务上，Itô映射能够从固定中间状态生成多样化且条件有效的端点样本，并展现出强大的引导（steering）性能。

Conclusion: 任意步SDE积分可作为一种有用的后验采样与随机控制原语，Itô映射为随机动力学提供了精确的蒸馏方案，并在推理时控制任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Recent one-step generative models accelerate sampling by learning deterministic flow maps of the underlying dynamics. These methods rely on learning from ordinary differential equations, leaving open how to define an exact distillation procedure for stochastic dynamics. We introduce the Itô map, an any-step stochastic flow map that takes an intermediate state and Brownian path and predicts future states in a single pass. The Itô map formulation yields novel estimators for inference-time control by providing cheap, differentiable access to posterior samples. Empirically, Itô maps produce diverse, conditionally valid endpoint samples from fixed intermediate states and support strong steering performance on synthetic and image-generation benchmarks. These results establish any-step SDE integration as a useful primitive for posterior sampling and stochastic control.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Mechanistic Analysis of Alignment Algorithms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09850)
*Aarush Sinha,Ishan Garg,Veeraraju Elluru,Arth Singh,Kushal Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对六种偏好优化方法（PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO、KTO）进行系统性机制分析，发现不同对齐算法在语言模型内部产生的表征几何变换存在质的差异：KTO和GRPO增强线性可分性，而DPO和ORPO反而降低可分性，揭示了行为对齐并不等同于统一的内部重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐算法几乎都被当作黑箱评估，缺乏对其如何重塑语言模型内部计算机制的理解。这阻碍了对齐效果的可解释性、安全性审计以及更优对齐目标的设计。

Method: 在三个开源模型家族上，系统分析六种偏好优化方法（PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO、KTO）。整合三层分析工具：逐层线性探测、稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）和交叉编码器（crosscoders），用于定位偏好表征并量化对齐引发的潜在空间几何变换。

Result: 1）偏好信号一致集中于早期-中层或中-晚期层；2）KTO和GRPO通过建设性特征共享和稀疏高显著性神经元招募增强线性可分性；3）DPO和ORPO通过非建设性几何旋转和特征衰减降低可分性；4）PPO和SimPO基本保持基线几何结构；5）上述变换具有架构依赖性变异。

Conclusion: 对齐是一种异质性干预，行为层面的对齐并不蕴含统一的内部重构。研究呼吁建立标准化的特征级审计机制以保障安全性和可解释性，并设计具备机制感知能力的优化目标。

Abstract: Post-training alignment algorithms are predominantly evaluated as black boxes, obscuring how they reshape language models' internal computations. We present a systematic mechanistic analysis of six preference-optimization methods: PPO, DPO, SimPO, ORPO, GRPO, and KTO across three open-weight model families. By integrating layer-wise linear probing, Sparse Autoencoders, and crosscoders, we localize preference representations and quantify alignment-induced geometric transformations in latent space. We find that preference signals consistently concentrate in early--mid or mid--late layers, but different objectives induce qualitatively distinct representational shifts. KTO and GRPO enhance linear separability through constructive feature sharing and sparse, high-salience recruitment. In contrast, DPO and ORPO degrade separability via non-constructive geometric rotation and feature attenuation, while PPO and SimPO largely preserve baseline geometry. These transformations exhibit architecture-dependent variability, demonstrating that behavioral alignment does not imply uniform internal restructuring. Our findings establish alignment as a heterogeneous intervention, motivate standardized feature-level auditing for safety and interpretability, and highlight the need for mechanism-aware optimization objectives.

</details>


### [244] [SynIB: Informational Bottleneck for Maximizing Synergy in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09853)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Teodora Gagaleska,Thomas Strypsteen,Christos Chatzichristos,Matthew Blaschko,Maarten De Vos,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SynIB（协同信息瓶颈）目标，通过惩罚模型在模态缺失时的高置信度，迫使模型学习真正的跨模态协同信息，在合成及真实基准上均显著优于标准训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习方法多聚焦于架构层面，但标准训练目标往往偏向单模态或冗余信息，难以捕捉仅由多模态联合使用才能获得的协同信息。本文旨在从训练目标层面解决这一问题。

Method: 基于信息论形式化多模态协同，提出SynIB：在标准任务损失之外，每次掩码一种模态进行前向传播，若模型仍高置信度预测则施加惩罚，从而抑制对单模态线索的依赖，迫使模型学习跨模态交互。分别在已知协同真值的合成XOR任务和5个真实世界基准上验证。

Result: 合成XOR任务中，标准训练无法恢复协同信息而SynIB成功恢复。真实基准（含MultiBench、Hateful Memes、CREMA-D讽刺扩展）上，协同依赖样本准确率最高提升7.8%，总体准确率最高提升3.8%。

Conclusion: SynIB从训练目标层面有效引导模型捕获多模态协同信息，是对架构层面融合方法的互补，为多模态学习提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: A central objective in multimodal learning is to capture synergy: task-relevant information that arises only from the joint use of multiple modalities, and is not available from any single modality alone. While most approaches operate at the architectural level through larger or more complex fusion models, we propose a complementary axis: shaping the training objective itself. Standard training often emphasizes unimodal or redundant information, falling short on examples that require cross-modal reasoning. We formalize multimodal synergy through information theory and introduce the Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB), a scalable objective that targets synergy directly. To prioritize learning synergy, SynIB motivates the model to predict accurately from all modalities while penalizing confidence when information from any modality is withheld. Alongside the standard task loss, the model runs forward passes with one modality masked at a time and is penalized for remaining confident, which would indicate reliance on unimodal cues rather than cross-modal interactions. We validate SynIB in two regimes. On synthetic XOR tasks where the ground-truth synergy is known by construction, standard training fails to recover it while SynIB does. On five real-world benchmarks, including three MultiBench affective tasks, Hateful Memes with CLIP-ViT and DeBERTa backbones, and a controllable irony extension of CREMA-D we introduce, SynIB improves accuracy on synergy-dependent examples by up to 7.8% and overall accuracy by up to 3.8%.

</details>


### [245] [Uncertainty-aware Multi-fidelity Closure via Conditional Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09857)
*Jice Zeng,Shady E. Ahmed,David Barajas-Solano,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件归一化流的多保真度框架，将ROM闭合建模转化为多保真学习问题，通过学习从低保真ROM系数到高保真系数的概率映射，在提升预测精度的同时量化不确定性。在二维涡合并问题上验证了直接学习和残差学习两种策略，残差学习表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型（ROM）因截断误差及解析/未解析尺度间相互作用表征不足，存在闭合问题，预测精度受限。现有方法缺乏对闭合模型不确定性的量化，影响ROM在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 将ROM闭合建模形式化为多保真度学习问题，提出基于条件归一化流（conditional normalizing flow）的不确定性感知多保真框架。该框架学习从低保真ROM系数到高保真系数的概率映射。研究两种修正策略：(1) 直接学习——从低维输入直接预测高保真系数；(2) 残差学习——学习低保真与高保真系数之间的差异并用于恢复校正后的高保真解。

Result: 在二维Navier-Stokes方程控制的涡合并问题上验证：两种修正策略均优于未校正ROM；残差学习在准确性上持续优于直接学习；两种基于深度生成模型的策略均能对校正后的ROM系数进行不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 所提出的条件归一化流多保真框架能有效提升ROM预测精度，并提供不确定性量化能力，对于评估预测置信度、支持ROM在实际应用中的可靠部署具有重要意义。残差学习策略是更推荐的校正方式。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) provide an efficient surrogate for complex multiscale systems, but their predictive accuracy is often compromised by truncation errors and the inadequate representation of interactions between resolved and unresolved scales. The missing effect of truncated (unresolved) scales on ROM (resolved) scales is often denoted as the closure problem. In this work, we formulate ROM closure modeling as a multi-fidelity (MF) learning problem and propose an uncertainty-aware MF framework based on conditional normalizing flow to enhance ROM predictive accuracy. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic mapping from low-fidelity (LF) ROM coefficients to high-fidelity (HF) coefficients, thereby improving predictive fidelity while quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned closure. Two correction strategies are investigated: direct learning, in which HF coefficients are predicted directly from LF inputs, and residual learning, which learns the discrepancy between LF and HF coefficients and uses it to recover the corrected HF solution. The framework is demonstrated on a vortex merging problem governed by the two-dimensional Navier Stokes equations. Results show that both correction strategies improve ROM accuracy over uncorrected ROM, with residual learning achieving consistently better performance than direct learning. Moreover, the two proposed deep generative model-based strategies provide uncertainty quantification for the corrected ROM coefficients, which is critical for assessing prediction confidence and supporting the reliable use of ROMs in practical applications.

</details>


### [246] [Mitigating Manifold Departure: Uncertainty-Aware Subspace Rectification for Trustworthy MLLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09859)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Jingxiao Yang,Miao Pan,Cheng Tan,Yuxiang Cai,Siwei Tan,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGAP 是一种无需训练的几何感知解码方法，通过 SVD 构建语言先验子空间，并利用一致性感知门控自适应地仅衰减投影到该子空间的先验分量，从而在抑制幻觉的同时保持语义流形结构，避免现有方法因盲目压制语言先验导致的"流形偏离"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的幻觉抑制方法（如惩罚语言先验的解码策略）忽视了语言先验的双重性——当与视觉证据对齐时有益，否则有害。盲目压制语言先验会破坏模型的语义流形（称为 Manifold Departure），导致性能退化。因此需要一种能选择性衰减有害先验、同时保留有益语义结构的方法。

Method: 提出 Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP)，步骤为：1) 通过 SVD 从盲隐藏状态（无视觉输入时）构建语言先验子空间；2) 解码时将每个多模态隐藏状态投影到该子空间；3) 使用一致性感知门控机制自适应地仅衰减投影后的先验分量，实现对先验成分的子空间选择性更新，同时保留正交的语义成分。

Result: 在 POPE 和 CHAIR 基准上的广泛实验表明，MGAP 优于现有解码基线方法，在实现更强幻觉抑制的同时不牺牲生成连贯性。

Conclusion: MGAP 通过几何感知的子空间选择性投影，有效解决了盲目压制语言先验导致的流形偏离问题，实现了幻觉抑制与语义保真度的更好平衡，为训练无关的解码策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: MLLMs frequently hallucinate objects inconsistent with visual inputs. This issue is typically attributed to the over-reliance on language priors, which can override the visual context. Recent training-free decoding strategies address this by penalizing language priors. However, these methods overlook the dual nature of language priors, where they can be both helpful and harmful depending on the alignment with visual evidence. In particular, blindly suppressing language priors often disrupts the model's semantic manifold, leading to performance degradation, a phenomenon we term Manifold Departure. To address this, we propose Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP), a geometry-aware, training-free decoding method that mitigates hallucinations while preserving representation structure. MGAP first constructs a language-prior subspace from blind hidden states via SVD. During decoding, MGAP projects each multimodal hidden state onto this subspace and applies a consistency-aware gate to adaptively attenuate only the projected prior component, yielding a subspace-selective update that largely preserves the orthogonal semantic components. Extensive experiments on POPE and CHAIR show that MGAP outperforms prior decoding baselines, achieving stronger hallucination suppression without sacrificing coherence.

</details>


### [247] [Conformal Risk Prediction for Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease Using Gradient Boosting with Distribution-Free Coverages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09860)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于梯度提升决策树与共形预测的机器学习框架，用于NAFLD风险预测，在保证个体风险估计的分布无关覆盖的同时，通过互信息稳定性选择识别出紧凑且临床可解释的特征子集，并在广州多中心队列上取得了优于深度神经网络、TabNet等方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAFLD影响全球约25%成人，肝病和心血管风险显著，但现有群体层面的筛查工具仍然不足，亟需兼具高精度与校准可靠性的风险预测工具。

Method: 框架核心包含三个组件：(1) 梯度提升决策树作为基预测器；(2) 基于互信息的稳定性选择（bootstrap重采样筛选稳定特征子集），确保特征紧凑且具临床可解释性；(3) 共形预测构造预测集，提供分布无关的边际覆盖保证，覆盖概率可证明超过用户指定置信水平。

Result: 在广州市多中心队列（主队列n=2,187，外部验证n=412）上，方法内部AUROC达0.912，外部验证0.891，优于DNN、TabNet、SVM和逻辑回归。共形预测在90%名义水平下实现91.3%经验覆盖。三层风险分层中，高风险组12个月进展率是低风险组的4.7倍。筛选出的关键特征（腰围、ALT、GGT、甘油三酯、空腹血糖、BMI）与已知代谢风险因子一致。

Conclusion: 该框架将高精度预测、统计校准保证与临床可解释性有效整合，有望成为NAFLD群体筛查的实用工具，为早期分层干预提供决策支持。

Abstract: Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) affects roughly 25% of global adults, posing substantial hepatic and cardiovascular risks. Yet, population-level screening tools remain inadequate. We present Method, a machine-learning framework for NAFLD risk prediction coupling gradient-boosted decision trees with conformal prediction to yield calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees on individual risk estimates. It integrates a mutual-information-based stability selection procedure to identify a compact, clinically interpretable feature subset via bootstrap resampling, constructing prediction sets whose marginal coverage provably exceeds a user-specified confidence level. We evaluated Method on a multicenter cohort from Guangzhou, China (primary n=2,187; external validation n=412) using 78 candidate features across demographics, metabolic biomarkers, and lifestyle factors. Method achieves an AUROC of 0.912 internally and 0.891 externally, outperforming deep neural networks, TabNet, support vector machines, and logistic regression. Conformal prediction sets achieve 91.3% empirical coverage at the 90% nominal level. A three-tier risk stratification derived from these scores separates the population into distinct groups, with the high-risk subgroup showing a 12-month progression rate 4.7 times that of the low-risk tier. The selected features -- notably waist circumference, ALT, GGT, triglycerides, fasting glucose, and BMI -- align with established metabolic risk factors, providing biological plausibility.

</details>


### [248] [Time Series as Language: A Universal Tokenizer for General-Purpose Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09861)
*Yunhao Zhang,Ruiying Qi,Jiale Zheng,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniTok-FM 提出了一种通用时间序列基础模型，通过离散 token 化和下一个 token 预测（NTP）实现对预测、生成和分类任务的统一支持，并首次实现无需训练即可进行上下文推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 预训练范式（NTP）在无界连续时间序列领域的适配仍不成熟。现有方法缺乏一种通用方案，能同时支持零样本预测、少样本生成与分类，且无法实现免训练的上下文推理。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出 UniTok 通用 token 化器——一种向量量化自编码器，包含前缀归一化、（用于稳定尺度的）渐进分辨率因果架构以及结构保持重建损失。UniTok-FM 直接采用现成 LLM 架构，在由相似模式序列构成的上下文窗口上进行 NTP 预训练，以捕获共享动态，而非孤立序列训练。

Result: 统一的 UniTok-FM 在预测、生成和分类任务上一致地优于统计基线和有监督基线，与任务特定的基础模型性能相当，且独具跨任务免训练上下文推理能力。

Conclusion: UniTok 与 UniTok-FM 共同构成了一个通用、统一的时间序列基础模型方案，首次实现零样本/提示增强预测以及免训练上下文学习，展示了 NTP 范式在连续时间序列建模中的广阔前景。

Abstract: While Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has unified LLM pretraining, its adaptation to unbounded, continuous time series (TS) remains open. To bridge the gap, we introduce UniTok, a universal tokenizer that transforms TS into discrete tokens, and UniTok-FM, a foundation model pretrained via NTP on these tokens. UniTok-FM is a general-purpose foundation model that supports zero-shot and prompt-boosted forecasting, as well as few-shot generation and classification via training-free in-context inference--a capability not achieved by prior works. Technically, UniTok is a vector-quantized autoencoder incorporating prefix normalization for scale stabilization, a progressive-resolution causal architecture for encoding and decoding, and a structure-preserving reconstruction loss for training. UniTok-FM adopts an off-the-shelf LLM architecture without TS-specific modifications. Instead of pretraining on isolated TS, it performs NTP on context windows formed by multiple series with similar patterns, aiming to capture their shared dynamics. Experiments on forecasting, generation, and classification show that a single unified UniTok-FM consistently outperforms statistical and supervised baselines, achieves competitive performance with task-specific foundation models, and uniquely enables training-free in-context inference across tasks.

</details>


### [249] [Blurry Window Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09862)
*Axel Laborieux,Christos Sourmpis,Juan Gabriel Kostelec,Qinghai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Blurry Window Attention (BLA) 的新型有界记忆注意力机制，灵感来源于状态空间模型。BLA通过Dirichlet核对频率窗口进行插值重建模糊的KV历史，可视为滑动窗口注意力的泛化或门控槽注意力的特例。在合成任务中，BLA的状态效率比滑动窗口注意力高8倍，且是唯一随状态规模增长而持续提升性能的线性模型之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer中的Softmax Attention具有序列长度的二次复杂度，且KV缓存状态持续增长，在长上下文场景中成为瓶颈。现有线性替代方案（SSMs、线性注意力、ABC）虽在语言困惑度上接近Transformer，但在需要检索或回忆特定信息的任务上仍有差距。因此需要一种兼具线性复杂度和有限状态大小、同时能更好完成信息检索/回忆任务的新方法。

Method: 提出Blurry Window Attention (BLA)，一种受SSM启发的ABC方法。核心思路是：存储一个频率窗口，利用Dirichlet核进行插值，从中重建模糊的KV历史。BLA可视为Sliding Window Attention (SWA) 的泛化（取决于Dirichlet核分辨率），也可视为Gated Slot Attention (GSA) 的特例（其中衰减因子由Dirichlet核实现）。论文详细描述了BLA的理论基础和高效实现。

Result: 在Multi-Query Associate Recall (MQAR) 合成任务上，BLA的状态效率比SWA高8倍，与流行的线性注意力模型竞争力相当。在RegBench合成任务中，所有测试的线性模型中，仅有BLA和SWA能随状态规模增长而持续提升性能。

Conclusion: BLA作为一种新型的有界记忆注意力机制，成功结合了SSM的效率和注意力机制的表达能力，在保持线性复杂度和有限状态的同时，显著提升了信息检索与回忆任务的性能，展现了其在长上下文场景中的潜力。

Abstract: The Softmax Attention operation in Transformer language models has a quadratic complexity in the sequence length and a growing state size in the form of KV cache, which becomes a bottleneck in long context scenarios. To overcome this limitation, alternative architectures with linear complexity and finite state size have been introduced, such as State-Space Models (SSMs), Linear Attention (LA), and Attention with Bounded-memory Control (ABC). Though linear models achieve similar language perplexity as Transformers, they are still behind in tasks which require retrieval or recall of specific information. In this work, we introduce Blurry Window Attention (BLA) a novel ABC method inspired by SSMs. BLA stores a frequency window from which a blurry KV history is reconstructed via interpolation using Dirichlet kernels. BLA can be understood as a generalization of Sliding Window Attention (SWA) depending on the Dirichlet kernels resolution or as a special case of the Gated Slot Attention (GSA), where the decay factor is implemented with Dirichlet kernels. We describe in details the theory and efficient implementation of BLA. On the Multi-Query Associate Recall (MQAR) synthetic task, we show that the state efficiency of BLA is 8$\times$ better than SWA and is competitive with popular linear attention models, and in the RegBench synthetic task, only BLA and SWA improve their performance as the state size grows among the linear models we tested.

</details>


### [250] [From Confident Closing to Silent Failure: Characterizing False Success in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09863)
*Laksh Advani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM智能体存在"假成功"（false success）现象，即任务实际失败却声称完成。该现象在高自主性场景中普遍存在（占失败的45-75.8%），而LLM评判器对此检测能力很差（AUROC最高仅0.65）。基于TF-IDF的轻量级检测器表现优异（AUROC 0.83-0.95），速度提升3300倍，建议生产环境应使用领域校准的轻量检测器替代LLM评判器进行监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在部署中可能静默失败——错误地声称任务完成而环境状态表明并非如此。这种"假成功"模式对生产系统危害极大，但目前缺乏系统性的量化研究和有效的检测手段。作者旨在：(1)量化假成功在不同benchmark和场景中的普遍性；(2)评估LLM评判器检测假成功的能力；(3)探索更轻量、更可靠的替代检测方案。

Method: 研究在两个智能体benchmark上进行：tau2-bench（9876条轨迹，8个模型家族）和AppWorld（1879条轨迹，4个模型家族）。首先通过环境ground truth识别假成功案例并统计分布；然后系统评估5种LLM评判器、5种提示策略在检测假成功上的表现（AUROC）；最后提出基于TF-IDF的轻量级检测器，利用轨迹文本特征（tau2-bench中的自信结束语、AppWorld中的动作序列量）进行检测，并与LLM评判器在检测率、flag rate和延迟上进行对比。

Result: (1)假成功普遍性：tau2-bench单控制域中占失败的45-48%，双控制电信域仅3%，AppWorld自评估编码智能体中占75.8%。(2)LLM评判器失效：无论哪种评判器、提示策略或任务规格组合，tau2-bench上AUROC均未超0.65，AppWorld API调用轨迹上仅0.54。评判器依赖表面代理特征（自信结束语、动作数量）而非真实状态验证。(3)TF-IDF检测器：tau2-bench跨任务AUROC达0.83，AppWorld达0.95，在相同flag rate下比最佳评判器多恢复4-8倍假成功，延迟降低3300倍。

Conclusion: 假成功是LLM智能体在生产部署中的关键失败模式，且现有LLM评判器无法有效检测。轻量级、领域校准的检测器（如TF-IDF）在检测性能和效率上均显著优于LLM评判器。生产监控系统应采用此类轻量检测器作为分流信号，而非依赖LLM评判器作为假成功的主要监控手段。

Abstract: LLM agents can fail silently by asserting task completion when the environment state shows otherwise. We study this failure mode, false success, across two agent benchmarks: 9,876 tau2-bench trajectories from 8 model families and 1,879 AppWorld trajectories from 4 model families with text-independent ground truth. False success is common but varies by setting: 45--48% of failures in single-control tau2-bench domains, 3% in dual-control telecom, and 75.8% among AppWorld self-assessing coding-agent trajectories with explicit status claims. LLM judges fail reliably: no configuration across 5 judges, 5 prompt strategies, and full task specifications exceeds AUROC 0.65 on tau2-bench, and the same judges reach only 0.54 AUROC on AppWorld API-call traces. Judges rely on surface completion proxies -- confident closing language in tau2-bench and coarse action-sequence volume in AppWorld -- rather than verified state changes. Lightweight TF-IDF detectors achieve task-disjoint AUROC 0.83 on tau2-bench and 0.95 on AppWorld, recovering 4--8x more false successes than the best judge at the same flag rate with 3,300x lower latency. These results suggest that production monitoring should use lightweight, domain-calibrated detectors as triage signals rather than relying on LLM judges as the primary monitor for false success.

</details>


### [251] [Disjoint or Overlapping? Inference Windowing for Reconstruction-Based Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09874)
*Guillaume Coulaud,Reza Akbarinia,Florent Masseglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于重构的单变量时间序列异常检测中推理步长（inference stride）的作用，发现重叠窗口推理一致地提升性能（平均相对增益达+28%），并提出统一的训练、调优和多种子评估协议，在TSB-AD和UCR基准上验证了重构方法的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于重构的时间序列异常检测方法因评估实践不一致、推理过程欠规范（如推理步长选择不明确），导致结果难以公平比较。作者旨在通过系统研究推理步长对异常检测性能的影响，建立可复现的标准化评估协议。

Method: 在TSB-AD基准上提出统一的训练、调优和多随机种子评估协议；系统比较非重叠窗口（disjoint）与重叠窗口（overlapping）两种推理模式；涵盖PCA基线、DLinear、AutoEncoder、TimesNet及Transformer变体等多种重构模型；进一步在完整UCR档案上使用滑动窗口重构的定位标准进行补充评估。

Result: 重叠窗口推理在所有模型上均带来一致提升，平均相对增益高达+28%，且可改变方法排名；不同数据集、随机种子和超参数配置间存在显著变异性；重构基线方法在TSB-AD和UCR两个基准上均表现出强竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于重构的异常检测性能不仅取决于模型架构和训练，还高度依赖推理阶段的选择（尤其是推理步长）。重叠推理应作为默认实践。该工作为建立清晰、可复现的评估协议提供了有力动机，并确认重构方法是单变量时间序列异常检测中实用且具有竞争力的方案。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are widely used for time series anomaly detection, where models are trained to reconstruct subsequences, and anomalies are identified through reconstruction errors. However, reported results are often hard to compare due to heterogeneous evaluation practices and underspecified inference procedures. In this paper, we revisit reconstruction-based anomaly detection in the univariate offline setting and study the role of the inference stride, which controls whether subsequences are processed as disjoint windows or with overlap. We propose a unified training, tuning, and multi-seed evaluation protocol on the curated TSB-AD benchmark, and study how overlapping inference affects anomaly detection performance for a range of reconstruction models, including PCA-based baselines, DLinear, an AutoEncoder, TimesNet, and Transformer variants. The results show that across all models, overlapping windows yield consistent improvements, with average relative gain up to +28%, and can alter method rankings. We further analyze variability across datasets, random seeds, and hyperparameter configurations. Finally, we complement the benchmark study with an evaluation on the full UCR archive using localization criteria aligned with sliding-window reconstruction. Overall, our results highlight that reconstruction-based anomaly detection performance depends not only on model architecture and training, but also on inference choices, motivating a clear and reproducible protocol. Our results show that reconstructionbased baselines achieve strong performance on both TSB-AD and UCR benchmarks, supporting them as competitive and practical approaches for univariate time series anomaly detection.

</details>


### [252] [Alignment Collapse Under KV Cache Quantization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09864)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Adarsh Kumarappan,Mu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV缓存的低位量化会无声地破坏LLM的安全对齐，根源在于安全特征存在于低维激活子空间中，对量化噪声极其敏感。作者提出Per-Channel Reduction (PCR)诊断方法，将模型分为三种失效模式，并通过轻量级校准实现最高97%的对齐恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化研究仅关注困惑度和准确率，完全忽略了安全性影响。作者发现低比特量化可能在困惑度仅微增1.03倍的情况下，导致安全拒绝率下降15.2%，且不存在通用的安全比特宽度，这一问题在标准指标下完全不可见。

Method: 首先在11个指令微调模型（3.8B-72B）和5个基准（1894条提示）上系统评估量化对安全对齐的影响；提出"几何诊断假说"，揭示安全特征位于比完整表示空间对量化噪声敏感10²-10³倍的低维激活子空间；设计Per-Channel Reduction (PCR)诊断方法，仅需20条校准提示即可将模型分为三种失效模式：outlier-crushes-safety、outlier-as-safety、multi-layer dilution；基于PCR制定无需训练的恢复协议，约35 GPU分钟。

Result: PCR在全部9个主要模型和1个独立家族的留出模型上正确预测了缓解方向；在KIVI等生产级量化器中实现最高97.2%的对齐恢复；在生产环境vLLM FP8 KV缓存服务中验证了漏洞；基于注意力的分配方法则在此任务中失败。

Conclusion: KV缓存量化对安全对齐的破坏是一个被严重忽视的问题，其根源是几何性的而非统计性的。PCR提供了一种轻量、无需训练的诊断与恢复方案，能以极小内存开销恢复最多97%的丢失对齐，呼吁将安全性纳入量化评估的标准流程。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache quantization is widely used to reduce Large Language Model (LLM) inference memory, yet existing evaluations solely focus on measuring perplexity and accuracy without assessing the safety impact. In this study, we explore alignment preservation under KV cache quantization. Across eleven instruction-tuned models (3.8B-72B) and five benchmarks (1,894 prompts), we find that low-bit quantization can silently destroy safety alignment: Mistral-7B loses 15.2% of its refusals at only 1.03x perplexity, and no universal safe bit-width exists, with sharp model-specific phase transitions invisible to standard metrics. We identify that the root cause is geometric: safety features occupy a low-dimensional activation subspace 10^2-10^3x more vulnerable to quantization noise than the full representation space perplexity averages over. Inspired by this observation, we propose Per-Channel Reduction (PCR), a diagnostic that classifies each model into one of three mechanistic failure modes: outlier-crushes-safety, where safety lives in non-outlier channels collaterally damaged by outlier-driven scale factors; outlier-as-safety, where safety overlaps outlier channels and finer granularity cannot rescue it; and multi-layer dilution, where safety is distributed across many layers and per-layer fixes fail. PCR predicts the correct mitigation direction on all nine primary models and one held-out model from an independent family using 20 calibration prompts. PCR generalizes across unseen prompts, models, and production quantizers, including KIVI with up to 97.2% recovery, succeeding where attention-based allocation methods fail. The resulting training-free protocol, requiring approximately 35 GPU-minutes, recovers up to 97% of lost alignment at minimal memory overhead, addressing vulnerabilities confirmed in production vLLM serving with FP8 KV cache on NVIDIA GPUs.

</details>


### [253] [Integrating Local and Global Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09875)
*Johanne Medina,Tianyi Zhou,Keivin Isufaj,Aristides Gionis,Sanjay Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出全局-局部不确定性（GLU）分数，将隐藏状态的几何复杂度（全局不确定性）与token级熵（局部不确定性）通过乘法门控融合，在单次前向传播中实现更可靠的不确定性量化，尤其能捕获局部信号遗漏的"自信但错误"的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法主要依赖token级信号，忽略了中间隐藏状态的几何结构。全局几何特征与局部信号捕捉不同的失败模式，尤其是局部方法无法检测的"自信但错误"的情况，因此需要融合两者以提升可靠性预测。

Method: 将隐藏状态矩阵的几何复杂度（几何熵）作为全局不确定性度量，token级熵作为局部度量。发现两者统计上近似正交。提出GLU分数：通过乘法门控以无监督、单次前向传播的方式融合全局与局部信号，且具有长度归一化和架构无关的特性。

Result: 在三个模型家族和六个基准上，GLU匹配或超越所有无监督基线方法，且仅需单次前向传播。

Conclusion: 全局几何复杂度和局部token级熵捕获互补的不确定性信号，GLU通过融合两者提供了简单、有效且通用的LLM不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate confidently, making uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for reliable deployment. Existing methods rely predominantly on token-level signals, leaving the geometric structure of intermediate hidden states underused. In this paper, we take the geometric complexity of hidden-state matrices as a measure of the global uncertainty of LLMs, while treating token-level uncertainty estimation as a local metric. We show that hidden-state geometric entropy (global uncertainty) and token-level entropy (local uncertainty) are statistically near-orthogonal, capturing distinct failure regimes for reliability prediction. In particular, global geometry recovers the confident-but-wrong failure mode that local signals systematically miss. Building on this, we propose Global-Local Uncertainty (GLU), an unsupervised, single-pass score that fuses the two signals via a multiplicative gate. Across three model families and six benchmarks, GLU matches or outperforms all unsupervised baselines while requiring only a single forward pass and remaining length-normalized and architecture-agnostic.

</details>


### [254] [LLM-as-a-Discriminator: When Synthetic Tables Still Look Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09865)
*Manel Slokom,Malek Slokom,Thierno Kante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）作为判别器来审计表格合成数据的隐私风险，通过让LLM判断样本是真实还是合成，实验表明该方法在不同模型和数据集上表现出显著差异，可作为实用的隐私审计信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的隐私审计一直是个难题，即使是人类也难以区分真实与合成表格。现有方法存在局限，亟需一种实用且有效的隐私审计手段来评估合成数据的隐私风险。

Method: 提出基于LLM判别的方法：让LLM对每个表格样本进行REAL或SYNTHETIC的二分类。设置两种测试条件——C1（仅表格数据）和C2（表格加分布元数据）。使用LLaMA作为开源模型，Gemini作为参考模型。对CTGAN、TVAE、Gaussian Copula三种合成模型在UCI Adult和ACS Census两个公开数据集上进行实验，共收集451次有效试验。同时与分类器双样本检验（C2ST）、记录链接以及人类标注员（2人240次试验）进行对比。

Result: 各模型间差异显著：在Adult数据集上，LLaMA在报告单元格中达到DRS=0%，而Gemini对CTGAN和TVAE达到DRS=100%。在Census数据集上，LLaMA对大多数样本预测为SYNTHETIC，Gemini在C1条件下保持高判别率，但在C2条件下对CTGAN和TVAE有所下降。LLM判别方法整体优于传统基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM判别是一种实用的隐私审计信号，但需谨慎处理模型选择、提供商报告机制以及数据编码等因素。该方法为合成表格数据的隐私评估提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Privacy and data sharing are often in tension. Many organizations use synthetic data to reduce privacy risk and still share useful data. For tabular data, auditing privacy remains hard. In many cases, even humans cannot easily tell if a table is real or synthetic. In this paper, we propose a method based on LLM discrimination. We ask an LLM to classify each table sample as REAL or SYNTHETIC. We test two settings: C1 with table only, and C2 with table plus distributional metadata. We use LLaMA as an open model and Gemini as a reference model. In our experiments, we run three synthesis models, CTGAN, TVAE, and Gaussian Copula, on two public datasets, UCI Adult and ACS Census. We collect 451 valid trials. Our results show clear differences between models. On Adult, LLaMA reaches DRS=0% in reported cells, while Gemini reaches DRS=100% for CTGAN and TVAE. On Census, LLaMA predicts SYNTHETIC for most samples, while Gemini stays high in C1 but drops for CTGAN and TVAE in C2. We also compare with a classifier two-sample test (C2ST) and record linkage as distributional baselines, and with a human pilot of 2 annotators and 240 trials. Our results show that LLM discrimination is a practical privacy audit signal when model choice, per provider reporting, and data encoding are handled with care. For reproducibility, code and experiment scripts are available at https://github.com/SlokomManel/LLM-as-a-Discriminator.

</details>


### [255] [TENP: Trapezoidal Expert Neuron Pruning For Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09885)
*Jiangyang He,Shaolin Zhu,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TENP 是一种针对 MoE 大语言模型的结构化梯形专家神经元剪枝框架：它先识别并保留重要专家，对次要专家进行神经元级剪枝，并在浅层到深层间采用梯形参数保留模式，从而实现高效压缩，在保持精度的同时显著减少参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 大模型虽通过稀疏激活实现高效扩展，但其庞大的静态专家参数量仍然制约部署。现有压缩方法要么移除整个专家破坏路由拓扑，要么依赖非结构化权重剪枝导致实际加速有限。因此需要一种保持路由结构、实现结构化压缩的新方案。

Method: TENP 包含三个核心设计：(1) 专家重要性评估：联合考量专家输出幅度及其改变输入向量方向的能力，利用少量样本识别关键专家；(2) 专家神经元剪枝（ENP）：对次要专家，度量每个神经元对专家输出的投影贡献，保留重要神经元；(3) 梯形保留模式：从浅层到深层呈梯形递减地保留专家参数（浅层保留更多参数，深层保留更少），以匹配各层对表示容量的差异化需求。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 DeepSeek 模型上的实验表明：在路由专家稀疏度 40%、激活专家参数平均 63.76% 的设置下，DeepSeek 模型相比全参数模型仅下降 1 个准确点；在代码生成任务上，TENP 压缩后的模型甚至比全参数模型性能提升 10%。

Conclusion: TENP 通过结构化梯形专家神经元剪枝，在保持路由拓扑完整性的前提下实现了 MoE 模型的高效压缩，验证了"保护重要专家 + 精细化裁剪次要专家 + 层级差异化参数保留"策略的有效性，为 MoE 大模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts large language models (LLMs) scale efficiently through sparse activation, yet their deployment is fundamentally constrained by the large static parameter footprint of experts. Existing compression approaches either remove entire experts, disrupting routing topology and harming performance, or rely on unstructured weight pruning with limited practical efficiency. To address the limitations, we propose TENP, a structured Trapezoidal ExpertNeuron Pruning framework. Using a few samples, we identify and retain important experts, while applying expert neuron pruning (ENP) to less important experts, reserving model parameters in a trapezoidal pattern from shallow to deep layers. When evaluating expert importance, we jointly consider both the magnitude of the expert output and its ability to change the direction of the input vector. For ENP, we measure each neuron's projected contribution to the expert output to identify and retain important neurons. We conduct extensive experiments on the Qwen and DeepSeek models. Under a routing expert sparsity of 40% and an average of 63.76% activated expert parameters, the DeepSeek model suffers only a 1-point drop in accuracy compared to the full-parameter model. Moreover, it outperforms the full-parameter model by 10% on code generation tasks.

</details>


### [256] [Two to Tango: Coupled Task-Reference Selection for Safe LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09866)
*Xinrui Chen,Jianhao Zhang,Ou Wu,Di Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualSelect 是一种针对安全对齐大语言模型微调的联合任务与参考选择框架，通过刷新安全参考并筛选兼容的任务样本，在保持任务效用的同时显著提升安全保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐 LLM 在 downstream 微调时面临安全行为被侵蚀的问题（safety forgetting），而已有方法（固定安全示例、全局约束、单侧任务过滤）未能充分考虑不同任务更新会暴露不同的安全约束。需要一种动态选择相关安全参考与兼容任务样本的联合机制。

Method: 提出 DualSelect 框架：(1) 采用 minimax 视角，选择具有高保留损失（preservation loss）和高任务冲突的安全参考；(2) 通过熵正则化打分代理（entropy-regularized scoring surrogates）实现参考与任务样本的联合选择；(3) 引入惰性参考刷新（lazy reference refresh）策略和梯度校正（gradient correction）机制，降低计算开销并提升选择质量。

Result: 在 1B-8B 参数规模的 LLM 上，DualSelect 在不损失任务效用的情况下有效保留安全性；使用 REDORCA 评判器，Safety Avg. 比最强基线至少提升 5.10 分，且在其他评判器上均保持最高 Safety Avg.，额外计算开销适中。

Conclusion: DualSelect 通过任务条件化安全参考刷新与兼容任务样本的联合筛选，有效解决了安全对齐 LLM 在下游微调中的 safety forgetting 问题，该方法还可扩展至面向保留的持续学习场景。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety aligned large language models (LLMs) on downstream data improves adaptation but may erode learned safety behavior. Existing methods use fixed safety examples, global constraints, or one-sided task filtering. Our diagnostics show task updates expose different safety constraints, motivating joint selection of relevant references and compatible task samples. We propose DualSelect, a coupled framework for task and reference selection that refreshes task conditioned safety references before filtering whole task samples compatible with the induced reference direction. Under a minimax view, DualSelect selects safety references with high preservation loss and task conflict, together with compatible task samples, through entropy-regularized scoring surrogates, lazy reference refresh, and gradient correction. On 1B-8B LLMs, DualSelect preserves safety without losing task utility; using the REDORCA judge, it improves Safety Avg. over the strongest baseline by at least 5.10 points and remains highest in Safety Avg. across judges with moderate overhead. This view extends to retention focused continual learning.

</details>


### [257] [Nonlinear Estimator: Dual Bayesian Affine Estimators for Parameter Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10111)
*Sasan Vakili,Daniël Woonings,Pradyumna Paruchuri,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Wiener型状态空间模型提出了一种非线性参数估计器，采用固定点架构耦合两个仿射最小均方误差估计器（参数估计和潜变量估计），通过两种动态基统计量构造策略，形成双基-参数估计器和双状态-参数估计器，后者在参数估计精度上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wiener型状态空间模型的非线性参数估计是一个具有挑战性的问题。现有方法（如粒子Gibbs、EM算法）或纯仿射估计器在精度和计算效率方面存在局限。作者旨在通过固定点架构耦合参数估计和潜变量估计，利用动态基统计量来提升非线性参数估计的性能。

Method: 提出一种固定点架构，耦合两个仿射最小均方误差估计器：一个估计未知参数，另一个估计潜变量。引入动态基统计量（DBS）来总结非线性基函数评估。基于两种DBS构造策略，提出：(1) 双基-参数估计器，将仿射基估计器与仿射参数估计器结合；(2) 双状态-参数估计器，先计算仿射状态估计及其协方差，再通过高斯DBS算子映射得到DBS估计。两者均采用交替迭代的固定点形式，利用前一次迭代的插件估计统计量更新先验。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明，双基-参数估计器的参数均方误差与纯仿射参数估计器相当；双状态-参数估计器实现了最低的参数均方误差，优于双基-参数估计器、纯仿射参数估计器，以及基于序列蒙特卡洛的粒子Gibbs和期望最大化方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的双估计器框架有效解决了Wiener型状态空间模型的非线性参数估计问题。双状态-参数估计器在参数均方误差方面表现最优，证明了通过高斯DBS算子利用状态估计统计量的策略能显著提升估计精度，优于多种经典方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a nonlinear parameter estimator for Wiener-type state-space models obtained as a fixed-point architecture that couples two affine minimum mean-squared error (MMSE) estimators: one for the unknown parameters and one for latent variables. The architecture retains the functional structure of the optimal affine MMSE parameter estimator while incorporating Dynamic Basis Statistics (DBS) estimates that summarize nonlinear basis-function evaluations. Two DBS construction strategies are developed, leading to two nonlinear estimator frameworks. The dual basis-parameter estimator combines an affine basis estimator with the affine parameter estimator, whereas the dual state-parameter estimator first computes affine state estimates and their covariances, then maps these state-estimate statistics through a Gaussian DBS operator to obtain DBS estimates. Both dual estimators admit fixed-point characterizations that alternate between estimating each component using the updated prior of the other, obtained from that component's plug-in estimate statistics from the previous iteration. The efficacy of the proposed methods is examined via extensive Monte Carlo experiments, showing that the dual basis-parameter estimator attains parameter mean-squared errors comparable to those of the purely affine parameter estimator, while the dual state-parameter estimator achieves the lowest parameter mean-squared error, outperforming both the dual basis-parameter and purely affine parameter estimators, as well as sequential Monte Carlo variants of classical Particle Gibbs and Expectation-Maximization schemes.

</details>


### [258] [SPACE: Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09868)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jiaqi Ding,Qianshan Wei,Nan Zhou,Jiaqi Li,Yongliang Wu,Tongxin Zhu,Xiaolin Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPACE，首个面向多模态大语言模型的无需源数据的机器遗忘框架，通过文本引导的代理锚选择与双约束语义隔离两阶段方法，在不访问目标数据的前提下有效擦除敏感概念，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM的机器遗忘方法依赖目标概念的视觉数据，但由于严格的数据保留政策，这些数据往往不可获取，因此迫切需要无需源数据的遗忘方法。

Method: SPACE包含两个阶段：(1) 文本引导的代理锚选择（TPAS），从共享特征空间中检索语义对齐的代理锚；(2) 双约束语义隔离（DCSI），通过优化这些代理锚来间接擦除目标概念，并将更新限制在保留知识零空间内，确保结构完整性。理论上证明了SPACE对保留知识的扰动有严格界限，并最大化特征谱熵。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，SPACE取得了与当前最优的数据依赖方法相当的性能，验证了其在无源数据机器遗忘场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPACE作为首个面向MLLM的无源遗忘框架，成功解决了目标数据不可获取时的概念擦除问题，在保护隐私的同时维持模型性能，为机器遗忘在隐私合规场景中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face growing privacy risks and regulatory constraints, machine unlearning (MU) has emerged as a crucial solution for removing sensitive data while preserving model performance. However, existing MU methods typically rely on visual data of the target concepts, which is often unavailable due to strict data retention policies, thus creating a demand for source-free unlearning approaches that operate without access to the target data. In this work, we propose Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure (SPACE), the first source-free unlearning framework specialized for MLLMs. SPACE consists of two stages: (1) Text-Guided Proxy Anchor Selection (TPAS), which retrieves semantically aligned proxy anchors from the shared feature space. (2) Dual-Constraint Semantic Isolation (DCSI), which optimizes these anchors to indirectly erase target concepts. DCSI confines updates to the null space of retained knowledge, ensuring structural integrity. We theoretically prove that SPACE strictly bounds the perturbation on retained knowledge and maximizes feature spectral entropy, thereby maintaining the model's performance. Furthermore, extensive experiments across six datasets show that SPACE achieves performance comparable to that of state-of-the-art data-dependent methods, validating its effectiveness in source-free MU scenarios. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [259] [Rank Collapse, Fixed Points, and the Renormalization Group Structure of MLP Residual Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10324)
*Parviz Haggi-Mani,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对深度神经网络与重整化群（RG）之间的类比进行了定量实证研究。通过在具有已知谱特性的合成马尔可夫链上训练纯MLP残差网络，作者发现：（1）残差流的有效秩随深度单调下降；（2）这种秩塌缩具有选择性——仅发生在短相关长度的序列上；（3）层间核漂移集中在少数特定过渡层。这些发现为MLP残差网络实现选择性粗粒化过程提供了首个位置级别的定量证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中反复提及深度神经网络前向传播与重整化群（RG）流之间的类比，但现有讨论仅限于定性层面：深度被描述为粗粒化尺度，注意力被类比为配分函数，表征被认为流向不动点。然而，尚无工作定义可测量的RG序参量、在输入分布受控变化下进行检验，或做出经实证验证的定量预测。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用最简架构使类比可处理：纯MLP残差堆叠网络，在具有已知谱特性的合成马尔可夫链序列上训练掩码token预测任务。通过有效秩（effective rank）作为序参量，测量残差流随深度的变化；通过控制马尔可夫链的相关长度（短相关长度≈1 vs. 长相关长度≈7），在位置级别（而非整体均值池化）上检验粗粒化的选择性；通过层间核漂移（inter-layer kernel drift）测量网络各层与不动点的距离。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）训练后残差流的有效秩随深度单调递减，与无关自由度的逐步积分一致；（2）秩塌缩具有选择性：仅出现在短相关长度（≈1）的链上，而长相关长度（≈7）的链上不出现，且该测量在位置级别进行以排除均值池化伪影——网络精确保留了预测任务相关的自由度，符合RG相关性判据；（3）层间核漂移集中在一到两个特定过渡层，网络其余部分接近不动点，呈现离散不动点平台结构。

Conclusion: 这些发现共同构成首个定量、位置级别的证据，表明MLP残差网络实现了由输入分布谱结构支配的选择性粗粒化过程。这为DNN-RG类比从定性描述走向定量科学奠定了基础。

Abstract: The analogy between deep neural network forward passes and renormalization group (RG) flows has been repeatedly noted in the literature, but existing treatments remain qualitative: depth is described as a coarse-graining scale, attention is likened to a partition function, and representations are said to flow toward fixed points. No existing work has defined a measurable RG order parameter, tested it under controlled variation of the input distribution, or made quantitative predictions that are empirically verified. We study the simplest architecture for which the analogy is tractable: a pure MLP residual stack trained on masked token prediction over synthetic Markov chain sequences with known spectral properties. We report three findings. (i) The effective rank of the residual stream decreases monotonically with depth after training, consistent with progressive integration of irrelevant degrees of freedom. (ii) This rank collapse is selective: it occurs for chains with short correlation length approximately 1 but is absent for chains with long correlation length approximately 7, measured at the position level to control for mean-pooling artifacts. The network preserves exactly the degrees of freedom relevant to the prediction task, the content of the RG relevance criterion. (iii) Inter-layer kernel drift is concentrated at one or two specific transitions, with the remainder of the network near a fixed point, consistent with a discrete fixed-point plateau. Together these findings constitute the first quantitative, position-level evidence that MLP residual networks implement a selective coarse-graining procedure governed by the spectral structure of the input distribution.

</details>


### [260] [QSplitFL: Capability Aware Deep Q-Learning for Optimal Split Point Selection in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09869)
*Nazmus Shakib Shadin,Xinyue Zhang,Jingyi Wang,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QSplitFL 提出了一种基于深度Q网络（DQN）的轻量级框架，通过客户端硬件指标（CPU、内存、电池、网络延迟）而非模型权重来选择联邦-分裂学习中的最优切分层，并引入衰减损失奖励函数和委员会投票机制来加速收敛、防止奖励黑客攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习与分裂学习结合的场景中，客户端硬件能力异构，固定的模型切分点会导致弱设备过载、通信和服务器负载增加，进而降低收敛速度和稳定性。现有方法依赖高维模型权重表征来选择切分点，计算开销大且不实用。

Method: QSplitFL 包含三个核心设计：(1) 轻量级状态表征：直接从客户端的CPU利用率、内存、电池电量和网络延迟等硬件指标构建状态向量，避免使用高维模型权重；(2) 衰减损失下降奖励函数（decayed loss-drop reward）：优先奖励训练早期的快速损失下降，引导智能体选择能加速收敛的切分点；(3) 基于委员会的DQN架构：训练多个DQN智能体，通过多数投票（majority voting）决定最终切分点，缓解奖励黑客攻击（reward hacking）问题。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100四个数据集上，使用CNN、ResNet50、MobileNetV4和ConvNeXt四种架构进行了广泛实验。结果表明，QSplitFL相比现有方法在收敛速度和准确率上均有提升，并能有效适配异构设备资源。

Conclusion: QSplitFL 通过硬件感知的轻量状态表征和委员会DQN架构，有效解决了异构环境下SFL的最优切分点选择问题，在多个数据集和模型架构上验证了其优越性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) combined with Split Learning (SL) is a privacy preserving paradigm that enables training deep neural networks (DNNs) on resource constrained devices while reducing overall training cost. However, determining the optimal split point, meaning the layer where the model is divided still remains a critical challenge, especially when clients have heterogeneous hardware capabilities. Fixed split points can overload weak devices and increase the communication and server load, which slows convergence and reduces stability. This paper introduces QSplitFL, a novel capability-aware Deep Q-Network (DQN) framework for optimal split point selection in Split learning based Federated Learning (SFL) environments. Unlike existing approaches that rely on high-dimensional model weight representations, QSplitFL employs a lightweight state representation derived directly from client hardware metrics, including CPU utilization, memory, battery level, and network latency. The proposed framework incorporates a decayed loss-drop reward function that prioritizes early convergence, and a committee-based DQN architecture with majority voting to mitigate reward hacking. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 datasets using CNN, ResNet50, MobileNetV4, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that our approach achieves better convergence and higher accuracy compared to existing methods, while effectively adapting to heterogeneous device resources. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/AIPO-Lab/QSplitFL.

</details>


### [261] [Advancing the State-of-the-Art in Empirical Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10481)
*Nicole Mitchell,Galen Andrew,Arun Ganesh,Brendan McMahan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用高温采样生成合成金丝雀（canary）来进行大规模语言模型参数高效微调的隐私审计，并扩展至合成数据的隐私泄漏评估，系统研究了模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调（PEFT）可能导致LLM对训练样本的隐私泄露，现有的经验隐私审计（EPA）面临设计有效金丝雀样本的挑战——金丝雀需具有高可识别性，同时不能威胁真实隐私数据的私密性。

Method: 1）通过高温采样（T≥0.8）从LLM生成合成金丝雀，使用针对隐私敏感数据定制的提示词，使其成为高影响力离群点；2）提出合成数据审计方法：在合成数据上微调辅助模型，再对该辅助模型审计原始金丝雀，以估计合成数据的隐私泄漏；3）系统研究模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化的交互效应。

Result: 高温采样的合成金丝雀具有高可识别性，能实现强有力的隐私审计；该方法不危及真实数据隐私；合成数据审计方法能有效估计通过合成数据的隐私泄漏；模型容量与金丝雀熵对记忆化存在显著的交互影响。

Conclusion: 高温采样合成金丝雀是一种有效且安全的EPA方法，合成数据审计扩展了隐私评估的适用范围，模型容量与金丝雀熵的交互效应为LLM隐私保护提供了系统性洞见。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can exhibit problematic memorization of individual training examples. Empirical privacy auditing (EPA) quantifies this risk by measuring realistic data leakage on membership inference (MI) or reconstruction attacks. A key challenge in EPA is designing ``canary'' examples that are mixed with the privacy-sensitive training data. We propose generating synthetic canaries via high-temperature sampling ($T \geq 0.8$) from LLMs, using prompts tailored to the privacy-sensitive training data. These canaries act as high-influence outliers, ensuring high identifiability and hence strong audits. Further, since the canaries are themselves non-private, they are inspectable and can be inserted with repetition without jeopardizing the privacy of the real data. An important use of models fine-tuned on privacy-sensitive data is the generation of synthetic data. This also comes with privacy risk. We introduce a powerful synthetic data audit based on fine-tuning an auxiliary model on the synthetic data. Auditing the auxiliary model for the original canaries then provides a strong estimate of the privacy leakage through the synthetic data. Finally, leveraging our strong auditing methodologies, we perform a systematic investigation into the interacting effects of model capacity and canary entropy on memorization.

</details>


### [262] [PatchSTG: Scalable Spatiotemporal Graph Transformers for Traffic Forecasting on Irregular Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09872)
*Jichao Li,Xuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PatchSTG 提出了一种基于分块（patch）的时空图Transformer，通过将不规则分布的传感器按地理位置划分为均衡且保持局部性的分块，并采用块内注意力与块间注意力交替的双重注意力编码器，在保持预测精度的同时将计算复杂度从二次降至近线性，适用于大规模、非均匀传感器网络下的交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通网络中传感器分布不均匀，导致现有基于图和注意力的模型在非规则空间结构上效果受限且计算成本高，尤其在大规模场景下可扩展性不足。

Method: 提出 PatchSTG：1）基于地理信息将传感器划分为平衡且保持局部性的分块（patch），构建层次化空间表示；2）设计双重注意力编码器，交替执行块内注意力（捕捉局部交互）和块间注意力（建模全局依赖），将复杂度从 O(N²) 降至近线性。

Result: 在罗德岛州真实交通数据及多个大规模数据集上，PatchSTG 在多个预测时间窗口上取得稳定且具竞争力的预测性能，同时显著提升计算效率；消融实验验证了空间分块和双重注意力机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于分块的时空建模为不规则空间分布下的交通预测提供了一种可扩展且高效的框架，有效平衡了局部与全局依赖的捕捉能力与计算效率。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a fundamental component of intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging in real-world settings due to irregular sensor distributions and the high computational cost of modeling large-scale spatiotemporal dependencies. In practical traffic networks, sensors are unevenly distributed across regions, leading to non-uniform spatial structures that limit the effectiveness and scalability of existing graph-based and attention-based models. To address these challenges, we propose PatchSTG, a patch-based spatiotemporal graph Transformer designed for efficient forecasting on irregular sensor networks. The key idea is to introduce a hierarchical spatial representation that partitions sensors into balanced, locality-preserving patches based on geographic information. On top of this structure, a dual attention encoder alternates between intra-patch attention for capturing local interactions and inter-patch attention for modeling global dependencies, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to near-linear scaling. We evaluate PatchSTG on real-world traffic data from Rhode Island and additional large-scale datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves stable and competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, while significantly improving computational efficiency. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of spatial partitioning and dual attention in capturing both local and long-range traffic dynamics. These results suggest that patch-based spatiotemporal modeling provides a scalable and effective framework for traffic forecasting under irregular spatial settings.

</details>


### [263] [Rotate2Think: Geometric Priming via Orthogonal Rotation to Improve Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09873)
*Aditya Sharma,Christopher J. Pal,Amal Zouaq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 推理模型在推理过程中，输入嵌入与思维嵌入呈现出高度锥形分布，且二者的均值方向非共线。作者将此过渡建模为正交Procrustes旋转问题，提出无训练的Rotate2Think方法：从少量正确样本估计旋转，在推理时注入合成"思维向量"作为几何引导，从而在32个模型-基准配置中的30个上提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对推理模型在"思考"过程中隐藏表征空间的内部结构理解不足——模型在推理时的隐藏状态与输入提示的嵌入之间究竟存在怎样的几何差异，以及能否利用这种结构在推理阶段激发出更强的推理能力，这些问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出Rotate2Think方法：（1）对输入嵌入和思维嵌入（推理轨迹上最后一层隐藏状态的均值池化）进行分析，发现二者均具有极高锥度（conicity），即所有向量紧密围绕单一均值方向聚集，且输入均值方向与思维均值方向非共线，思维嵌入在嵌入空间中占据几何上不同的区域；（2）将输入到思维的过渡建模为一个旋转问题，通过正交Procrustes分析获得闭式解；（3）从少量正确求解的示例中估计该旋转矩阵，在推理时将旋转后的合成"思维向量"注入到思维定界符之间，作为推理轨迹起始阶段的几何先验引导。该方法完全无训练。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码等多个基准任务上，跨多种模型家族评估了32个模型-基准配置，其中30个配置的准确率获得提升；此外，该方法在MATH-Vision多模态推理任务上实现了零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 推理模型的输入嵌入与思维嵌入之间存在可建模的几何结构差异，通过正交Procrustes旋转可以有效地将输入表征"旋转"到思维区域，以无训练方式为推理过程提供几何引导，从而一致地提升推理性能。

Abstract: Reasoning models achieve strong performance on challenging tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces before producing a final answer. Yet the internal structure of representation space when reasoning remains poorly understood: how do a model's hidden representations differ during thinking versus the embeddings of the input prompt, and can this structure be exploited to elicit stronger reasoning at inference time? We show that both input embeddings and thinking embeddings (mean-pooled last-layer hidden states over the prompt and reasoning trace, respectively) exhibit extremely high conicity, with all vectors clustering tightly around a single mean direction. Crucially, these mean input and thinking directions are non-collinear, with thinking embeddings occupying a geometrically distinct region of embedding space across many different models and benchmark tasks. This observation motivates casting the input-to-thinking transition as a rotation problem admitting a closed-form solution via orthogonal Procrustes analysis. We propose Rotate2Think, a training-free method that estimates this rotation from a small set of correctly solved examples and injects the resulting synthetic thinking vector between thinking delimiters at inference time, providing a geometric primer at the onset of the reasoning trace. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks and model families, Rotate2Think improves accuracy in 30 of 32 model-benchmark configurations across mathematics, science, and code tasks, and generalizes zero-shot to multimodal reasoning on MATH-Vision.

</details>


### [264] [LMT: A Bayesian Framework for Causal Discovery from Textual Alarm Records in Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09892)
*Xiaofeng Xiao,Jianhong Chen,Qiuzhuang Sun,Naichen Shi,Xubo Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LMT框架，结合大语言模型从事件描述中提取的语义因果信号与基于泊松过程的时间戳统计证据，通过贝叶斯方法联合建模，实现工程事件数据的因果发现，在小样本场景下尤为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯依赖LLM编码的文本信息进行因果发现存在不足：语义模式无法直接揭示因果机制，且容易混淆因果关系与相关性或频繁时序模式。因此需要一种能够同时利用文本语义线索和时间戳统计证据的因果发现方法。

Method: 提出LMT（LLM-Merged Temporal）贝叶斯因果发现框架：第一步，利用LLM从事件文本描述中提取语义因果信号，构建事件类型（或事件聚类）间因果图的先验分布；第二步，引入基于泊松过程的似然函数，将时间戳统计证据纳入后验推断，对LLM先验进行修正和精炼；最终输出兼具可解释性与数据支撑的因果图。

Result: 仿真研究表明，LMT框架在不同设置下均表现有效，尤其在少样本告警事件场景中优势显著。

Conclusion: 融合文本语义先验与时序统计证据的贝叶斯因果发现方法，能够有效弥补纯LLM方法的不足，为工程告警日志等文本事件数据的因果分析提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Textual event records, such as alarm logs, have become an increasingly common data source in engineering and manufacturing systems. Beyond identifying correlations or recurring patterns, engineers are often interested in understanding which types of events causally trigger or influence other events during system operation. Textual event descriptions may contain semantic clues about such causal relationships, and recent large language models (LLMs) provide a promising tool for extracting these signals. However, relying solely on LLM-encoded textual information is insufficient for accurate causal discovery, since semantic patterns do not directly reveal causal mechanisms and may confuse causation with correlation or frequent sequential patterns. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{LMT}, a Bayesian causal discovery framework for engineering event data that jointly leverages textual descriptions and timestamps. Specifically, LMT first uses LLMs to extract semantic causal signals from event descriptions and constructs a prior distribution over causal graphs among event types or event clusters. It then incorporates temporal evidence through a Poisson-process-based likelihood, allowing the LLM-informed prior to be refined by timestamp-based statistical evidence. By integrating the textual and temporal information, LMT produces a causal graph that is both interpretable and data-supported. Simulation studies show that the proposed framework is effective across different settings and is especially advantageous in small-sample alarm-event scenarios.

</details>


### [265] [SPACR: Single-Pass Adaptive Training of Uncertainty-Aware Conformal Regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10734)
*Soundouss Messoudi,Sylvain Rousseau,Sébastien Destercke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPACR 提出一种在可微损失中直接训练不确定性感知回归器的方法，无需分批或预定义置信水平，单次训练即可在推理时生成多置信度下的有效预测区间，在区间宽度和覆盖-效率权衡上优于标准 CP 和 DOICR，且大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统保形预测（CP）通常在模型训练后以事后方式施加，导致模型训练目标与产生高效（窄）预测区间的保形目标不一致。现有方法如 DOICR 需要在不同置信水平下重新训练，计算成本高昂。因此需要一种能将效率与有效性联合优化、且支持多置信水平推理的单次训练方法。

Method: SPACR（Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor）将保形预测的效率与有效性目标融入一个可微损失函数中，实现端到端训练。该方法无需批次分割（batch-splitting），也不依赖预定义的置信水平，通过单次训练即可让模型学会输出适应性的预测区间。推理时，单一模型可直接生成多个置信水平下的有效预测区间。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SPACR 相比标准 CP 和 DOICR，持续产生更窄的预测区间，覆盖-效率权衡更优，同时计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: SPACR 成功将保形目标嵌入训练过程，实现了区间效率与有效性的联合优化，以单模型、单次训练支持多置信度推理，在性能和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) provides robust uncertainty guarantees for predictive models, but is typically applied post hoc, which misaligns model training with the conformal goal of producing efficient (i.e, narrow) intervals. We propose SPACR (Single-Pass Adaptive Conformal Regressor), a novel method for directly training uncertainty-aware regressors within a differentiable loss. SPACR jointly optimizes efficiency and validity without batch-splitting or a predefined confidence levels during training. As a result, a single SPACR model yields valid prediction intervals at multiple confidence levels during inference, avoiding the costly retraining required by methods like DOICR. Experiments on diverse datasets show that SPACR consistently gives tighter intervals and better coverage-efficiency trade-offs compared to standard CP and DOICR, while significantly reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [266] [Conservation Laws from Data Symmetry in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10913)
*Jakob Galley,Vahid Shahverdi,Axel Flinth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了训练数据的内在对称性是否会在神经网络梯度流训练过程中产生守恒量。核心结论是：对于一般的非多项式解析损失函数，数据对称性不会产生额外的守恒量；但对于MSE损失，数据增强可能产生额外守恒量，作者利用"可张量化网络"框架描述了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究训练数据的对称性是否会在梯度流动力学中诱导出额外的守恒量（积分运动常数），从而影响神经网络训练的动态行为和泛化性能。

Method: 假设损失函数为解析且非多项式，从理论上证明数据对称性不会产生额外守恒量。针对MSE损失的特殊情形，提出"可张量化网络"框架，该类架构的参数依赖和输入依赖可通过中间表示分离，涵盖线性网络、多项式网络及Lightning Attention等。

Result: （1）在解析非多项式损失下，数据对称性一般不诱导额外积分运动常数；（2）MSE损失下存在数据增强产生额外守恒量的情形；（3）建立了可张量化网络框架来系统描述此现象。

Conclusion: 数据对称性对梯度流守恒量的影响高度依赖于损失函数的形式：一般分析情形下无额外守恒量，而MSE损失构成重要的例外，可通过可张量化网络框架加以刻画。

Abstract: We explore whether intrinsic symmetries of the training data lead to conserved quantities during gradient-flow training of neural networks. Under the assumption that the loss function is analytic and non-polynomial, we prove that data symmetries generically do not induce any additional integrals of motion. For mean squared error (MSE) loss, on the other hand, there are situations in which data augmentation yields extra conserved quantities. We build a framework, utilizing \emph{tensorizable networks} to describe this phenomenon. Tensorizable networks are a family of architectures whose dependence on parameters and inputs can be separated using an intermediate representation. They include linear and polynomial networks, as well as Lightning Attention.

</details>


### [267] [Express Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10944)
*Albert Gong,Annabelle Michael Carrell,Raaz Dwivedi,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Express 是一种将非因果注意力近似转换为因果注意力近似的工具，结合 Thinformer 后实现了 $O(\log^{3/2}(n)/s)$ 的近似误差，仅需 $O(s)$ 内存和 $O(s^2\log^2(n))$ 压缩开销，并在语言建模的四个资源瓶颈上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果注意力近似方法在近似质量与资源效率之间存在权衡，而非因果注意力近似已有较好的理论保证。本文旨在通过将非因果近似转化为因果近似，桥接这一差距，从而在长序列场景中以更低的内存和计算开销获得高质量的因果注意力近似。

Method: 提出 Express 工具，可将任意非因果注意力近似的保证无损转化为因果版本；与 Thinformer 结合后，在因果设定下达到 $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ 的近似误差界，内存复杂度 $O(s)$，压缩开销 $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$；配套实现了 I/O 感知的 Triton 内核以提升实际运行效率。

Result: 在理论层面，将因果注意力的最优已知近似误差提升至 $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$。在实验层面，相比 FlashAttention 2 取得显著加速，并在长上下文预填充、KV 缓存压缩、内存受限的长文本解码、计算受限的长文本解码四个瓶颈场景中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: Express 成功将非因果注意力的强理论保证迁移到因果场景，兼具理论优雅性与工程实用性，为高效长序列语言建模提供了新的有力工具。

Abstract: We introduce a new tool, Express, for converting a non-causal attention approximation into a causal approximation with matching approximation guarantees. When combined with the state-of-the-art Thinformer approximation, Express improves upon the best known causal attention guarantees, delivering $\log^{3/2}(n)/s$ approximation error with only $O(s)$ memory and $O(s^2 \log^2(n))$ compression overhead for a sequence of length $n$. We pair these developments with an efficient I/O-aware Triton implementation, demonstrate substantial speedups over FlashAttention 2, and use Express to overcome four resource bottlenecks in the language modeling pipeline: long-context prefill, KV cache compression, long-form memory-constrained decoding, and long-form compute-constrained decoding.

</details>


### [268] [Calibrating Overconfidence Without Sacrificing Confidence: Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09876)
*Ke Li,Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Feng Liu,Qunli Zhang,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCHI是一种推理时干预方法，利用冻结探针检测模型可能产生的高置信错误回答，并在置信度生成过程中对注意力头输出进行条件性重新缩放，从而选择性减少口头过度自信，同时最大限度保留正确回答的置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常对错误答案表达高置信度（过度自信），而传统校准方法全局或分数层级操作，在抑制不当置信的同时容易损害正确回答应有的置信度。该工作旨在解耦这两种效应，实现选择性减少错误回答的置信度。

Method: 提出Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI)：在推理时，使用冻结的线性探针检测可能"错误但高置信"的响应（基于中间层隐藏表征）；一旦检测到，对下游注意力头的输出进行条件性重新缩放，干预位置包括读出token（readout-token）和上游置信模板token（upstream confidence-template tokens）的联合干预。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上求解OpenMathInstruct问题（结构化二元置信场）：读出token级别的PCHI将82.2%原本"错误-是"的置信读out转化为"否"；上游联合干预将ECE从21.9%降至9.2%，仅损害5.1%原本"正确-是"的读out。Gemma3-4B上读出token效应同样存在，但上游干预较弱且更依赖mask设计。

Conclusion: 口头过度自信可通过条件性内部干预选择性减少，PCHI成功部分解耦了"抑制不当置信"与"保留应有置信"这两个目标，为模型置信度校准提供了一种更精细的推理时控制方法。

Abstract: Large language models often express high confidence in answers that are wrong. Standard calibration remedies typically act globally or at the score level, reducing unwarranted confidence but also risking erosion of warranted confidence on correct answers. We introduce Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI), an inference-time method that uses a frozen probe to detect likely wrong-but-confident responses and conditionally rescales downstream attention-head outputs during confidence generation. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct solving OpenMathInstruct problems with a structured binary confidence field, readout-token PCHI converts 82.2% of originally wrong-yes confidence readouts to $\texttt{no}$, while a joint intervention across upstream confidence-template tokens reduces ECE from 21.9% to 9.2% and damages only 5.1% of originally correct-yes readouts. The readout-token effect also appears on Gemma3-4B, though upstream interventions are weaker and more mask-dependent. These results show that verbalized overconfidence can be selectively reduced through conditionally applied internal intervention, partially decoupling the suppression of unwarranted confidence from the loss of warranted confidence.

</details>


### [269] [Flexible Kernels for Protein Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11057)
*Martin Jankowiak,Yerdos Ordabayev,Rudraksh Tuwani,Henry N. Ward,Hunter Nisonoff,James M. McFarland,Gevorg Grigoryan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一类利用进化替换矩阵和局部线性的序列核函数，构建高斯过程以在稀疏数据下高效建模蛋白质性质景观，并通过结构感知的核函数实现多任务学习，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏实验数据中预测蛋白质性质（如结合亲和力、热稳定性）对蛋白质设计至关重要但极具挑战，现有基于基础模型嵌入的方法在该场景下数据效率不足。

Method: 提出一类序列核函数，核心创新包括：(1) 利用进化替换矩阵（如BLOSUM）编码序列相似性；(2) 引入局部线性假设增强核函数表达能力；(3) 进一步扩展为结构感知核函数，通过从基础模型中学习结构信息来调节替换矩阵，从而将结构信息融入核函数。基于这些核函数构建高斯过程模型，并用于多任务学习。

Result: 所提出的高斯过程模型在稀疏数据条件下频繁优于基于基础模型嵌入的替代方法；结构条件核函数在多任务学习中可决定性地超越局部监督学习方法。

Conclusion: 结合进化信息与结构感知的序列核函数为蛋白质性质预测提供了一种数据高效的方案，在多任务学习场景下表现尤为突出，有望推动蛋白质设计应用。

Abstract: Despite its importance to applications in protein design, predicting protein properties like binding affinity and thermostability from sparse experimental data remains a significant challenge. Accordingly, we introduce a class of sequence kernels that exploit evolutionary substitution matrices as well as local linearity and demonstrate that the resulting Gaussian processes provide data-efficient models of protein property landscapes, frequently outperforming alternatives that rely on foundation model embeddings. Furthermore--by learning what are in effect structure-aware substitution matrices--we show that our kernels can readily incorporate structural information from foundation models. We demonstrate that these structure-conditioned kernels are well suited to multi-task learning across multiple protein property landscapes and can decisively outperform local supervised learning methods.

</details>


### [270] [Streaming Knowledge Compilation: Proactive Materiality-Scored Pinning for Time-Evolving LLM Wikis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09877)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了流式知识编译问题：在文档流、固定token预算、查询未知的条件下，维护一个编译后的知识库以最小化相对于全知预言机的累积遗憾。核心创新是引入重要性信号φ_t(k,n)作为查询相关性的代理，用于主动选择文档，并证明了O(√(T log K))遗憾界。在金融和维基百科两个领域进行了实例化验证，发现LLM-as-judge存在训练后知识混淆，论证遗憾分析才是可靠的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM wiki系统依赖预填充KV缓存加速推理，但假设语料库是静态的。现实世界中知识持续更新（如金融新闻、维基百科编辑），静态假设不再成立。需要一种能在流式文档到达时、查询未知的情况下，动态维护编译知识库的方法。

Method: 将问题形式化为流式知识编译（Streaming Knowledge Compilation），在固定token预算下维护编译wiki，最小化对离线全知预言机的累积遗憾。核心是引入重要性信号φ_t(k,n)∈[0,1]作为查询相关性代理，在查询到达前进行主动pinning。证明了O(√(T log K))遗憾界，其中ε=E[|φ_t−φ̂_t|]是唯一领域相关的量。在金融领域用frozen Llama 3.1 8B分类头预测异常股票波动率作为φ_t（AUROC=0.728，76K文章，严格时间分割）；在维基百科领域用异常编辑比率（AER，截面归一化编辑速度）作为φ_t。

Result: 金融领域累积遗憾收敛至-20.0（-0.12/步），维基百科收敛至+16.0（+0.13/步）。维基百科正值证实编辑内容确实属于训练后知识，更丰富上下文持续提升评分（No Wiki 3.80 vs Oracle 4.74）。端到端QA评估（金融173对、维基百科119对）揭示LLM-as-judge存在训练后知识混淆，证明遗憾分析而非绝对QA分数才是编译知识系统的可靠评估指标。

Conclusion: O(√(T log K))遗憾保证适用于任何可通过流式信号预测知识缺口的领域。遗憾分析被确立为编译知识系统的可靠评估指标，因为LLM-as-judge的绝对QA分数受训练后知识混淆影响。

Abstract: LLM wiki systems compile knowledge into pre-filled KV caches for efficient inference, but assume a static corpus -- an assumption that fails whenever the underlying information landscape evolves. We formalize Streaming Knowledge Compilation: given a document stream, a fixed token budget, and future queries unknown at ingestion time, maintain a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret against an offline oracle with perfect foresight. The enabling insight is a materiality signal $φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$ that scores document importance for entity $k$ at time $t$, acting as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning before queries arrive; we prove an $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ regret bound where $\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$ is the only domain-specific quantity. We instantiate in two domains: finance, where $φ_t$ is abnormal stock volatility predicted by frozen Llama 3.1 8B classification head (AUROC = 0.728 on 76K articles, strict temporal split; $1.49\times$ higher realized forward volatility for predicted-material articles); and Wikipedia, where $φ_t$ is the Abnormal Edit Ratio (AER), a cross-sectionally normalized edit velocity -- showing the same algorithm generalizes beyond the finance domain. End-to-end QA evaluation on 173 matched pairs (finance) and 119 (Wikipedia) reveals a pervasive LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge, establishing that regret analysis -- not absolute QA scores -- is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0 (-0.12/step); Wikipedia to +16.0 (+0.13/step), with the positive sign confirming that Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training -- richer context consistently improves scores (No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74) -- and eliminates this confound. The $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals.

</details>


### [271] [Limitations of Learning Tanh Neural Networks with Finite Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11104)
*Philipp Grohs,Matěj Trödler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限精度计算和L^p精度保证下，基于点评估学习tanh神经网络的根本性局限。作者通过迭代tanh激活构造了尖锐局部化隆起函数，证明在有限精度设置下，任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法的L^p收敛速率不会超过蒙特卡洛速率O(m^{-1/p})，除非采样预算随网络参数和架构规模指数增长。该结果将先前ReLU网络的相关结论推广到了tanh网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Berner等人(2023)揭示了有限精度下ReLU网络学习的根本局限，但该结论是否适用于其他激活函数（尤其是平滑激活函数如tanh）尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究有限精度计算对tanh神经网络学习能力的理论限制。

Method: 核心技术手段是通过迭代复合tanh激活函数，构造出具有尖锐局部化性质的隆起函数（bump functions）。利用这一构造，在有限精度计算模型中分析自适应随机算法在L^p范数下的收敛行为，建立信息论下界，证明蒙特卡洛速率是不可逾越的，除非样本预算随网络规模指数增长。

Result: 证明在有限精度设定下，任何基于m个样本的自适应随机算法，其L^p收敛速率被限制在O(m^{-1/p})，即蒙特卡洛速率。要突破这一界限，采样预算必须随网络参数数量和架构规模呈指数级增长。这揭示了有限精度对包含局部化隆起函数的函数类可学习性的根本限制。

Conclusion: 有限精度计算对tanh神经网络的学习能力施加了与ReLU网络类似的理论限制，表明这一局限具有跨激活函数类型的普适性。局部化隆起函数的存在是造成这一瓶颈的关键结构因素。

Abstract: We investigate limitations of learning $\tanh$ neural networks from point evaluations under finite-precision computations and $L^p$ accuracy guarantees, building on Berner, Grohs, and Voigtländer (2023). Our approach is based on a novel construction of sharply localized bump functions via iterated $\tanh$ activations. Using this mechanism, we show that, in a finite-precision setting, no adaptive randomized algorithm based on $m$ samples can achieve a convergence rate higher than the Monte Carlo rate $O(m^{-1/p})$ in the $L^p$ norm, unless the sampling budget grows exponentially with the size of the network parameters and architecture. The results reveal fundamental limitations imposed by finite precision on the learnability of classes containing localized bump functions, extending previous results for ReLU networks to the $\tanh$ setting.

</details>


### [272] [FailureScope: Cross-Regime Behavioral Diagnosis of Language Model Weaknesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09878)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FailureScope 是一种行为诊断方法，通过留一模型（LOMO）交叉通过/失败模式对评估探针进行聚类，在单轮基准、多轮对话和对抗性攻击三种场景下均能生成稳定、可解释的失败分类法，大幅提升故障检测效率与跨模型失败预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准仅报告聚合准确率，无法揭示模型具体的薄弱能力。实践者需要知道模型在哪些特定能力上存在缺陷，而非笼统的整体分数。

Method: 提出 FailureScope，核心是留一模型（LOMO）聚类方法：将多个模型在大量评估探针上的通过/失败模式作为特征，对探针进行聚类，形成失败分类法。在三种场景（单轮基准、多轮对话、对抗性代理攻击）中验证该方法，并引入分类法条件下的采样策略与跨模型失败预测。

Result: 在2664个单轮任务和18个模型上，分类法条件采样在仅50个任务时Kendall's τ达0.81（随机采样仅0.34），跨模型失败预测AUC达0.88。在363个多轮任务和630条对抗代理轨迹上同样恢复出可解释聚类，并发现LLM评判得分与实际执行间存在73-100个百分点的元失败差距。三种场景下聚类内聚性均保持强劲。

Conclusion: 行为聚类是一种可移植的诊断原语，能泛化到单一基准之外。FailureScope 为模型能力缺陷诊断提供了标准化、跨场景适用的方法论，并已开源全套流水线、标注语料与跨场景分类法。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks report aggregate accuracy, but practitioners need to know which specific capabilities a model lacks. We introduce FailureScope, a behavioral-diagnosis method that clusters evaluation probes by their cross-model pass/fail patterns (leave-one-model-out, LOMO), and show it yields stable, interpretable failure taxonomies across three regimes usually studied separately: single-turn benchmarks, multi-turn dialogue, and adversarial agent attacks. On 2,664 single-turn tasks across 18 models, taxonomy-conditioned sampling reaches Kendall's tau = 0.81 at 50 tasks (versus 0.34 for random selection), and cross-model failure prediction reaches AUC 0.88. The same primitive recovers interpretable clusters on a 363-task multi-turn corpus and on 630 adversarial agent traces, where it exposes a meta-failure mode: a 73-100 percentage-point gap between LLM-judge ASR and real execution. Cluster cohesion remains strong across all three regimes, which we take as evidence that behavioral clustering is a portable diagnosis primitive that generalizes beyond any single benchmark. We release the pipeline, three annotated corpora, and the cross-regime taxonomies.

</details>


### [273] [Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11118)
*Rahul Roy,Nur Sunar,Jayashankar M. Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个双边服务平台上的动态品类优化问题，其中顾客和卖家的选择模型参数均未知。作者提出了一种数据驱动的在线学习算法，在学习双方偏好的同时优化平台收益，并证明该算法的遗憾值随时间呈多项式对数增长，且该速率是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态品类优化文献通常假设至少一方（顾客或卖家）的选择行为是已知的，但现实中双边平台往往对双方的偏好参数均缺乏先验知识。这一信息不完整带来的探索-利用权衡构成了核心研究动机。

Method: 在离散时间框架下，每期平台向到达的顾客展示一组卖家，顾客依MNL模型选择至多一个卖家发起交易提议；积累若干期后，卖家依另一MNL模型从收到的提议中选择至多一个顾客。平台在不知双方选择参数的情况下，设计数据驱动算法进行在线学习与决策优化。采用遗憾值（regret）作为性能度量，与知晓所有参数和到达序列的全知基准进行对比，并推导匹配的下界以证明速率最优性。

Result: 所提算法的worst-case遗憾值上界随时间呈多项式对数（polylogarithmic）增长，且作者推导了匹配的下界，从而证明了算法在速率意义上是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文首次研究了双边选择参数均未知的动态品类优化问题，填补了该领域的理论空白。所提出的数据驱动算法在理论上具有速率最优性，为双边平台在信息不完整环境下的运营决策提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


### [274] [Operator Fusion for LLM Inference on the Tensix Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09879)
*Qingbo Wu,Ke Li,Wenzhu Wang,Jie Yu,Ruian Zhang,Lili Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向Tenstorrent Tensix架构的算子融合策略，将RMSNorm与自注意力和FFN中的矩阵乘法融合，结合NoC多播机制实现多核并行，在Wormhole平台上取得最高37.44%的延迟降低，同时保持98.75%以上的数值一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在端侧部署时存在推理瓶颈，主要源于内存受限型算子（如RMSNorm）与计算受限型算子（如矩阵乘法）之间频繁的DRAM读写，导致数据局部性差、访存开销大。因此需要一种算子融合策略来减少中间结果对DRAM的访问，提升片上SRAM利用率。

Method: 提出算子融合策略：(1) 将RMSNorm与自注意力中的矩阵乘法融合，以及将RMSNorm与FFN中的矩阵乘法融合，使内存受限和计算受限算子可在片上SRAM中背靠背执行，大幅减少中间结果的DRAM读写和调度开销；(2) 为支持多核并行，利用基于NoC的多播机制，由行/列主节点通过核心网格高效分发输入和权重，缓解DRAM带宽争用。

Result: 在Tenstorrent Wormhole平台上对Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-4B进行实验：注意力部分延迟降低最高37.44%，MLP部分降低最高15.89%，每个解码器层延迟降低最高7.91%；皮尔逊相关系数保持在98.75%以上，证明在数值一致性约束下实现了显著的端到端效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过将RMSNorm与矩阵乘法进行算子融合并结合NoC多播机制，可有效缓解Transformer在Tensix架构上的访存瓶颈，在保证数值精度的前提下显著降低推理延迟，为端侧Transformer部署提供了高效的优化方案。

Abstract: This study addresses on-device inference bottlenecks of Transformer models on Tenstorrent's Tensix architecture and proposes an operator fusion strategy that enhances data locality. RMSNorm is fused with matrix multiplication in self-attention and in the FFN, enabling back-to-back execution of memory-bound and compute-bound operators in on-chip SRAM to significantly reduce DRAM reads/writes of intermediate results and scheduling overhead. To support multi-core parallelism, a NoC-based multicast mechanism is leveraged in which row/column master nodes efficiently distribute inputs and weights across the core mesh, alleviating DRAM bandwidth contention. Experiments on the Wormhole platform with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen3-0.6B, and Qwen3-4B show up to 37.44% latency reduction for attention and 15.89% for MLP, with up to 7.91% reduction per decoder layer, while Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) remains above 98.75%, confirming significant end-to-end efficiency gains under numerical consistency.

</details>


### [275] [Hyperparameter Learning for Latent Factorization of Tensors for Representation Learning to Large-scale Dynamic Weighted Directed Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09880)
*Yaqian Zhan,Jialan He,Tianzhu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DE-LFT框架，将差分进化（Differential Evolution）集成到潜在张量因子分解（LFT）的训练过程中，自动优化正则化超参数λ₁、λ₂、λ₃，从而在动态加权有向网络预测任务中提升精度并减少人工调参成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LFT模型的性能严重依赖超参数选择，而现有方法依赖人工调参或网格搜索，计算开销大、人力成本高。因此需要一种自动化超参数优化方法来提升效率与精度。

Method: 提出DE-LFT框架，将差分进化（DE）算法嵌入LFT模型的训练流程中，实现对三个正则化参数λ₁、λ₂、λ₃的自动搜索与自适应学习，使模型在超参数空间中高效寻优。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，DE-LFT相比人工调参的基线方法取得了更低的MAE和RMSE，同时大幅减少了对广泛参数调优的需求。

Conclusion: DE-LFT能够有效自动化LFT的超参数优化，提升动态加权有向网络的预测性能，并显著降低计算资源和人工调参开销，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Large-scale dynamic weighted directed networks (DWDNs) are widely used to model time-varying interactions among nodes. Latent factorization of tensors (LFT) extracts target knowledge from DWDNs via low-rank embedding. However, similar to many machine learning models, the performance of LFT heavily depends on the selection of hyperparameters. In practice, these parameters are often tuned manually or through grid search, which requires significant computational resources and human effort. Motivated by this challenge, this paper proposes an automated hyperparameter optimization framework based on Differential Evolution (DE) for LFT (DE-LFT). The proposed method integrates DE into the training process of the LFT model to automatically learn optimal regularization parameters $λ_1$, $λ_2$ and $λ_3$. As a result, the model can adaptively search the hyperparameter space and improve prediction accuracy. Experimental results on four real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves lower MAE and RMSE compared with manually tuned baselines while reducing the need for extensive parameter tuning.

</details>


### [276] [Toward Calibrated, Fair, and accurate Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09881)
*Ryan Brown,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Face-Fairness (FF) 是一个无需人口统计标签的即插即用框架，通过 Face-Feature Tuning (FFT) 对冻结人脸嵌入进行 logit 重映射来缓解深度伪造检测中的群体偏差，在保持甚至提升整体准确率的同时显著缩小了不同群体间的性能差距。


<details>
